Lanjut ke **Seri 15/24**. Fokusnya logging di Kubernetes: log harus keluar ke stdout/stderr, terstruktur, punya correlation ID, aman dari kebocoran secret/PII, dan bisa ditelusuri dari request Jersey sampai Pod/container yang menjalankannya.

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 15/24  
## Logging Strategy untuk Java/Jersey di Kubernetes: stdout/stderr, Structured JSON Logs, Correlation ID, MDC, Log Aggregation, Multiline Stacktrace, dan Debugging dengan `kubectl logs`

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana mendesain logging Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice agar bisa dipakai untuk debugging production di Kubernetes, bukan sekadar “ada log”?**

Di Kubernetes, container runtime menangani output aplikasi ke **stdout** dan **stderr**, lalu kubelet membuat log tersebut dapat diakses melalui mekanisme logging node. Kubernetes sendiri menekankan bahwa cluster-level logging perlu dipikirkan terpisah karena Pod, container, dan node bersifat transient. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/cluster-administration/logging/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Logging di Kubernetes bukan file lokal aplikasi.

```text
Wrong mental model:
Java app → tulis /app/logs/app.log → SSH ke server → tail file

Kubernetes mental model:
Java app → stdout/stderr
         → container runtime log
         → kubelet / kubectl logs
         → node-level/cluster-level log collector
         → centralized log platform
         → query by service, namespace, pod, traceId, requestId
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice, log harus menjawab:

```text
Request apa yang terjadi?
Dari caller mana?
Masuk ke endpoint mana?
Diproses oleh Pod mana?
Trace/request ID apa?
DB/downstream call mana yang lambat?
Error domain apa?
Apakah error transient, validation, auth, dependency, atau system failure?
Apakah ada secret/PII yang bocor?
```

---

# 2. Diagram Logging Flow

```text
Java/Jersey Application
│
├── SLF4J API
│   └── Logback / Log4j2 backend
│
├── MDC Context
│   ├── requestId
│   ├── traceId
│   ├── spanId
│   ├── userId masked/hashed if needed
│   ├── tenantId / customerId if allowed
│   ├── method
│   ├── path
│   └── pod metadata
│
├── Structured JSON Log
│   └── stdout/stderr
│
├── Kubernetes Node Logging
│   ├── container runtime captures stdout/stderr
│   └── kubelet exposes logs
│
├── kubectl logs
│   ├── current container logs
│   ├── previous crashed container logs
│   └── follow/tail/since
│
└── Cluster Log Aggregation
    ├── Fluent Bit / Fluentd / Vector / OTel Collector / vendor agent
    ├── Elasticsearch / OpenSearch / Loki / Splunk / Datadog / Cloud logging
    └── Query by labels + JSON fields
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Jersey/JAX-RS | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **stdout/stderr logging** | Aplikasi tulis log ke standard streams. | Kubernetes native log flow bekerja otomatis. | Log hilang di file lokal container. |
| 2 | **Structured JSON logs** | Log sebagai JSON key-value, bukan teks bebas. | Mudah query berdasarkan `requestId`, `traceId`, `path`, `status`. | Debugging bergantung grep manual. |
| 3 | **SLF4J facade** | Pakai API logging umum, backend bisa Logback/Log4j2. | Konsisten di seluruh Java service. | Library logging campur-aduk. |
| 4 | **MDC** | Context per request masuk ke semua log line. SLF4J MDC menyimpan key-value diagnostic context yang bisa dipakai backend logging. ([SLF4J](https://www.slf4j.org/api/org/slf4j/MDC.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Correlation ID muncul otomatis di log request flow. | Log error tidak bisa dikaitkan ke request tertentu. |
| 5 | **Jersey request/response filter** | Inject requestId, traceId, method, path, status, duration. | Titik terbaik untuk request logging. | Tidak ada access log aplikasi yang konsisten. |
| 6 | **Correlation ID propagation** | Terima/generate `X-Request-ID`, propagate ke downstream. | Debug lintas microservice. | Setiap service punya ID berbeda, tracing manual sulit. |
| 7 | **Trace-log correlation** | Masukkan `traceId`/`spanId` ke log. OpenTelemetry logs dirancang agar bisa dikorelasikan dengan resource, traces, dan metrik. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/otel/logs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Dari trace bisa lompat ke log; dari log bisa cari trace. | Observability terpisah-pisah. |
| 8 | **Log level discipline** | ERROR/WARN/INFO/DEBUG/TRACE jelas. | Menghindari log noise dan biaya storage tinggi. | Production penuh log tidak berguna. |
| 9 | **Exception logging** | Stacktrace cukup, tidak double/triple logged. | Error root cause terlihat jelas. | Stacktrace berulang, noise besar. |
| 10 | **Multiline handling** | Stacktrace Java harus bisa diparse collector. | Centralized log tidak pecah per baris sembarangan. | Stacktrace terfragmentasi. |
| 11 | **Sensitive data masking** | Mask secret, token, password, PII. | Regulatory/enterprise defensibility. | Kebocoran credential/data personal di log. |
| 12 | **Pod metadata enrichment** | Tambahkan namespace, pod, container, image, version. | Debug rollout dan incident per Pod. | Tidak tahu error dari versi/Pod mana. |
| 13 | **kubectl logs debugging** | `logs`, `-f`, `--previous`, `--since`, `--tail`, `-c`. `kubectl logs` memang digunakan untuk mencetak log container dalam Pod atau resource lain. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_logs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Triage cepat CrashLoopBackOff dan rollout issue. | Terlalu bergantung ke dashboard logging. |
| 14 | **Log aggregation awareness** | Paham collector dan retention. | Log production searchable. | Log hilang saat Pod/node mati. |
| 15 | **Operational log contract** | Log harus punya schema stabil dan field wajib. | Query/alert bisa distandarkan. | Setiap service format log beda. |

---

# 4. Prinsip Utama Logging di Kubernetes

## Prinsip 1 - Tulis ke stdout/stderr

Untuk containerized app, default terbaik adalah log ke stdout/stderr.

```text
INFO/DEBUG/WARN/ERROR application logs → stdout
fatal startup error / crash detail       → stderr boleh, tergantung backend config
```

Jangan andalkan:

```text
/app/logs/app.log
/var/log/my-service.log
```

Kenapa?

```text
Pod bisa mati.
Container filesystem ephemeral.
Pod bisa pindah node.
Log file lokal tidak otomatis terkumpul jika collector hanya membaca stdout/stderr.
```

Kubernetes menyediakan `kubectl logs` untuk mengambil log container, tetapi Kubernetes juga menyatakan bahwa cluster-level logging butuh backend/collector terpisah agar log tetap tersedia melampaui lifecycle Pod/node/container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/cluster-administration/logging/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Logging Architecture untuk Java/Jersey

Recommended stack umum:

```text
Application code
  → SLF4J API

Logging backend
  → Logback atau Log4j2

Output format
  → JSON encoder ke console/stdout

Kubernetes
  → container runtime captures stdout/stderr

Collector
  → Fluent Bit / Fluentd / Vector / OpenTelemetry Collector / vendor agent

Storage/query
  → Loki / Elasticsearch / OpenSearch / Splunk / Datadog / cloud logging
```

Untuk Java SE 17+ Jersey app, cukup umum memakai:

```text
slf4j-api
logback-classic
logstash-logback-encoder
```

atau:

```text
slf4j-api
log4j2
JSON layout
```

Yang penting bukan brand library-nya, tetapi contract:

```text
structured
single-line JSON
stdout
MDC included
secret masked
trace/request correlation included
```

---

# 6. Structured JSON Log

Plain text log:

```text
2026-07-13 10:10:11 INFO Order created successfully
```

Masalah:

```text
requestId tidak ada
traceId tidak ada
orderId mungkin tidak konsisten
status code tidak jelas
query sulit
```

Structured JSON log:

```json
{
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T10:10:11.123Z",
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "environment": "prod",
  "namespace": "order-prod",
  "pod": "order-api-7df98d6659-xr2qc",
  "version": "1.8.3",
  "requestId": "req-7ad1c8",
  "traceId": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736",
  "spanId": "00f067aa0ba902b7",
  "method": "POST",
  "path": "/orders",
  "status": 201,
  "durationMs": 42,
  "message": "Order created"
}
```

Keuntungan:

```text
mudah query: level=ERROR service=order-api
mudah filter: traceId=...
mudah aggregate: p95 duration by path
mudah correlate: pod/version/image
mudah audit: requestId, caller, operation
```

---

# 7. Field Wajib Log

Minimal field untuk semua log event:

| Field | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `timestamp` | `2026-07-13T10:10:11.123Z` | Gunakan UTC/ISO-8601. |
| `level` | `INFO` | Standard level. |
| `service` | `order-api` | Sama dengan service name. |
| `environment` | `prod` | Dari env/config. |
| `namespace` | `order-prod` | Dari Downward API. |
| `pod` | `order-api-...` | Dari Downward API. |
| `container` | `order-api` | Jika tersedia. |
| `version` | `1.8.3` | App version/image tag/git commit. |
| `logger` | `com.company.order.OrderResource` | Class/logger name. |
| `thread` | `http-worker-12` | Berguna untuk thread issue. |
| `message` | `Order created` | Human-readable. |

Field request-level:

| Field | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `requestId` | `req-abc123` |
| `traceId` | OpenTelemetry trace ID |
| `spanId` | OpenTelemetry span ID |
| `method` | `GET` |
| `path` | `/orders/{id}` atau raw path |
| `status` | `200` |
| `durationMs` | `42` |
| `client` | service caller jika diketahui |
| `userId` | masked/hashed bila perlu |
| `tenantId` | jika multi-tenant dan allowed |
| `errorCode` | domain/system error code |

---

# 8. Downward API untuk Metadata Pod

Agar log tahu Pod/namespace/node, inject metadata via Downward API:

```yaml
env:
  - name: POD_NAME
    valueFrom:
      fieldRef:
        fieldPath: metadata.name

  - name: POD_NAMESPACE
    valueFrom:
      fieldRef:
        fieldPath: metadata.namespace

  - name: POD_IP
    valueFrom:
      fieldRef:
        fieldPath: status.podIP

  - name: NODE_NAME
    valueFrom:
      fieldRef:
        fieldPath: spec.nodeName

  - name: APP_VERSION
    value: "1.8.3"
```

Lalu masukkan ke logging context/global fields.

```text
namespace=order-prod
pod=order-api-7df98d6659-xr2qc
node=worker-12
version=1.8.3
```

Ini sangat penting saat rollout:

```text
version 1.8.3 error rate naik?
hanya Pod di node tertentu?
hanya satu replica?
hanya setelah rolling update?
```

---

# 9. MDC untuk Correlation Context

MDC atau Mapped Diagnostic Context memungkinkan aplikasi menaruh key-value context ke logging framework, lalu setiap log line dari thread yang sama bisa menyertakan field tersebut. SLF4J menjelaskan MDC sebagai map diagnostic context yang dikelola oleh logging framework dan bisa dipakai untuk memasukkan data ke log message. ([SLF4J](https://www.slf4j.org/manual.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh field MDC:

```text
requestId
traceId
spanId
userId
tenantId
method
path
client
```

Contoh Java:

```java
import org.slf4j.MDC;

public final class LoggingContext {
    private LoggingContext() {}

    public static void put(String key, String value) {
        if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
            MDC.put(key, value);
        }
    }

    public static void clear() {
        MDC.clear();
    }
}
```

Penting:

```text
MDC harus dibersihkan setelah request selesai.
Jika tidak, context request A bisa bocor ke request B pada thread reuse.
```

---

# 10. Jersey Request Logging Filter

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS, gunakan `ContainerRequestFilter` dan `ContainerResponseFilter`.

Contoh baseline:

```java
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.slf4j.MDC;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestLoggingFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(RequestLoggingFilter.class);

    private static final String START_TIME = "request.startTime";
    private static final String REQUEST_ID_HEADER = "X-Request-ID";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        long start = System.nanoTime();
        requestContext.setProperty(START_TIME, start);

        String requestId = Optional
                .ofNullable(requestContext.getHeaderString(REQUEST_ID_HEADER))
                .filter(value -> !value.isBlank())
                .orElseGet(() -> UUID.randomUUID().toString());

        String method = requestContext.getMethod();
        String path = requestContext.getUriInfo().getPath();

        MDC.put("requestId", requestId);
        MDC.put("method", method);
        MDC.put("path", path);

        // Make requestId available downstream and to response filter.
        requestContext.setProperty("requestId", requestId);

        log.info("HTTP request started");
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) throws IOException {
        try {
            Long start = (Long) requestContext.getProperty(START_TIME);
            long durationMs = start == null
                    ? -1
                    : (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000;

            String requestId = (String) requestContext.getProperty("requestId");

            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, requestId);

            MDC.put("status", String.valueOf(responseContext.getStatus()));
            MDC.put("durationMs", String.valueOf(durationMs));

            log.info("HTTP request completed");
        } finally {
            MDC.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text
Jangan log body request/response secara default.
Jangan log Authorization header.
Jangan log cookie/session token.
Jangan log password/token/secret.
```

---

# 11. Propagasi Request ID ke Downstream

Saat `order-api` memanggil `customer-api`, request ID harus diteruskan.

```text
External request
  → order-api requestId=req-123
  → order-api calls customer-api with X-Request-ID=req-123
  → customer-api logs requestId=req-123
```

Jersey Client filter:

```java
import jakarta.ws.rs.client.ClientRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.client.ClientRequestFilter;
import org.slf4j.MDC;

import java.io.IOException;

public class CorrelationClientFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        String requestId = MDC.get("requestId");
        if (requestId != null && !requestId.isBlank()) {
            requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Request-ID", requestId);
        }

        String traceId = MDC.get("traceId");
        if (traceId != null && !traceId.isBlank()) {
            requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Trace-ID", traceId);
        }
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
Client client = ClientBuilder.newBuilder()
        .register(new CorrelationClientFilter())
        .connectTimeout(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build();
```

---

# 12. Trace ID dan OpenTelemetry

Kalau Anda memakai OpenTelemetry, log sebaiknya menyertakan `traceId` dan `spanId`.

OpenTelemetry Java instrumentation mendukung context propagation sehingga traces, metrics, dan logs bisa saling melengkapi; dokumentasi Java OpenTelemetry menyebut context propagation sebagai mekanisme korelasi antar sinyal telemetry. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/languages/java/instrumentation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Log event ideal:

```json
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-api",
  "traceId": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736",
  "spanId": "00f067aa0ba902b7",
  "requestId": "req-abc",
  "message": "Failed to call payment-api",
  "errorCode": "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT"
}
```

Dengan ini:

```text
Trace waterfall menunjukkan payment-api lambat.
Log order-api bisa difilter dengan traceId yang sama.
Log payment-api juga bisa ditemukan dengan traceId yang sama.
```

---

# 13. Logback JSON Example

Contoh `logback.xml` dengan JSON encoder:

```xml
<configuration>
    <appender name="CONSOLE" class="ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender">
        <encoder class="net.logstash.logback.encoder.LoggingEventCompositeJsonEncoder">
            <providers>
                <timestamp>
                    <fieldName>timestamp</fieldName>
                </timestamp>

                <logLevel>
                    <fieldName>level</fieldName>
                </logLevel>

                <loggerName>
                    <fieldName>logger</fieldName>
                </loggerName>

                <threadName>
                    <fieldName>thread</fieldName>
                </threadName>

                <message>
                    <fieldName>message</fieldName>
                </message>

                <mdc/>

                <stackTrace>
                    <fieldName>stacktrace</fieldName>
                </stackTrace>

                <globalCustomFields>
                    {
                      "service":"${APP_NAME:-order-api}",
                      "environment":"${APP_PROFILE:-unknown}",
                      "namespace":"${POD_NAMESPACE:-unknown}",
                      "pod":"${POD_NAME:-unknown}",
                      "version":"${APP_VERSION:-unknown}"
                    }
                </globalCustomFields>
            </providers>
        </encoder>
    </appender>

    <root level="${LOG_LEVEL:-INFO}">
        <appender-ref ref="CONSOLE"/>
    </root>
</configuration>
```

Prinsip:

```text
ConsoleAppender → stdout
JSON one event per line
MDC included
global fields included
stacktrace included as field
```

---

# 14. Log Level Discipline

Gunakan level secara konsisten.

| Level | Kapan Dipakai | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `ERROR` | Kegagalan yang perlu perhatian/operator/action. | DB unavailable, unhandled exception, request gagal 5xx. |
| `WARN` | Kondisi tidak normal tapi request mungkin masih bisa lanjut. | Retry downstream, fallback cache, validation unusual. |
| `INFO` | Event lifecycle dan business milestone penting. | Startup complete, request summary, shutdown started. |
| `DEBUG` | Detail teknis untuk investigasi sementara. | SQL parameter sanitized, mapping detail. |
| `TRACE` | Detail sangat granular. | Hampir tidak untuk production normal. |

Anti-pattern:

```text
Semua exception di-log ERROR walau expected validation error.
Semua request body di-log INFO.
Semua SQL di-log DEBUG production.
Semua retry di-log ERROR.
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Validation/client error → INFO/WARN tergantung konteks.
Dependency timeout → WARN jika recovered, ERROR jika request gagal.
Unhandled system error → ERROR.
```

---

# 15. Request Access Log

Setiap HTTP request sebaiknya punya satu summary log.

Contoh sukses:

```json
{
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T10:10:11.123Z",
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "traceId": "abc",
  "method": "GET",
  "path": "/orders/ORD-123",
  "status": 200,
  "durationMs": 37,
  "message": "HTTP request completed"
}
```

Contoh gagal:

```json
{
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T10:10:12.777Z",
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-456",
  "traceId": "def",
  "method": "POST",
  "path": "/orders",
  "status": 500,
  "durationMs": 3120,
  "errorCode": "DB_TIMEOUT",
  "message": "HTTP request failed"
}
```

Jangan log terlalu banyak per request jika tidak perlu.

Pattern yang sehat:

```text
1 access log per request
additional domain log untuk event penting
ERROR log untuk failure signifikan
metrics untuk volume/latency
trace untuk dependency chain
```

---

# 16. Exception Logging

Bad pattern:

```java
try {
    service.createOrder(request);
} catch (Exception e) {
    log.error("Failed to create order", e);
    throw e;
}
```

Lalu global exception mapper juga log error yang sama:

```java
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {
    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        log.error("Unhandled exception", exception);
        return Response.serverError().build();
    }
}
```

Efek:

```text
satu exception di-log dua kali
stacktrace duplicate
noise tinggi
root cause sulit dibaca
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Log exception di boundary tunggal.
Service layer boleh menambah context tanpa stacktrace jika perlu.
ExceptionMapper/global boundary log stacktrace final.
```

Contoh:

```java
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String errorId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

        MDC.put("errorId", errorId);
        MDC.put("errorType", exception.getClass().getSimpleName());

        log.error("Unhandled application error", exception);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(Map.of(
                        "errorId", errorId,
                        "code", "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                        "message", "Unexpected error"
                ))
                .build();
    }
}
```

---

# 17. Jangan Log Sensitive Data

Jangan log:

```text
Authorization header
Cookie
Session token
JWT raw token
Password
Client secret
API key
Refresh token
Private key
DB URL dengan credential
Full credit card number
Full national ID
Sensitive personal data
Raw request body untuk login/payment/PII
```

Masking example:

```java
public final class LogSanitizer {
    private LogSanitizer() {}

    public static String maskHeader(String name, String value) {
        if (name == null) {
            return value;
        }

        String lower = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

        if (lower.contains("authorization")
                || lower.contains("cookie")
                || lower.contains("token")
                || lower.contains("secret")
                || lower.contains("password")
                || lower.contains("key")) {
            return "****";
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

Senior-level rule:

```text
Log harus cukup untuk diagnosis, bukan menjadi data lake rahasia.
```

---

# 18. Request/Response Body Logging

Default: **jangan log body**.

Alasan:

```text
PII/secret risk
log volume besar
performance overhead
multipart/file upload bisa sangat besar
JSON body bisa mengandung nested sensitive field
```

Jika harus untuk troubleshooting:

```text
aktifkan sementara
hanya di environment non-prod atau traffic tertentu
sampling
mask sensitive fields
limit size
redact by schema
audit siapa yang mengaktifkan
matikan setelah selesai
```

Lebih aman log metadata:

```json
{
  "method": "POST",
  "path": "/orders",
  "contentLength": 842,
  "contentType": "application/json",
  "requestId": "req-123"
}
```

---

# 19. Multiline Stacktrace Problem

Java stacktrace multiline bisa menjadi masalah di log aggregation.

Plain stacktrace:

```text
java.lang.IllegalStateException: failed
    at com.company.OrderService.create(OrderService.java:42)
    at com.company.OrderResource.create(OrderResource.java:21)
```

Jika collector membaca line-by-line, stacktrace bisa terpecah menjadi banyak event.

Solusi:

```text
Gunakan JSON log dengan stacktrace sebagai field.
Atau konfigurasi multiline parsing di collector.
```

Preferensi untuk Kubernetes:

```text
JSON one event per line
stacktrace escaped dalam field JSON
collector tidak perlu menebak multiline
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "message": "Unhandled application error",
  "stacktrace": "java.lang.IllegalStateException: failed\n\tat ..."
}
```

---

# 20. Logging untuk Startup

Startup logs harus menjawab:

```text
versi aplikasi apa?
profile/env apa?
config non-secret efektif apa?
port listen apa?
database pool size berapa?
downstream base URL apa?
feature flag penting apa?
JVM memory/container info apa?
readiness kapan true?
```

Contoh startup log:

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "version": "1.8.3",
  "environment": "prod",
  "namespace": "order-prod",
  "pod": "order-api-abc",
  "javaVersion": "17.0.x",
  "httpPort": 8080,
  "dbPoolMaxSize": 10,
  "message": "Application startup completed"
}
```

Jangan log:

```text
DB_PASSWORD
OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET
JWT signing key
full token
```

---

# 21. Logging untuk Shutdown

Dari seri graceful shutdown, log lifecycle penting:

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "pod": "order-api-abc",
  "message": "Shutdown signal received"
}
```

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "readiness": false,
  "reason": "shutting_down",
  "message": "Readiness changed"
}
```

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "activeRequests": 3,
  "message": "Waiting for in-flight requests to complete"
}
```

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-api",
  "durationMs": 12340,
  "message": "Shutdown completed"
}
```

Ini membantu membedakan:

```text
rollout normal
SIGKILL karena grace period habis
liveness restart
node eviction
app crash
```

---

# 22. Logging untuk Downstream Calls

Untuk setiap dependency penting, log summary failure dengan context.

Contoh downstream timeout:

```json
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "traceId": "abc",
  "dependency": "payment-api",
  "operation": "authorizePayment",
  "method": "POST",
  "targetPath": "/payments/authorize",
  "durationMs": 3000,
  "timeoutMs": 3000,
  "errorCode": "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT",
  "message": "Downstream call timed out"
}
```

Jangan log semua successful downstream calls di INFO jika traffic tinggi. Gunakan metrics/traces. Log successful call detail di DEBUG atau sampling.

Pattern:

```text
Success:
  metrics + trace
  optional DEBUG/sampled log

Failure:
  WARN/ERROR with dependency, duration, error class, status
```

---

# 23. Logging untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL

Hindari log SQL raw + parameter sensitif di production.

Lebih baik log:

```json
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "operation": "OrderMapper.findById",
  "durationMs": 1200,
  "thresholdMs": 500,
  "message": "Slow database operation"
}
```

Untuk error DB:

```json
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "dbOperation": "insertOrder",
  "sqlState": "23505",
  "errorCode": "DB_UNIQUE_VIOLATION",
  "message": "Database constraint violation"
}
```

Jangan log:

```text
full SQL dengan raw PII
password/JDBC credential
large result set
full request body
```

---

# 24. Logging untuk Camunda Worker

Worker logs harus menjawab:

```text
task topic apa?
business key apa?
process instance id apa?
activity id apa?
worker id apa?
lock duration berapa?
attempt/retry berapa?
handler duration berapa?
complete/fail/BPMN error?
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-worker",
  "topic": "order-processing",
  "processInstanceId": "proc-123",
  "businessKey": "ORD-123",
  "workerId": "order-worker-1",
  "durationMs": 823,
  "message": "Camunda task completed"
}
```

Failure:

```json
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-worker",
  "topic": "order-processing",
  "processInstanceId": "proc-123",
  "businessKey": "ORD-123",
  "retriesLeft": 2,
  "errorCode": "PAYMENT_TIMEOUT",
  "message": "Camunda task failed"
}
```

Jangan log variables mentah jika mengandung PII/secret.

---

# 25. kubectl logs Cheat Sheet

`kubectl logs` mencetak log container dalam Pod atau resource; jika Pod hanya punya satu container, nama container opsional. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_logs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Logs dari Deployment

```bash
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

## Follow logs

```bash
kubectl logs -f deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

## Tail log terakhir

```bash
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --tail=100
```

## Logs sejak durasi tertentu

```bash
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --since=10m
```

## Logs container tertentu

```bash
kubectl logs pod/order-api-abc -n order-prod -c order-api
```

## Logs previous crashed container

```bash
kubectl logs pod/order-api-abc -n order-prod --previous
```

`--previous` sangat penting untuk `CrashLoopBackOff`, karena container saat ini mungkin baru start dan log error sebenarnya ada di container sebelumnya.

## Logs semua Pod dengan label

```bash
kubectl logs -n order-prod -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api --tail=100
```

## Dengan timestamp

```bash
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --timestamps
```

---

# 26. Debugging CrashLoopBackOff dengan Logs

Golden path:

```bash
kubectl get pods -n order-prod
```

```bash
kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n order-prod
```

```bash
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod --previous
```

Cari:

```text
Missing env var
Config invalid
DB credential failed
Cannot bind port
Permission denied due to readOnlyRootFilesystem
OutOfMemoryError
ClassNotFoundException
Migration failure
Health probe failed after startup
```

Kalau log kosong:

```text
app crash sebelum logger init
entrypoint salah
image command salah
JVM tidak start
container killed sangat cepat
```

Cek `describe pod` events.

---

# 27. Log Aggregation

Kubernetes native logging tidak cukup untuk production jangka panjang.

Cluster-level logging biasanya punya:

```text
node agent / daemonset collector
central collector / pipeline
storage/query backend
retention policy
indexing strategy
access control
redaction/masking
alert integration
```

Contoh alur:

```text
Pod stdout/stderr
  → node file from container runtime
  → Fluent Bit DaemonSet
  → OpenSearch / Loki / Splunk / Datadog / cloud logging
```

Atau:

```text
Pod stdout/stderr
  → OpenTelemetry Collector / vendor agent
  → telemetry backend
```

Kubernetes menyebut cluster-level logging sebagai hal yang perlu arsitektur terpisah karena log harus memiliki storage dan lifecycle yang tidak tergantung pada node, Pod, atau container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/cluster-administration/logging/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 28. Query Patterns yang Harus Bisa

Di centralized log platform, Anda harus bisa query:

```text
service=order-api AND level=ERROR
service=order-api AND requestId=req-123
service=order-api AND traceId=...
service=order-api AND status>=500
service=order-api AND durationMs>1000
service=order-api AND dependency=payment-api
namespace=order-prod AND pod=order-api-...
version=1.8.3 AND errorCode=DB_TIMEOUT
```

Kalau field tidak structured, query semacam ini jadi susah.

---

# 29. Log Volume dan Cost Control

Log terlalu banyak bisa menyebabkan:

```text
storage cost naik
query lambat
collector overload
node disk pressure
signal-to-noise buruk
alert fatigue
```

Praktik:

```text
INFO untuk lifecycle/request summary penting
DEBUG hanya sementara atau sampling
hindari log body
hindari log success event terlalu granular
gunakan metrics untuk high-cardinality counts
gunakan traces untuk flow detail
gunakan sampling untuk noisy successful operations
```

High cardinality fields seperti `orderId`, `userId`, `requestId` berguna untuk search, tetapi hati-hati kalau dijadikan metric label. Untuk logs, masih masuk akal jika ada governance data sensitivity.

---

# 30. Log Level Runtime Configuration

Gunakan env/config:

```yaml
env:
  - name: LOG_LEVEL
    value: "INFO"
  - name: LOG_LEVEL_COM_COMPANY_ORDER
    value: "INFO"
```

Logback contoh:

```xml
<root level="${LOG_LEVEL:-INFO}">
    <appender-ref ref="CONSOLE"/>
</root>

<logger name="com.company.order" level="${LOG_LEVEL_COM_COMPANY_ORDER:-INFO}"/>
```

Saat incident, bisa naikkan logger tertentu:

```text
com.company.order.client.PaymentClient=DEBUG
```

Jangan naikkan root DEBUG di production terlalu lama.

---

# 31. Async Logging

Async logging bisa mengurangi latency request, tetapi punya trade-off.

Kelebihan:

```text
request thread tidak selalu blocking saat log write
lebih stabil saat log volume sedang
```

Risiko:

```text
log bisa hilang saat crash jika queue belum flush
queue penuh bisa drop/block tergantung config
shutdown harus flush log
```

Untuk service critical:

```text
gunakan async logging dengan bounded queue
pastikan shutdown flush
monitor dropped log events jika backend mendukung
jangan jadikan async logging alasan untuk log berlebihan
```

---

# 32. Logging dan Threading/MDC Leakage

MDC biasanya thread-local. Jika aplikasi memakai async executor:

```text
request thread punya MDC
async executor thread belum tentu punya MDC
```

Efek:

```text
log async task kehilangan requestId/traceId
atau lebih buruk, memakai MDC lama dari task sebelumnya
```

Solusi:

```text
wrap Runnable/Callable dengan MDC context copy
clear MDC setelah task selesai
gunakan instrumentation library jika tersedia
```

Contoh sederhana:

```java
public final class MdcAwareRunnable implements Runnable {
    private final Runnable delegate;
    private final Map<String, String> contextMap;

    public MdcAwareRunnable(Runnable delegate) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
        this.contextMap = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        Map<String, String> previous = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();
        try {
            if (contextMap != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(contextMap);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            if (previous != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(previous);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 33. Logging untuk Security Events

Security-relevant events perlu log khusus, tapi tetap aman.

Contoh event:

```text
authentication failed
authorization denied
invalid token
expired token
permission denied
suspicious repeated failure
admin action
secret rotation event
privileged operation
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DENIED",
  "userIdHash": "u_8f1a...",
  "operation": "cancelOrder",
  "resourceType": "Order",
  "resourceId": "ORD-123",
  "message": "Authorization denied"
}
```

Jangan log token mentah.

---

# 34. Logging Contract per Service

Setiap service sebaiknya punya logging contract:

```text
1. Format JSON one line.
2. Wajib field service/environment/namespace/pod/version.
3. Wajib field requestId untuk request path.
4. Wajib traceId/spanId jika tracing aktif.
5. Tidak log secret/PII raw.
6. Satu access log per HTTP request.
7. Error boundary log stacktrace satu kali.
8. Downstream failure log punya dependency + operation + duration + errorCode.
9. Startup/shutdown lifecycle log tersedia.
10. Log level bisa dikonfigurasi.
```

Ini membuat service baru konsisten dengan platform observability.

---

# 35. Manifest Example: Logging Metadata

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.8.3"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.8.3
          env:
            - name: APP_NAME
              value: "order-api"

            - name: APP_PROFILE
              value: "prod"

            - name: APP_VERSION
              value: "1.8.3"

            - name: LOG_LEVEL
              value: "INFO"

            - name: POD_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.name

            - name: POD_NAMESPACE
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.namespace

            - name: POD_IP
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: status.podIP

            - name: NODE_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: spec.nodeName
```

---

# 36. Common Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Cara Cek |
|---|---|---|
| `kubectl logs` kosong | App tidak menulis stdout/stderr, crash sebelum logger init | `describe pod`, entrypoint |
| Log hilang setelah Pod mati | Tidak ada cluster log aggregation | cek collector/backend |
| Stacktrace pecah per baris | Multiline parsing salah / bukan JSON one-line | cek collector config |
| Tidak bisa cari request | requestId tidak dibuat/propagate | cek Jersey filter/client filter |
| Log service A/B tidak correlate | traceId/requestId tidak propagated | cek headers/instrumentation |
| Log terlalu mahal/banyak | DEBUG/root verbose/body logging | turunkan level/sampling |
| Secret muncul di log | Sanitizer tidak ada, env dump, body logging | audit logs, patch masking |
| Error duplicate | Exception di-log di banyak layer | boundary logging |
| Log tidak tahu Pod/version | Downward API/global field belum ada | env/logback |
| Async log hilang saat crash | queue tidak flush/shutdown | async logging config |
| MDC bocor antar request | `MDC.clear()` tidak dipanggil | filter finally block |

---

# 37. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Log ke file lokal container

Buruk:

```text
/app/logs/order-api.log
```

Lebih baik:

```text
stdout/stderr → collector
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Log body request production

Buruk:

```java
log.info("requestBody={}", body);
```

Risiko:

```text
PII
password
token
large payload
storage cost
```

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Tidak Ada Request ID

Buruk:

```text
ERROR Failed to create order
```

Lebih baik:

```json
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "traceId": "abc",
  "errorCode": "DB_TIMEOUT",
  "message": "Failed to create order"
}
```

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Double Logging Exception

Buruk:

```text
DAO logs exception
Service logs exception
Resource logs exception
ExceptionMapper logs exception
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Log stacktrace di boundary final.
Lower layer throw typed exception with context.
```

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Root DEBUG di Production

Buruk:

```text
LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG
```

untuk semua logger dalam waktu lama.

Lebih baik:

```text
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
LOG_LEVEL_COM_COMPANY_ORDER_CLIENT_PAYMENT=DEBUG
```

dan revert setelah incident.

---

# 38. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Output | Apakah app log ke stdout/stderr? |
| Format | Apakah log JSON one-line? |
| Required fields | Apakah service/env/namespace/pod/version selalu ada? |
| Request context | Apakah requestId dibuat dan dipropagate? |
| Trace correlation | Apakah traceId/spanId ada jika tracing aktif? |
| MDC lifecycle | Apakah MDC clear di `finally`? |
| Async context | Apakah MDC dipropagasi ke executor/async task? |
| Access log | Apakah satu summary log per request tersedia? |
| Error logging | Apakah stacktrace tidak duplicate? |
| Sensitive data | Apakah token/password/PII dimasking? |
| Body logging | Apakah disabled by default? |
| Multiline | Apakah stacktrace tidak pecah di aggregator? |
| kubectl debugging | Apakah `--previous`, `--tail`, `--since`, `-c` dikuasai? |
| Aggregation | Apakah log masuk centralized backend dengan retention jelas? |
| Cost | Apakah log volume/log level terkendali? |
| Lifecycle | Apakah startup/shutdown logs cukup untuk incident? |

---

# 39. Ringkasan Seri 15

```text
Kubernetes logging baseline:
  app writes stdout/stderr
  container runtime captures logs
  kubelet exposes logs
  kubectl logs reads logs
  cluster collector ships logs to backend
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice:

```text
Gunakan SLF4J + Logback/Log4j2.
Output JSON one-line ke console/stdout.
Gunakan MDC untuk requestId, traceId, spanId, method, path.
Bersihkan MDC setelah request.
Propagate correlation ID ke downstream.
Jangan log secret/PII/body mentah.
Log exception satu kali di boundary.
Pastikan stacktrace tidak pecah di aggregator.
Tambahkan pod/namespace/version via Downward API.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text
Log yang baik bukan log yang banyak.
Log yang baik adalah evidence: terstruktur, aman, bisa dikorelasikan,
cukup untuk rekonstruksi failure, dan tidak menciptakan risiko baru.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:14:41 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 16/24  
## Metrics, Monitoring, JVM Metrics, HTTP Metrics, DB Pool Metrics, Prometheus/OpenTelemetry, SLI/SLO, dan Alert Design untuk Java/Jersey di Kubernetes

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **metric apa saja yang wajib dimiliki Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice di Kubernetes agar kita bisa tahu service sehat, cepat, scalable, dan tidak sedang menuju incident?**

Di seri 15 kita membahas **logging**. Logging menjawab “apa yang terjadi pada event/request tertentu”. Metrics menjawab “apakah sistem secara agregat sehat atau memburuk”.

Kubernetes punya resource metrics pipeline untuk CPU/memory Pod dan Node. Metrics Server mengimplementasikan Metrics API dan digunakan oleh komponen autoscaling seperti HPA, serta oleh `kubectl top`. Namun resource metrics saja tidak cukup untuk memahami kesehatan aplikasi Java, HTTP, database pool, downstream call, dan worker behavior. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-cluster/resource-metrics-pipeline/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Metrics harus dilihat dalam beberapa layer:

```text id="qb7e5l"
Monitoring Layers
│
├── Kubernetes / Container Metrics
│   ├── CPU usage
│   ├── CPU throttling
│   ├── memory working set / RSS
│   ├── restart count
│   ├── Pod ready/unready
│   └── OOMKilled
│
├── JVM Metrics
│   ├── heap / non-heap
│   ├── GC pause
│   ├── threads
│   ├── class loading
│   ├── direct buffer
│   └── process CPU
│
├── HTTP / Jersey Metrics
│   ├── request rate
│   ├── latency
│   ├── status code
│   ├── in-flight requests
│   └── endpoint-level behavior
│
├── Database / MyBatis / PostgreSQL Metrics
│   ├── connection pool active/idle/pending
│   ├── query latency
│   ├── transaction latency
│   ├── error count
│   └── slow operation
│
├── Dependency Metrics
│   ├── downstream HTTP latency/error
│   ├── Camunda task backlog/duration/failure
│   ├── Kafka lag if applicable
│   ├── Redis latency/error
│   └── external API failure
│
└── Business / SLI Metrics
    ├── successful order creation rate
    ├── failed order creation rate
    ├── workflow completion time
    ├── payment authorization success
    └── customer-impacting error rate
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="p4mstj"
CPU/memory only tells you whether the container is busy.
It does not tell you whether users are succeeding.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Metrics Flow

```text id="h9voyh"
Java/Jersey Application
│
├── Micrometer / OpenTelemetry SDK / Java Agent
│   ├── HTTP metrics
│   ├── JVM metrics
│   ├── DB pool metrics
│   ├── custom business metrics
│   └── dependency metrics
│
├── /metrics endpoint or OTLP exporter
│
├── Collector / Scraper
│   ├── Prometheus
│   ├── OpenTelemetry Collector
│   ├── cloud/vendor agent
│   └── service monitor/operator if used
│
├── Storage / Query
│   ├── Prometheus / Thanos / Cortex / Mimir
│   ├── Grafana
│   ├── Datadog / New Relic / CloudWatch / Azure Monitor
│   └── OpenSearch/Splunk if metrics integrated
│
└── Alerting
    ├── SLO burn rate
    ├── availability/error alerts
    ├── latency alerts
    ├── saturation alerts
    └── dependency degradation alerts
```

OpenTelemetry Java provides APIs, SDKs, instrumentation, and Java agent options for collecting telemetry such as metrics, logs, and traces from Java applications. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/languages/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Metrics vs Logs vs Traces** | Metrics untuk agregat, logs untuk event, traces untuk flow request. | Observability tidak timpang. | Semua debugging dipaksa pakai log. |
| 2 | **Prometheus Metric Types** | Counter, Gauge, Histogram, Summary. Prometheus instrumentation libraries mengenal empat tipe metric utama. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/concepts/metric_types/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Memilih metric type benar untuk request, latency, pool, backlog. | Counter dipakai untuk nilai yang turun-naik atau histogram tidak ada untuk latency. |
| 3 | **Labels / Dimensions** | Label seperti service, method, status, endpoint. | Query dan dashboard granular. | Cardinality explosion. |
| 4 | **JVM Metrics** | Heap, GC, thread, classloader, direct buffer. Micrometer menyediakan binder untuk JVM metrics. ([Micrometer Docs](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/reference/jvm.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug OOM, GC pause, thread leak. | Melihat memory container tapi tidak tahu heap/non-heap. |
| 5 | **HTTP Server Metrics** | RPS, latency, status code, in-flight. | Kesehatan REST API utama. | Tidak tahu endpoint mana lambat/error. |
| 6 | **DB Pool Metrics** | Active/idle/pending/max connections. | MyBatis/PostgreSQL bottleneck. | Scale-out membunuh DB tanpa terlihat awal. |
| 7 | **Downstream Metrics** | Latency/error/retry per dependency. | Service-to-service reliability. | Error terlihat di caller tapi dependency tidak jelas. |
| 8 | **Kubernetes Metrics** | Pod ready, restarts, CPU, memory, throttling, OOMKilled. | Runtime health dan capacity. | Incident dianggap app bug padahal resource issue. |
| 9 | **SLI/SLO** | Availability, latency, correctness, freshness. | Monitoring berbasis user impact. | Alert terlalu teknis dan noisy. |
| 10 | **Alert Design** | Alert actionable, threshold tepat, burn rate. | Mengurangi alert fatigue. | Banyak alert tidak penting atau terlambat. |
| 11 | **Dashboard Design** | Golden signals + dependency + saturation. | Cepat triage incident. | Dashboard penuh metric tapi tidak menjawab. |
| 12 | **Instrumentation Strategy** | Manual, library, agent, sidecar/collector. | Jersey app non-Spring tetap observable. | Metric tidak konsisten antar service. |
| 13 | **High Cardinality Control** | Jangan label dengan `orderId`, `userId`, raw path. | Prometheus stability. | Time-series meledak dan backend mahal. |
| 14 | **Correlation with Logs/Traces** | Metric spike → trace/log terkait. | Triage cepat. | Metrics menunjukkan masalah tapi tidak bisa drill-down. |

---

# 4. Metrics vs Logs vs Traces

| Signal | Menjawab | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Metrics** | “Apakah sistem memburuk?” | Error rate 5xx naik ke 3%. |
| **Logs** | “Event spesifik apa yang terjadi?” | `DB_TIMEOUT` untuk requestId tertentu. |
| **Traces** | “Request menghabiskan waktu di mana?” | 2.7s di payment-api, 300ms di DB. |

Contoh incident:

```text id="lv11yj"
Alert:
  order-api p95 latency > 1s

Metrics:
  latency naik, DB pool pending threads naik

Traces:
  banyak span lambat di PostgreSQL query

Logs:
  WARN slow database operation OrderMapper.findById

Kesimpulan:
  bottleneck DB/query/pool, bukan Kubernetes DNS atau Ingress.
```

---

# 5. Prometheus Metric Types

## Counter

Nilai hanya naik atau reset saat process restart.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="id5pgq"
http_requests_total
http_errors_total
orders_created_total
downstream_requests_total
camunda_tasks_completed_total
```

Contoh:

```text id="q8hzog"
order_api_http_requests_total{method="GET",route="/orders/{id}",status="200"} 12345
```

## Gauge

Nilai bisa naik dan turun.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="6033kg"
http_requests_in_flight
db_pool_active_connections
db_pool_idle_connections
jvm_threads_live
kafka_consumer_lag
camunda_active_tasks
```

## Histogram

Mengukur distribusi nilai, terutama latency/duration/size.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="r7pelv"
http_server_duration_seconds
db_query_duration_seconds
downstream_request_duration_seconds
camunda_task_duration_seconds
```

## Summary

Juga untuk distribusi, tetapi dalam Prometheus banyak tim lebih memilih histogram karena lebih cocok untuk agregasi antar instance.

Rule praktis:

```text id="4cjccw"
Rate/count:
  Counter

Current state:
  Gauge

Latency/duration/size:
  Histogram

Avoid Summary unless you know aggregation trade-off.
```

Prometheus menyimpan metrics sebagai time series dengan timestamp dan label key-value, sehingga desain label sangat menentukan performa dan kegunaan query. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/introduction/overview/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Label Design dan Cardinality

Labels membuat metric bisa dianalisis.

Baik:

```text id="jvoa2n"
service="order-api"
namespace="order-prod"
method="GET"
route="/orders/{id}"
status="200"
dependency="payment-api"
operation="authorizePayment"
```

Buruk:

```text id="qs1pfc"
orderId="ORD-928381827"
userId="user-123456"
email="alice@example.com"
rawPath="/orders/ORD-928381827"
exceptionMessage="timeout after 3021ms to host x"
```

Kenapa buruk?

```text id="3az1js"
High cardinality.
Setiap value unik bisa membuat time series baru.
Backend metrics bisa mahal/lambat/rusak.
```

Gunakan templated route, bukan raw path:

```text id="p6yn43"
Baik:
  route="/orders/{id}"

Buruk:
  path="/orders/ORD-123"
```

---

# 7. Golden Signals untuk REST API

Untuk Jersey REST API, minimal monitor empat golden signals:

```text id="h9i52o"
Traffic:
  request rate

Errors:
  4xx/5xx/error code rate

Latency:
  p50/p95/p99 request duration

Saturation:
  CPU, memory, threads, DB pool, in-flight requests
```

Contoh metric:

```text id="jzj95p"
http_server_requests_seconds_count
http_server_requests_seconds_bucket
http_server_requests_seconds_sum
http_requests_in_flight
```

Dashboard utama seharusnya langsung menjawab:

```text id="51riwb"
Apakah request masuk normal?
Apakah error naik?
Endpoint mana yang lambat?
Apakah DB pool saturated?
Apakah Pod restart/OOM?
Apakah dependency tertentu melambat?
```

---

# 8. HTTP Metrics untuk Jersey/JAX-RS

Field penting:

| Metric | Type | Label | Tujuan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `http_server_requests_total` | Counter | method, route, status | Request/error rate. |
| `http_server_duration_seconds` | Histogram | method, route, status | Latency percentile. |
| `http_server_active_requests` | Gauge | route optional | In-flight request. |
| `http_server_request_size_bytes` | Histogram | route | Payload issue. |
| `http_server_response_size_bytes` | Histogram | route | Large response issue. |

Untuk Jersey non-Spring, bisa instrument via filter.

Pseudo-code:

```java id="tco43s"
@Provider
public class MetricsFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {
    private static final String START = "metrics.startNanos";

    private final MeterRegistry registry;

    public MetricsFilter(MeterRegistry registry) {
        this.registry = registry;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        requestContext.setProperty(START, System.nanoTime());
        registry.counter("http_server_requests_started_total",
                "method", requestContext.getMethod()
        ).increment();
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        long start = (long) requestContext.getProperty(START);
        double durationSeconds = (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000_000.0;

        String method = requestContext.getMethod();
        String route = resolveRouteTemplateSafely(requestContext);
        String status = String.valueOf(responseContext.getStatus());

        registry.timer("http_server_requests",
                "method", method,
                "route", route,
                "status", status
        ).record(Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start));
    }

    private String resolveRouteTemplateSafely(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        // Implement using Jersey matched resource if available.
        // Fallback to controlled template, not raw unbounded path.
        return "unknown";
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="efqbeg"
Jangan pakai raw URI sebagai label.
Gunakan route template.
```

---

# 9. JVM Metrics

Untuk Java 17+ service, wajib monitor:

| Area | Metric | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|---|
| Heap | used/committed/max | OOM/leak/GC pressure. |
| Non-heap | metaspace/code cache | Classloader/metaspace issue. |
| GC | pause count/duration | Latency spike. |
| Threads | live/daemon/peak/states | Thread leak, blocking. |
| Classes | loaded/unloaded | Dynamic class growth. |
| Direct buffer | used/capacity/count | Native/direct memory issue. |
| Process CPU | process CPU usage | JVM CPU vs container CPU. |
| Uptime | process uptime | Restart/crash detection. |

Micrometer menyediakan JVM metric binders seperti memory, GC, threads, classes, dan virtual thread metrics untuk Java 21. Untuk stack Java 17, binder JVM klasik tetap relevan. ([Micrometer Docs](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/reference/jvm.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh Micrometer setup konseptual:

```java id="wxxsvc"
MeterRegistry registry = new PrometheusMeterRegistry(PrometheusConfig.DEFAULT);

new ClassLoaderMetrics().bindTo(registry);
new JvmMemoryMetrics().bindTo(registry);
new JvmGcMetrics().bindTo(registry);
new JvmThreadMetrics().bindTo(registry);
new ProcessorMetrics().bindTo(registry);
new UptimeMetrics().bindTo(registry);
```

---

# 10. Container/Kubernetes Metrics

Metrics Kubernetes penting:

| Metric | Arti |
|---|---|
| CPU usage | Apakah container memakai CPU tinggi. |
| CPU throttling | Apakah CPU limit membatasi app. |
| Memory working set/RSS | Memory aktual container. |
| Restart count | Crash/OOM/liveness restart. |
| OOMKilled | Container mati karena memory. |
| Pod Ready | Apakah Pod menerima traffic. |
| Pending Pods | Scheduling/resource issue. |
| HPA desired/current replicas | Autoscaling behavior. |

Commands:

```bash id="a0n6r2"
kubectl top pod -n order-prod
```

```bash id="3j8uj9"
kubectl get hpa -n order-prod
```

```bash id="d7lf47"
kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n order-prod
```

Metrics Server memang difokuskan untuk resource metrics CPU/memory autoscaling dan debugging dasar; application metrics tetap perlu pipeline lain seperti Prometheus/OpenTelemetry/vendor platform. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-cluster/resource-metrics-pipeline/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 11. DB Pool Metrics untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL

Karena stack Anda memakai MyBatis + PostgreSQL, DB pool metrics sangat penting.

Jika menggunakan HikariCP, monitor:

```text id="g6z9gn"
hikaricp_connections_active
hikaricp_connections_idle
hikaricp_connections_pending
hikaricp_connections_max
hikaricp_connections_min
hikaricp_connections_timeout_total
hikaricp_connections_creation_seconds
hikaricp_connections_usage_seconds
```

Interpretasi:

| Gejala | Makna |
|---|---|
| active mendekati max | Pool saturated. |
| pending > 0 | Thread menunggu connection. |
| timeout meningkat | Request gagal karena tidak dapat connection. |
| idle selalu 0 | Pool underprovisioned atau DB lambat. |
| active tinggi + DB CPU tinggi | DB bottleneck. |
| active tinggi + app CPU rendah | I/O wait atau DB latency. |

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="z0w9pf"
HTTP latency sering naik bukan karena CPU Java,
tetapi karena DB pool pending dan query latency.
```

---

# 12. Query / MyBatis Operation Metrics

Jangan hanya ukur pool. Ukur operasi DB penting.

Metric:

```text id="f8lkbn"
db_operation_duration_seconds{operation="OrderMapper.findById"}
db_operation_errors_total{operation="OrderMapper.insertOrder",sqlState="23505"}
db_operation_slow_total{operation="OrderMapper.searchOrders"}
```

Contoh wrapper sederhana:

```java id="tcmy4h"
public final class MeteredOrderRepository {
    private final OrderMapper mapper;
    private final MeterRegistry registry;

    public MeteredOrderRepository(OrderMapper mapper, MeterRegistry registry) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.registry = registry;
    }

    public Order findById(String orderId) {
        return registry.timer("db_operation_duration",
                "operation", "OrderMapper.findById"
        ).record(() -> mapper.findById(orderId));
    }
}
```

Jangan jadikan SQL raw sebagai label. Gunakan nama operasi yang bounded.

---

# 13. Downstream HTTP Metrics

Untuk setiap dependency HTTP:

```text id="16x2g0"
dependency request count
dependency error count
dependency latency histogram
dependency timeout count
dependency retry count
circuit breaker state if used
```

Metric contoh:

```text id="ikpcqa"
downstream_http_requests_total{
  dependency="payment-api",
  operation="authorizePayment",
  method="POST",
  status="504"
}

downstream_http_duration_seconds_bucket{
  dependency="payment-api",
  operation="authorizePayment"
}

downstream_http_timeouts_total{
  dependency="payment-api",
  operation="authorizePayment"
}
```

Label `dependency` dan `operation` harus bounded.

Jangan label:

```text id="tyxjrx"
url="http://payment-api/payments/ORD-123/auth"
requestId="req-..."
```

---

# 14. Camunda Worker Metrics

Untuk Camunda worker, monitor:

| Metric | Type | Arti |
|---|---|---|
| `camunda_tasks_fetched_total` | Counter | Task yang diambil. |
| `camunda_tasks_completed_total` | Counter | Task sukses. |
| `camunda_tasks_failed_total` | Counter | Task gagal. |
| `camunda_task_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Durasi handler. |
| `camunda_active_tasks` | Gauge | Task sedang diproses. |
| `camunda_task_retries_left` | Histogram/Gauge | Retry health. |
| `camunda_fetch_errors_total` | Counter | Engine/client issue. |
| `camunda_backlog` | Gauge | Jika tersedia dari engine/query. |

Label:

```text id="8c9snv"
topic
worker
result
errorCode
```

Hati-hati label:

```text id="a9uivb"
processInstanceId
businessKey
```

Itu high cardinality. Lebih cocok untuk logs/traces, bukan metric label.

---

# 15. Redis/Kafka Metrics jika Ada

## Redis client metrics

```text id="z61ust"
redis_command_duration_seconds
redis_command_errors_total
redis_pool_active
redis_pool_pending
redis_timeouts_total
```

Label bounded:

```text id="zfuhw4"
command="GET"
command="SET"
operation="cacheGetOrder"
```

Jangan label key Redis.

## Kafka consumer metrics

```text id="medimp"
consumer_lag
records_consumed_total
processing_duration_seconds
commit_latency_seconds
rebalance_total
poll_duration_seconds
consumer_errors_total
```

Untuk Kafka, lag adalah SLI penting bagi freshness/backlog, tetapi harus dibaca bersama processing rate dan error rate.

# 16. Business Metrics

Technical metrics tidak cukup. Tambahkan business metrics yang aman.

Contoh CPQ/order-management style:

```text id="v3ku8y"
orders_created_total
orders_creation_failed_total
orders_cancelled_total
quote_submitted_total
quote_priced_total
order_workflow_completed_total
order_workflow_duration_seconds
order_provisioning_failed_total
```

Label aman:

```text id="jgs2ze"
channel
productType bounded
result
reasonCode bounded
```

Hindari label:

```text id="odzzmp"
customerId
orderId
email
phoneNumber
```

Business metrics membantu menjawab:

```text id="09666d"
Apakah user sukses membuat order?
Apakah workflow stuck?
Apakah failure technical berdampak bisnis?
```

---

# 17. `/metrics` Endpoint

Jika memakai Prometheus pull model, expose endpoint:

```text id="14eu8l"
GET /metrics
```

Contoh Jersey resource konseptual:

```java id="i7ebmw"
@Path("/metrics")
public class MetricsResource {
    private final PrometheusMeterRegistry prometheusRegistry;

    public MetricsResource(PrometheusMeterRegistry prometheusRegistry) {
        this.prometheusRegistry = prometheusRegistry;
    }

    @GET
    @Produces("text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8")
    public String scrape() {
        return prometheusRegistry.scrape();
    }
}
```

Security:

```text id="43bhfa"
Jangan expose /metrics public lewat Ingress.
Allow hanya Prometheus/collector via NetworkPolicy.
Pastikan metrics tidak mengandung PII/high cardinality labels.
```

---

# 18. OpenTelemetry Metrics Export

Jika memakai OpenTelemetry Collector, bisa export via OTLP:

```text id="th42no"
Java app
  → OTLP metrics exporter
  → OpenTelemetry Collector
  → Prometheus remote write / vendor backend / cloud monitoring
```

Contoh env konseptual:

```yaml id="p4d7pc"
env:
  - name: OTEL_SERVICE_NAME
    value: "order-api"

  - name: OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES
    value: "deployment.environment=prod,k8s.namespace.name=order-prod"

  - name: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
    value: "http://otel-collector.observability.svc.cluster.local:4317"

  - name: OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER
    value: "otlp"
```

OpenTelemetry Java agent dapat di-attach ke aplikasi Java 8+ untuk menangkap telemetry dari banyak library tanpa perubahan kode, lalu diekspor dalam berbagai format. ([GitHub](https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-java-instrumentation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Prometheus Scrape di Kubernetes

Pola umum:

```text id="c1jjsm"
Service exposes port metrics/http
Prometheus discovers target
Prometheus scrapes /metrics
Metrics stored as time series
Grafana dashboard queries PromQL
Alertmanager sends alerts
```

Contoh Service annotation, jika platform masih memakai annotation-based discovery:

```yaml id="g1ydsy"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
  annotations:
    prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
    prometheus.io/path: "/metrics"
    prometheus.io/port: "8080"
spec:
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 80
      targetPort: 8080
```

Jika memakai Prometheus Operator, biasanya pakai `ServiceMonitor`/`PodMonitor`, tergantung platform.

---

# 20. SLI dan SLO

## SLI

SLI adalah metric yang mewakili kualitas layanan.

Contoh untuk `order-api`:

| SLI | Definisi |
|---|---|
| Availability | Proporsi request non-5xx untuk endpoint utama. |
| Latency | Proporsi request selesai di bawah threshold. |
| Correctness | Proporsi order creation berhasil tanpa business error. |
| Freshness | Workflow/order status updated dalam waktu tertentu. |
| Dependency success | Payment/Camunda/DB operation success rate. |

## SLO

SLO adalah target.

Contoh:

```text id="ebgejq"
Availability:
  99.9% successful requests over 30 days

Latency:
  95% GET /orders/{id} < 300ms over 7 days
  99% POST /orders < 1s over 7 days

Workflow:
  99% order workflow completed under 5 minutes
```

Metric tanpa SLO sering berubah menjadi dashboard dekoratif. SLO mengubah metric menjadi keputusan operasional.

---

# 21. Alert Design

Alert harus actionable.

Buruk:

```text id="o2em6d"
CPU > 80% for 1 minute
```

Bisa noise jika service normal memang CPU burst.

Lebih baik:

```text id="bt7l1f"
High 5xx error rate for order-api
AND traffic above minimum threshold
for 5 minutes
```

Atau:

```text id="hxb2t2"
SLO burn rate high:
  order-api availability error budget burning too fast
```

Alert hierarchy:

```text id="bve13p"
Page:
  user-impacting, urgent, needs human action now

Ticket:
  degradation/non-urgent

Dashboard only:
  useful context, not alert
```

---

# 22. Recommended Alerts

## Availability

```text id="cm7stt"
5xx rate > 1% for 5m
and request rate > minimum traffic threshold
```

## Latency

```text id="if3jsi"
p95 latency > SLO threshold for 10m
```

## DB pool saturation

```text id="ptdcnd"
db_pool_pending_connections > 0 for 5m
or active/max > 0.9 for 10m
```

## Pod restarts

```text id="qqfjaq"
restart count increases
especially with OOMKilled or CrashLoopBackOff
```

## Memory/OOM

```text id="spra9l"
container memory > 90% limit for sustained period
or OOMKilled occurred
```

## CPU throttling

```text id="12ovr7"
CPU throttling ratio high
and latency/error rate elevated
```

## Dependency failure

```text id="e3fx1e"
downstream payment-api timeout/error rate > threshold
```

## Camunda worker backlog

```text id="p0xikq"
task backlog growing
and completion rate insufficient
```

---

# 23. Alert Anti-patterns

```text id="5e384o"
Alert on every single 500.
Alert on CPU only without user impact.
Alert on memory high for Java without trend/context.
Alert on low traffic endpoint with one failure.
Alert without runbook.
Alert that no one owns.
Alert that fires during every deploy.
Alert for condition that self-heals in seconds.
```

Better:

```text id="x2dqro"
Alert on sustained rate, ratio, or burn rate.
Add minimum traffic guard.
Tie alert to SLO or saturation.
Include service, namespace, dashboard link, runbook, likely causes.
```

---

# 24. Dashboard Design

## Executive Service Health

```text id="c5zg6a"
Availability SLI
Latency SLI
Request rate
Error budget burn
Top failing endpoints
Top slow endpoints
Current version/rollout status
```

## Engineer Triage Dashboard

```text id="tg1ufa"
HTTP RPS by route/status
p50/p95/p99 latency by route
5xx/4xx rate
Pod restarts/OOMKilled
CPU/memory/throttling
JVM heap/GC/thread metrics
DB pool active/idle/pending
PostgreSQL query latency
Downstream latency/errors
Camunda/Kafka/Redis metrics if relevant
```

## Capacity Dashboard

```text id="ur99yn"
HPA current/desired replicas
CPU request/limit usage
Memory limit usage
DB connection budget
maxReplicas × pool size
queue/backlog
node scheduling pressure
```

---

# 25. RED, USE, and Four Golden Signals

## RED for services

```text id="suzau1"
Rate
Errors
Duration
```

Cocok untuk REST API.

## USE for resources

```text id="6asdxq"
Utilization
Saturation
Errors
```

Cocok untuk CPU, memory, DB pool, thread pool.

## Four Golden Signals

```text id="ibwhgg"
Latency
Traffic
Errors
Saturation
```

Praktisnya:

```text id="3u7151"
REST endpoint:
  RED

DB pool/thread pool/container:
  USE

Service dashboard:
  Golden signals
```

---

# 26. Metrics untuk HPA

HPA sering memakai CPU utilization, tetapi untuk service I/O-bound bisa perlu custom metrics.

HPA metric candidates:

| Workload | Metric |
|---|---|
| CPU-bound Jersey API | CPU utilization |
| I/O-bound REST API | in-flight requests / RPS per Pod |
| DB-bound API | hati-hati; DB pool saturation bukan selalu alasan scale-out |
| Camunda worker | backlog / active tasks / completion rate |
| Kafka consumer | consumer lag, max replicas ≤ partitions |
| Queue worker | queue depth / oldest message age |

Reminder dari Seri 10:

```text id="nbbcev"
Scaling app tanpa menghitung DB/downstream capacity bisa memperbesar incident.
```

---

# 27. Instrumentation Strategy

## Option A - Java Agent

Kelebihan:

```text id="x0bds4"
cepat diterapkan
banyak auto-instrumentation
minim code change
bagus untuk traces/basic metrics
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="pyjm0x"
tidak semua business metrics otomatis
version/config agent perlu governance
debugging instrumentation bisa kompleks
```

## Option B - Library / Manual Instrumentation

Kelebihan:

```text id="5awch1"
kontrol metric names/labels
business metrics bisa tepat
tidak bergantung penuh pada auto-instrumentation
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="hnhztz"
butuh code changes
perlu standard internal agar konsisten
```

## Option C - Hybrid

Recommended:

```text id="e875kw"
OpenTelemetry Java agent untuk baseline telemetry
Manual instrumentation untuk business metrics dan domain-specific metrics
Micrometer/OTel bridge sesuai platform
```

---

# 28. Kubernetes Manifest untuk Metrics Endpoint

```yaml id="2n50c5"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
spec:
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 80
      targetPort: 8080
    - name: metrics
      port: 9090
      targetPort: 9090
```

Jika metrics di port sama dengan app:

```text id="k4l7s9"
http://order-api/metrics
```

Jika metrics port terpisah:

```text id="emf2jx"
http://order-api:9090/metrics
```

Security choice:

```text id="xj4jwk"
Same port:
  simpler app

Separate port:
  easier NetworkPolicy separation
  easier not to expose via Ingress
```

---

# 29. NetworkPolicy untuk Metrics Scrape

Jika default deny aktif, allow Prometheus/collector scrape.

```yaml id="0f78ho"
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-prometheus-scrape-order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  policyTypes:
    - Ingress
  ingress:
    - from:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              name: observability
          podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: prometheus
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 9090
```

Jangan expose `/metrics` public lewat Ingress kecuali ada kontrol kuat dan alasan jelas.

---

# 30. Example PromQL Queries

## Request rate

```promql id="o7apcc"
sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{service="order-api"}[5m]))
```

## 5xx rate

```promql id="xtasjj"
sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{service="order-api",status=~"5.."}[5m]))
/
sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{service="order-api"}[5m]))
```

## p95 latency

```promql id="do1cci"
histogram_quantile(
  0.95,
  sum by (le, route) (
    rate(http_server_requests_seconds_bucket{service="order-api"}[5m])
  )
)
```

## DB pool saturation

```promql id="n8d8rn"
max(hikaricp_connections_active{service="order-api"})
/
max(hikaricp_connections_max{service="order-api"})
```

## JVM heap usage ratio

```promql id="vcyp0r"
sum(jvm_memory_used_bytes{service="order-api",area="heap"})
/
sum(jvm_memory_max_bytes{service="order-api",area="heap"})
```

## Pod restart increase

```promql id="9ezfk4"
increase(kube_pod_container_status_restarts_total{
  namespace="order-prod",
  container="order-api"
}[10m]) > 0
```

Metric names bisa berbeda tergantung Micrometer/OpenTelemetry/exporter/platform. Sesuaikan dengan naming convention yang dipakai perusahaan.

---

# 31. Common Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Metric yang Dilihat |
|---|---|---|
| Latency naik, CPU normal | DB/downstream lambat, thread pool wait | DB pool pending, downstream latency |
| 5xx naik setelah deploy | Bug versi baru/config issue | error rate by version/pod |
| Pod restart | Crash/OOM/liveness failure | restart count, OOMKilled, logs previous |
| OOMKilled tapi heap rendah | native/direct/thread memory | RSS, direct buffer, thread count |
| HPA tidak scale | metrics missing/request salah | HPA status, CPU request |
| HPA scale tapi error naik | bottleneck DB/downstream | DB active/pending, downstream error |
| Metrics hilang | scrape failure/network policy | target up, scrape errors |
| Prometheus high cardinality | label raw path/user/orderId | series count by metric/label |
| Alert noisy | threshold terlalu sensitif | alert history, traffic guard |
| Dashboard tidak membantu | tidak ada SLI/dependency view | redesign around golden signals |

---

# 32. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Hanya CPU dan Memory

```text id="hz8s8s"
CPU normal
memory normal
tetapi order creation gagal 20%
```

CPU/memory tidak cukup. Tambahkan HTTP, DB, dependency, business metrics.

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Label dengan ID Unik

Buruk:

```text id="8i3cmz"
http_requests_total{orderId="ORD-123456"}
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="70bix2"
http_requests_total{route="/orders/{id}"}
```

Order ID masuk log/trace, bukan metric label.

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Alert tanpa User Impact

Buruk:

```text id="au8qqk"
Alert: CPU 85% for 1 minute
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="gr6e1i"
Alert: p95 latency above SLO and request rate significant
```

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Tidak Memantau DB Pool

REST API MyBatis bisa terlihat sehat dari CPU tetapi stuck karena pool habis.

Wajib monitor:

```text id="5bp63i"
active
idle
pending
timeout
max
usage duration
```

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Metrics Endpoint Public

Buruk:

```text id="xirv2w"
https://api.company.com/metrics
```

tanpa auth/restriction.

Metrics bisa membocorkan:

```text id="egfi91"
service topology
dependency names
internal paths
error details
business volumes
```

---

# 33. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| HTTP | Apakah RPS, error rate, latency, in-flight tersedia? |
| JVM | Apakah heap, non-heap, GC, threads tersedia? |
| DB | Apakah pool active/idle/pending/timeout tersedia? |
| MyBatis | Apakah query/operation latency penting diukur? |
| Downstream | Apakah dependency latency/error/retry diukur per dependency? |
| Kubernetes | Apakah restarts/OOM/CPU/memory/throttling tersedia? |
| Business | Apakah success/failure domain utama diukur? |
| Labels | Apakah label bounded dan tidak high cardinality? |
| SLI/SLO | Apakah service punya SLI/SLO jelas? |
| Alerts | Apakah alert actionable dan berbasis impact/saturation? |
| Dashboard | Apakah dashboard menjawab golden signals dan dependency? |
| Security | Apakah `/metrics` tidak public dan bebas PII/secrets? |
| HPA | Apakah metric autoscaling sesuai workload? |
| Correlation | Apakah metrics bisa dikaitkan dengan logs/traces? |
| Ownership | Apakah setiap alert punya owner dan runbook? |

---

# 34. Ringkasan Seri 16

```text id="e60mmc"
Metrics menjawab:
  apakah sistem sehat secara agregat?

Untuk Java/Jersey di Kubernetes, wajib punya:
  Kubernetes/container metrics
  JVM metrics
  HTTP metrics
  DB pool metrics
  downstream metrics
  business metrics
  SLI/SLO metrics
```

Metric type:

```text id="qlovb7"
Counter:
  nilai naik, cocok untuk total count/error

Gauge:
  nilai naik-turun, cocok untuk active connections/backlog

Histogram:
  distribusi latency/duration/size

Summary:
  gunakan hati-hati, histogram biasanya lebih mudah diagregasi
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="2eg3w6"
Monitoring yang baik tidak berhenti di “Pod hidup”.
Monitoring yang baik menjawab:
apakah user berhasil,
apakah latency sesuai SLO,
apakah dependency sehat,
apakah resource saturated,
dan apakah sistem sedang membakar error budget.
```
