# Kubernetes Series - Seri 17/24  
## Distributed Tracing dan OpenTelemetry di Kubernetes: Trace Context Propagation, Spans, Service Map, Jersey Filters, Downstream HTTP, MyBatis/PostgreSQL, Camunda Worker, dan Trace-Log Correlation

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana melacak satu request end-to-end ketika request tersebut melewati banyak service, database, worker, broker, dan Pod Kubernetes?**

Logging membantu melihat event spesifik. Metrics membantu melihat agregat. **Tracing** membantu melihat perjalanan request lintas service: siapa memanggil siapa, berapa lama, error di mana, dan dependency mana yang menjadi bottleneck.

OpenTelemetry adalah framework observability open source dan vendor-neutral untuk menangkap telemetry seperti traces, metrics, dan logs dari aplikasi. OpenTelemetry juga menyediakan API, SDK, agent, dan collector agar telemetry bisa dikirim ke backend observability berbeda. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Distributed tracing memodelkan satu request sebagai **trace**.

```text id="pmfc2k"
Trace
└── Span: inbound HTTP request ke order-api
    ├── Span: validate request
    ├── Span: query PostgreSQL via MyBatis
    ├── Span: call customer-api
    ├── Span: call payment-api
    └── Span: publish/fetch/process async task if applicable
```

Istilah inti:

| Istilah | Arti |
|---|---|
| **Trace** | Seluruh perjalanan satu request/operation lintas service. |
| **Span** | Satu unit kerja dalam trace, misalnya HTTP handler, DB query, downstream call. |
| **Trace ID** | ID global untuk seluruh trace. |
| **Span ID** | ID untuk span tertentu. |
| **Parent span** | Span yang memicu span lain. |
| **Root span** | Span awal trace, biasanya inbound request pertama. |
| **Context propagation** | Mekanisme meneruskan trace context antar service/process. |

OpenTelemetry menjelaskan **context propagation** sebagai mekanisme yang memungkinkan traces membangun informasi kausal lintas service/process/network boundary, sekaligus membantu korelasi traces, metrics, dan logs. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/context-propagation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram End-to-End Trace

```text id="6z36ah"
Client
  │
  │ traceparent: 00-<traceId>-<spanId>-01
  ▼
Ingress / Gateway
  │
  ▼
order-api Pod
  │
  ├── SERVER span: HTTP POST /orders
  │
  ├── INTERNAL span: validate order request
  │
  ├── CLIENT span: SELECT/INSERT PostgreSQL via MyBatis/JDBC
  │
  ├── CLIENT span: HTTP GET customer-api
  │
  ├── CLIENT span: HTTP POST payment-api
  │
  └── PRODUCER/CLIENT span: start Camunda workflow / publish message
  │
  ▼
customer-api Pod
  └── SERVER span: HTTP GET /customers/{id}
  │
  ▼
payment-api Pod
  └── SERVER span: HTTP POST /payments/authorize
  │
  ▼
PostgreSQL
Camunda
Kafka/RabbitMQ/Redis if applicable
```

Dengan tracing, incident seperti ini menjadi terlihat:

```text id="ue220v"
POST /orders lambat 3.2s

Trace breakdown:
  order-api handler          120ms
  PostgreSQL insert          80ms
  customer-api call          90ms
  payment-api call        2,850ms
  response serialization     20ms

Kesimpulan:
  bottleneck ada di payment-api, bukan order-api CPU.
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Jersey/JAX-RS | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Trace Mental Model** | Trace, span, traceId, spanId, parent-child relation. | Melacak request lintas Jersey API, DB, downstream, worker. | Incident sulit direkonstruksi. |
| 2 | **Context Propagation** | Meneruskan trace context antar service via headers. | Jersey Client/OpenFeign/OpenAPI client harus propagate context. | Trace putus antar service. |
| 3 | **W3C Trace Context** | `traceparent` dan `tracestate`. W3C mendefinisikan `traceparent` sebagai header umum untuk merepresentasikan incoming request dalam tracing system. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Standard header lintas vendor/tool. | Service memakai header custom sendiri-sendiri. |
| 4 | **OpenTelemetry Java** | Agent, SDK, manual instrumentation. | Java 17+ app bisa auto/manual trace. | Instrumentasi tidak konsisten. |
| 5 | **Jersey Server Instrumentation** | Inbound HTTP span untuk resource method. | Trace root/child span untuk JAX-RS request. | Tidak tahu endpoint mana lambat. |
| 6 | **Jersey Client Instrumentation** | Outbound HTTP span dan context injection. | Downstream call terlihat sebagai child span. | Caller/callee trace tidak tersambung. |
| 7 | **JDBC/MyBatis Tracing** | DB span via JDBC instrumentation atau manual wrapper. | Query latency terlihat dalam trace. | Semua waktu terlihat “di service” tanpa tahu DB bottleneck. |
| 8 | **Semantic Conventions** | Nama attribute standar untuk HTTP, DB, messaging, Kubernetes resource. OpenTelemetry Semantic Conventions memberi nama umum untuk operasi dan data telemetry agar konsisten lintas codebase/library/platform. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/semantic-conventions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Trace query/dashboard lebih konsisten. | Attribute kacau, query susah. |
| 9 | **Async/Worker Tracing** | Propagasi context ke queue/message/workflow. | Camunda worker/Kafka/RabbitMQ trace tidak putus. | Async flow tidak terlihat. |
| 10 | **Trace Sampling** | Parent-based, ratio, tail sampling. | Mengontrol volume dan biaya trace. | Terlalu mahal atau trace penting hilang. |
| 11 | **Trace-Log Correlation** | `traceId`/`spanId` masuk log MDC. | Dari trace bisa lompat ke log detail. | Trace dan log tidak bisa dikaitkan. |
| 12 | **Kubernetes Metadata Enrichment** | Tambah namespace, pod, deployment, node. OpenTelemetry k8s attributes processor bisa menambahkan metadata seperti namespace, Pod, deployment, dan node ke telemetry. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/platforms/kubernetes/collector/components/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Tahu error dari Pod/version/node mana. | Trace tidak berguna untuk debugging Kubernetes runtime. |
| 13 | **Collector Pipeline** | App/agent → OTel Collector → backend. | Standardisasi export OTLP, batching, enrichment. | Setiap service kirim langsung ke vendor dengan config beda. |
| 14 | **Privacy/Security** | Jangan masukkan PII/secret sebagai span attribute. | Trace sering disimpan lama dan searchable. | Kebocoran data sensitif di observability backend. |

---

# 4. Trace, Span, dan Parent-Child Relationship

Contoh trace untuk `POST /orders`:

```text id="i3cd45"
Trace ID: 4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736

Span A: HTTP POST /orders                         3200ms
├── Span B: OrderValidation.validate                20ms
├── Span C: PostgreSQL INSERT order                 80ms
├── Span D: HTTP GET customer-api/customers/{id}    90ms
├── Span E: HTTP POST payment-api/authorize       2850ms
└── Span F: Camunda start process                  120ms
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="wxepyk"
Span A adalah parent.
Span B-F adalah child span.
Payment-api span paling lambat.
Order-api bukan root cause utama, walau request order-api terlihat lambat.
```

Tanpa tracing, Anda mungkin hanya melihat log:

```text id="5u581c"
order-api request completed durationMs=3200
```

Dengan tracing, Anda tahu **3200ms itu habis di mana**.

---

# 5. W3C Trace Context: `traceparent` dan `tracestate`

Modern distributed tracing sebaiknya mengikuti W3C Trace Context.

Header utama:

```text id="7e1xqv"
traceparent: 00-4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736-00f067aa0ba902b7-01
```

Struktur konseptual:

```text id="i75qgv"
version
trace-id
parent-id
trace-flags
```

Header tambahan:

```text id="319fbd"
tracestate: vendor-specific state
```

W3C Trace Context mendefinisikan `traceparent` dan `tracestate`; `traceparent` membawa identitas trace umum lintas vendor, sedangkan `tracestate` membawa informasi vendor-specific. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Prinsip:

```text id="cez4ps"
Jangan membuat header tracing custom sendiri jika tidak perlu.
Gunakan traceparent/tracestate.
```

---

# 6. OpenTelemetry Deployment Model di Kubernetes

Ada beberapa pola.

## Pattern A - Java Agent + OTLP Exporter

```text id="i1ar7b"
Java app + OpenTelemetry Java agent
  → OTLP
  → OpenTelemetry Collector
  → tracing backend
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="bdqt3a"
cepat diterapkan
banyak auto-instrumentation
minim perubahan kode
bagus untuk baseline tracing
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="6wxc2r"
business/domain span tetap perlu manual instrumentation
agent config perlu governance
beberapa library mungkin belum auto-instrumented sesuai kebutuhan
```

OpenTelemetry Java instrumentation project menyediakan auto-instrumentation untuk banyak library/framework/application server dan bisa dipakai lewat Java agent. ([GitHub](https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-java-instrumentation/blob/main/docs/supported-libraries.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Pattern B - Manual SDK Instrumentation

```text id="6j6qwp"
Application code
  → OpenTelemetry API/SDK
  → manual spans/attributes
  → OTLP exporter
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="l3uqlm"
kontrol penuh
business spans akurat
attribute domain bisa distandarkan
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="f7lhlj"
butuh code changes
butuh discipline agar tidak over-instrument
```

## Pattern C - Hybrid

Recommended untuk senior engineer:

```text id="5yvrci"
Java agent:
  baseline HTTP/JDBC/client traces

Manual instrumentation:
  business operation, Camunda handler, critical MyBatis operation,
  workflow boundary, custom domain error
```

---

# 7. Kubernetes Manifest: Java Agent

Contoh sederhana memakai Java agent yang sudah ada di image atau di-mount:

```yaml id="y0n8z4"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.8.3"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.8.3
          env:
            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: >-
                -javaagent:/otel/opentelemetry-javaagent.jar
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
                -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError

            - name: OTEL_SERVICE_NAME
              value: "order-api"

            - name: OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES
              value: >-
                deployment.environment=prod,
                service.version=1.8.3,
                k8s.namespace.name=order-prod

            - name: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
              value: "http://otel-collector.observability.svc.cluster.local:4317"

            - name: OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER
              value: "otlp"

            - name: OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER
              value: "otlp"

          volumeMounts:
            - name: otel-agent
              mountPath: /otel
              readOnly: true

      volumes:
        - name: otel-agent
          configMap:
            name: otel-java-agent
```

Catatan:

```text id="cwizgx"
Cara distribusi agent tergantung standar perusahaan:
  baked into image
  initContainer download/copy
  mounted volume
  sidecar/injection platform
```

Untuk enterprise, hindari runtime download dari internet saat Pod start. Lebih aman memakai image/registry internal atau artifact yang sudah divalidasi.

---

# 8. OpenTelemetry Collector di Kubernetes

Collector sering dipakai sebagai perantara:

```text id="zuxc72"
Application Pods
  → OTLP
  → OTel Collector
  → backend tracing/metrics/logging
```

Fungsi Collector:

```text id="6tr7ac"
receive telemetry
batch/export
retry
enrich Kubernetes metadata
filter/drop attributes
tail sampling
route per environment/service
hide vendor-specific backend from app
```

Contoh konseptual Collector pipeline:

```yaml id="b4s8zm"
receivers:
  otlp:
    protocols:
      grpc:
      http:

processors:
  k8sattributes:
    auth_type: serviceAccount
    extract:
      metadata:
        - k8s.namespace.name
        - k8s.pod.name
        - k8s.deployment.name
        - k8s.node.name

  batch:

exporters:
  otlp:
    endpoint: tracing-backend.observability.svc.cluster.local:4317
    tls:
      insecure: true

service:
  pipelines:
    traces:
      receivers: [otlp]
      processors: [k8sattributes, batch]
      exporters: [otlp]
```

OpenTelemetry Kubernetes documentation menyebut `k8sattributes` processor dapat menambahkan metadata Kubernetes seperti namespace, Pod, deployment, node, labels, dan annotations ke traces, metrics, dan logs. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/platforms/kubernetes/collector/components/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Resource Attributes yang Wajib

Resource attributes menjelaskan “telemetry ini berasal dari workload apa”.

Minimal:

```text id="gm8vkw"
service.name=order-api
service.version=1.8.3
deployment.environment=prod
k8s.namespace.name=order-prod
k8s.pod.name=order-api-...
k8s.deployment.name=order-api
k8s.node.name=worker-...
```

Jika multi-cluster:

```text id="jyeok2"
k8s.cluster.name=prod-jakarta-1
cloud.provider=aws/azure/onprem
cloud.region=...
```

Manfaat:

```text id="rsm70f"
filter trace by version
lihat error hanya di namespace tertentu
lihat issue hanya di node tertentu
bandingkan staging vs prod
debug rollout version
```

---

# 10. Jersey Server Span

Inbound request harus menghasilkan server span.

Contoh semantic span:

```text id="kil9xo"
name:
  HTTP POST /orders

kind:
  SERVER

attributes:
  http.request.method=POST
  url.path=/orders
  http.response.status_code=201
  server.address=order-api
  server.port=8080
  user_agent.original=...
```

OpenTelemetry semantic conventions menyediakan skema nama attribute umum untuk berbagai operasi, termasuk HTTP, database, messaging, dan resource. Tujuannya agar telemetry dari berbagai library/platform tetap konsisten dan queryable. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/semantic-conventions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk Jersey, auto-instrumentation mungkin cukup jika runtime/library didukung. Jika tidak, pakai filter.

---

# 11. Manual Jersey Filter untuk Span

Contoh konseptual manual instrumentation:

```java id="9bte5o"
@Provider
public class TracingServerFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private static final String SPAN_PROPERTY = "otel.span";
    private static final String SCOPE_PROPERTY = "otel.scope";

    private final Tracer tracer;

    public TracingServerFilter(OpenTelemetry openTelemetry) {
        this.tracer = openTelemetry.getTracer("order-api");
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String method = requestContext.getMethod();
        String path = requestContext.getUriInfo().getPath();

        Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("HTTP " + method + " " + path)
                .setSpanKind(SpanKind.SERVER)
                .setAttribute("http.request.method", method)
                .setAttribute("url.path", "/" + path)
                .startSpan();

        Scope scope = span.makeCurrent();

        requestContext.setProperty(SPAN_PROPERTY, span);
        requestContext.setProperty(SCOPE_PROPERTY, scope);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        Span span = (Span) requestContext.getProperty(SPAN_PROPERTY);
        Scope scope = (Scope) requestContext.getProperty(SCOPE_PROPERTY);

        try {
            if (span != null) {
                span.setAttribute("http.response.status_code", responseContext.getStatus());

                if (responseContext.getStatus() >= 500) {
                    span.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR);
                }
            }
        } finally {
            if (scope != null) {
                scope.close();
            }
            if (span != null) {
                span.end();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="wkkbuv"
Ini contoh konseptual.
Untuk production, perhatikan extraction context dari incoming traceparent,
route template, exception handling, async processing, dan integration dengan auto-agent.
```

Kalau memakai Java agent, jangan double-create span manual untuk hal yang sudah otomatis diinstrumentasi.

---

# 12. Exception di Span

Jika request gagal, span harus mencatat error.

```java id="k8zlig"
try {
    return service.createOrder(request);
} catch (Exception e) {
    Span current = Span.current();
    current.recordException(e);
    current.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR, e.getMessage());
    throw e;
}
```

Tapi hindari:

```text id="y874wp"
record exception di banyak layer
span error duplicate
message exception sensitif
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="vmcchp"
record exception di boundary yang jelas
gunakan error code domain
hindari attribute dengan secret/PII
```

---

# 13. Trace Context Propagation untuk Jersey Client

Outbound HTTP client harus inject context ke headers.

Dengan OpenTelemetry instrumentation, ini biasanya otomatis. Jika manual:

```java id="yqub5i"
public class OtelClientRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final TextMapPropagator propagator;

    public OtelClientRequestFilter(OpenTelemetry openTelemetry) {
        this.propagator = openTelemetry.getPropagators().getTextMapPropagator();
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        propagator.inject(
                Context.current(),
                requestContext.getHeaders(),
                (headers, key, value) -> headers.putSingle(key, value)
        );
    }
}
```

Kemudian:

```java id="e1l1dh"
Client client = ClientBuilder.newBuilder()
        .register(new OtelClientRequestFilter(openTelemetry))
        .connectTimeout(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build();
```

Result:

```text id="84p5l9"
order-api outbound call
  → includes traceparent
customer-api inbound request
  → extracts traceparent
  → continues same trace
```

---

# 14. Downstream HTTP Span

Outbound HTTP span harus menunjukkan dependency.

```text id="n78yhv"
Span:
  HTTP GET customer-api /customers/{id}

attributes:
  server.address=customer-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local
  http.request.method=GET
  url.path=/customers/{id}
  http.response.status_code=200
```

Untuk queryability, tambahkan bounded custom attribute:

```text id="e3b7u9"
peer.service=customer-api
dependency=customer-api
operation=getCustomer
```

Jangan tambahkan:

```text id="1luib2"
full URL dengan customerId/orderId sebagai attribute bernilai unik
Authorization header
request body
email/phone/customer name
```

---

# 15. MyBatis/PostgreSQL Tracing

MyBatis sendiri biasanya memakai JDBC di bawahnya. Auto-instrumentation JDBC bisa memberi DB spans. OpenTelemetry semantic conventions untuk database mendefinisikan attribute untuk database client spans, metrics, dan logs, meski beberapa versi instrumentasi lama bisa mengikuti versi semconv yang berbeda. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/db/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh DB span:

```text id="l0x6y8"
Span:
  SELECT order

kind:
  CLIENT

attributes:
  db.system.name=postgresql
  db.namespace=orderdb
  db.operation.name=SELECT
  db.collection.name=orders
```

Praktik aman:

```text id="3l83t5"
Instrument operation name:
  OrderMapper.findById
  OrderMapper.insertOrder
  OrderMapper.searchOrders

Avoid raw SQL with sensitive literals.
Avoid full SQL as high-cardinality attribute.
```

Manual wrapper:

```java id="liybex"
public Order findById(String orderId) {
    Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("OrderMapper.findById")
            .setSpanKind(SpanKind.CLIENT)
            .setAttribute("db.system.name", "postgresql")
            .setAttribute("db.operation.name", "SELECT")
            .setAttribute("db.collection.name", "orders")
            .startSpan();

    try (Scope scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
        return mapper.findById(orderId);
    } catch (Exception e) {
        span.recordException(e);
        span.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR);
        throw e;
    } finally {
        span.end();
    }
}
```

Kalau JDBC auto-instrumentation sudah aktif, manual span seperti ini bisa menjadi parent business span, bukan pengganti JDBC span.

---

# 16. Trace untuk MyBatis Query Lambat

Trace sangat berguna untuk membedakan:

```text id="cplz5y"
Service CPU lambat?
DB pool wait?
Query PostgreSQL lambat?
Downstream service lambat?
```

Ideal trace:

```text id="844q76"
HTTP POST /orders                  2400ms
├── validate request                 20ms
├── Hikari acquire connection       800ms
├── OrderMapper.insertOrder          90ms
├── PaymentClient.authorize        1400ms
└── serialize response               10ms
```

Jika `Hikari acquire connection` lama, masalahnya mungkin pool saturation, bukan query. Jika query span lama, cek PostgreSQL plan/index/lock.

---

# 17. Camunda Worker Tracing

Camunda worker sering asynchronous, sehingga tracing perlu desain khusus.

Flow:

```text id="hqyhgn"
order-api receives POST /orders
  ├── starts Camunda process
  └── stores trace context as process variable/header if safe

Later:
order-worker fetches task
  ├── extracts trace context
  ├── starts CONSUMER/INTERNAL span
  ├── handles task
  ├── calls downstream/DB
  └── completes/fails task
```

Span attributes yang berguna:

```text id="27eqoe"
camunda.topic=order-processing
camunda.worker.id=order-worker
camunda.process.definition.key=order-process
camunda.activity.id=reserveInventory
camunda.result=completed/failed/bpmn_error
```

Jangan jadikan metric label, tapi boleh sebagai trace/log attribute dengan governance:

```text id="j7f411"
processInstanceId
businessKey
orderId
```

Hati-hati PII/secret.

---

# 18. Propagasi Trace Context di Async Workflow

Masalah async:

```text id="kfka1k"
HTTP request selesai.
Worker berjalan beberapa detik/menit kemudian.
Thread/process/Pod berbeda.
Trace context tidak otomatis ikut kecuali disimpan/propagate.
```

Solusi umum:

```text id="sc1cmr"
Simpan traceparent/tracestate sebagai metadata message/workflow/task header.
Saat worker mengambil task, extract context.
Buat span baru dengan parent dari extracted context.
```

Pseudo-code menyimpan:

```java id="ml10yz"
String traceparent = currentTraceparentHeader();

Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
        "orderId", orderId,
        "traceparent", traceparent
);

// start Camunda process with variables/headers sesuai API yang dipakai
```

Pseudo-code worker:

```java id="hv7ple"
String traceparent = task.getVariable("traceparent");

Context parentContext = extractContextFromTraceparent(traceparent);

Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("Camunda task order-processing")
        .setParent(parentContext)
        .setSpanKind(SpanKind.CONSUMER)
        .startSpan();

try (Scope scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
    handleTask(task);
} finally {
    span.end();
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="ipnrbn"
Detail API Camunda berbeda tergantung Camunda 7 external task, Camunda 8 job worker, atau client library.
Prinsipnya sama: carry trace context as metadata, not as business identity.
```

---

# 19. Kafka/RabbitMQ/Redis Messaging Tracing

Untuk messaging, trace harus membedakan producer dan consumer.

```text id="0tjj4g"
order-api
  └── PRODUCER span: publish OrderCreated event

order-event-worker
  └── CONSUMER span: consume OrderCreated event
```

OpenTelemetry semantic conventions juga mencakup messaging systems spans, metrics, dan logs, walaupun status dan versi convention dapat berkembang. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/messaging/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Trace context propagation untuk message:

```text id="u0lc66"
inject traceparent into message headers
consumer extracts traceparent from headers
consumer span continues trace
```

Jangan simpan trace context sebagai payload bisnis jika message system menyediakan header metadata.

---

# 20. Trace-Log Correlation

Agar trace dan log nyambung, masukkan `traceId` dan `spanId` ke log.

Contoh log:

```json id="yrn7t4"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-api",
  "traceId": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736",
  "spanId": "00f067aa0ba902b7",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "errorCode": "PAYMENT_TIMEOUT",
  "message": "Payment authorization timed out"
}
```

OpenTelemetry logs specification dirancang agar logs bisa dikorelasikan dengan traces, metrics, dan resource metadata. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/semantic-conventions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dengan ini:

```text id="3lnmxd"
Dari dashboard trace:
  copy traceId → query logs

Dari log error:
  copy traceId → buka trace waterfall
```

---

# 21. MDC dengan OpenTelemetry Context

Jika memakai SLF4J/Logback, inject traceId/spanId ke MDC.

Contoh konseptual:

```java id="4m1j6f"
Span current = Span.current();
SpanContext spanContext = current.getSpanContext();

if (spanContext.isValid()) {
    MDC.put("traceId", spanContext.getTraceId());
    MDC.put("spanId", spanContext.getSpanId());
}
```

Pastikan:

```text id="qigqpq"
MDC dibersihkan setelah request selesai.
Async executor menyalin context dengan benar.
Jangan membuat traceId custom terpisah dari OpenTelemetry traceId.
```

---

# 22. Sampling Strategy

Tracing semua request bisa mahal.

Jenis sampling:

| Strategy | Arti | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Always on | Semua trace disimpan. | Dev/staging/low traffic. |
| Parent-based ratio | Ikuti keputusan parent, sample sebagian root traces. | Default umum. |
| Head sampling | Keputusan di awal request. | Murah, tapi bisa kehilangan error langka. |
| Tail sampling | Keputusan setelah melihat trace lengkap. | Bisa simpan error/slow trace, perlu Collector. |
| Rule-based | Sample endpoint/error/service tertentu. | Incident/debug atau critical flow. |

Contoh env:

```yaml id="47ylg4"
env:
  - name: OTEL_TRACES_SAMPLER
    value: "parentbased_traceidratio"

  - name: OTEL_TRACES_SAMPLER_ARG
    value: "0.10"
```

Makna:

```text id="xblmyh"
Sample sekitar 10% root traces,
tetap menghormati keputusan parent trace.
```

Untuk production:

```text id="uzkr46"
Gunakan sampling ratio untuk traffic normal.
Pertimbangkan tail sampling untuk menyimpan error/slow traces.
Jangan kehilangan semua traces untuk endpoint critical.
```

---

# 23. Tail Sampling di Collector

Tail sampling memungkinkan keputusan setelah trace selesai/nyaris lengkap.

Contoh policy konseptual:

```yaml id="49rvi0"
processors:
  tail_sampling:
    decision_wait: 10s
    num_traces: 50000
    policies:
      - name: errors
        type: status_code
        status_code:
          status_codes: [ERROR]

      - name: slow-traces
        type: latency
        latency:
          threshold_ms: 1000

      - name: baseline
        type: probabilistic
        probabilistic:
          sampling_percentage: 5
```

Manfaat:

```text id="n2tlkt"
simpan semua error traces
simpan slow traces
sample sebagian normal traces
biaya lebih terkendali
```

Trade-off:

```text id="ck2lqb"
Collector butuh memory/buffer
decision terlambat
arsitektur lebih kompleks
```

---

# 24. Span Naming Strategy

Nama span harus stabil dan bounded.

Baik:

```text id="7kzgom"
HTTP GET /orders/{id}
OrderMapper.findById
PaymentClient.authorize
Camunda task reserve-inventory
Kafka publish OrderCreated
```

Buruk:

```text id="srz4bl"
HTTP GET /orders/ORD-12345
SELECT * FROM orders WHERE id='ORD-12345'
Payment for customer Alice
```

Alasan:

```text id="znjvja"
Span names high-cardinality membuat UI/backend sulit dipakai.
Sensitive data bisa bocor.
Aggregation jadi buruk.
```

---

# 25. Attribute Design

Attribute bagus:

```text id="9bosnl"
service.name
service.version
deployment.environment
http.request.method
http.response.status_code
url.path
db.system.name
db.operation.name
db.collection.name
messaging.system
messaging.destination.name
error.type
```

Custom bounded attributes:

```text id="hsg8hz"
business.operation=createOrder
dependency=payment-api
camunda.topic=order-processing
worker.type=camunda-external-task
```

Hindari:

```text id="ofdlok"
password
token
Authorization
Cookie
full request body
email
phone
full address
orderId sebagai metric/high-cardinality label
raw SQL dengan literal
full URL dengan ID unik
```

---

# 26. Service Map

Tracing backend biasanya bisa membuat service map:

```text id="2obduh"
ingress-gateway
  → order-api
    → customer-api
    → payment-api
    → PostgreSQL
    → Camunda
      → order-worker
        → inventory-api
```

Manfaat:

```text id="glms3d"
melihat dependency aktual
menemukan shadow dependency
melihat error/latency per edge
membantu impact analysis sebelum deploy
```

Tapi service map hanya akurat jika:

```text id="ja4u5k"
context propagation tidak putus
service.name benar
outbound calls terinstrumentasi
async workflows membawa context
```

---

# 27. Debugging Trace yang Putus

Gejala:

```text id="8khccb"
order-api punya trace sendiri
customer-api punya trace lain
tidak ada parent-child relation
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="642n25"
traceparent tidak dikirim oleh client
Jersey Client tidak terinstrumentasi
gateway menghapus headers
custom HTTP client tidak inject context
async executor kehilangan Context
message broker tidak membawa headers
worker tidak extract context
sampling parent/child tidak konsisten
```

Cek:

```text id="3jumw2"
Apakah incoming request punya traceparent?
Apakah outbound request membawa traceparent?
Apakah downstream menerima traceparent?
Apakah service.name unik dan benar?
Apakah collector menerima traces dari kedua service?
Apakah sampling drop salah satu sisi?
```

---

# 28. Debugging Tidak Ada Trace

Golden path:

```text id="tpn0jj"
1. Apakah Java agent/SDK aktif?
2. Apakah OTEL_SERVICE_NAME diset?
3. Apakah exporter endpoint benar?
4. Apakah Pod bisa connect ke OTel Collector?
5. Apakah NetworkPolicy mengizinkan egress ke Collector?
6. Apakah Collector menerima OTLP?
7. Apakah Collector export ke backend berhasil?
8. Apakah sampling terlalu rendah?
9. Apakah service/filter/library memang terinstrumentasi?
```

Commands:

```bash id="pxhoqo"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --tail=200
```

```bash id="y58xo0"
kubectl logs deploy/otel-collector -n observability --tail=200
```

```bash id="sq3d5w"
kubectl get svc -n observability
```

```bash id="lqkikp"
kubectl run curl-test \
  -n order-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -v http://otel-collector.observability.svc.cluster.local:4318/
```

OTLP gRPC di 4317 tidak selalu bisa dites dengan curl biasa; OTLP HTTP 4318 lebih mudah untuk sanity check konektivitas HTTP.

---

# 29. NetworkPolicy untuk OTel Export

Jika default deny egress aktif, izinkan app mengirim telemetry ke collector.

```yaml id="twk4u4"
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-order-api-egress-to-otel-collector
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  policyTypes:
    - Egress
  egress:
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              name: observability
          podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: otel-collector
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 4317
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 4318
```

Jika traces hilang setelah NetworkPolicy diterapkan, cek rule ini.

---

# 30. Trace dan Performance Overhead

Tracing punya overhead.

Sumber overhead:

```text id="fqv2b7"
span creation
context propagation
exporting telemetry
attribute allocation
stacktrace/exception recording
high sampling rate
too many manual spans
large attributes
```

Praktik aman:

```text id="ad7nk8"
gunakan sampling
batasi attribute
hindari span terlalu granular untuk loop kecil
gunakan batch exporter/collector
jangan record request/response body
monitor overhead CPU/memory
```

Jangan membuat span untuk setiap item dalam list besar:

```text id="giq6yi"
for each of 10,000 orders:
  create span
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="r4ivhc"
span: process batch
attribute: item.count=10000
event: failed item count
```

---

# 31. Trace Events

Span event berguna untuk milestone internal tanpa membuat span baru.

Contoh:

```java id="8e9r9r"
Span.current().addEvent("order.validation.completed");
Span.current().addEvent("payment.authorization.requested");
```

Gunakan event untuk:

```text id="87jxsc"
milestone penting
state transition
retry attempt
fallback activated
```

Jangan gunakan event untuk:

```text id="c4xjky"
log semua item
request body
sensitive data
high-volume loop
```

---

# 32. Baggage: Hati-hati

OpenTelemetry/W3C ecosystem juga punya konsep baggage untuk membawa key-value context lintas service. Gunakan sangat hati-hati.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="806gkr"
tenant tier bounded
region
routing hint
low-cardinality context
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="dcvvsj"
user email
token
password
large values
PII
order details
anything sensitive
```

Baggage bisa dikirim lintas banyak service, sehingga risiko data leakage lebih besar.

---

# 33. Tracing untuk Rollout dan Version Debugging

Pastikan trace punya `service.version`.

Saat deploy versi baru:

```text id="3fkyh2"
service.version=1.8.3
service.version=1.8.4
```

Anda bisa membandingkan:

```text id="9hrapk"
p95 latency by service.version
error rate by service.version
slow traces only version 1.8.4
```

Ini membantu menentukan apakah rollback diperlukan.

---

# 34. Tracing untuk Kubernetes Runtime Issue

Trace bisa menunjukkan app latency, tetapi tidak selalu menunjukkan resource issue. Korelasikan dengan metrics/logs:

```text id="d3tnp1"
Trace:
  order-api span lambat tanpa child span lambat

Metrics:
  CPU throttling tinggi
  GC pause naik

Kesimpulan:
  resource/JVM issue, bukan downstream.
```

Atau:

```text id="qw0ztl"
Trace:
  Hikari acquire connection span lama

Metrics:
  DB pool pending tinggi

Kesimpulan:
  pool/DB saturation.
```

Atau:

```text id="wzwbq2"
Trace:
  payment-api call lambat

Metrics:
  payment-api p95 naik

Kesimpulan:
  dependency degradation.
```

---

# 35. Full Trace Design untuk Jersey + MyBatis + Camunda

Target trace untuk order creation:

```text id="xa5lmw"
Trace: POST /orders
│
├── SERVER span: order-api POST /orders
│   ├── INTERNAL span: validate command
│   ├── CLIENT span: PostgreSQL OrderMapper.insertOrder
│   ├── CLIENT span: customer-api GET /customers/{id}
│   ├── CLIENT span: payment-api POST /payments/authorize
│   └── CLIENT span: Camunda start order-process
│
└── Later async continuation:
    └── CONSUMER span: order-worker Camunda task reserve-inventory
        ├── CLIENT span: inventory-api POST /reservations
        ├── CLIENT span: PostgreSQL OrderMapper.updateStatus
        └── CLIENT span: Camunda complete task
```

Attributes:

```text id="ukfxh7"
service.name
service.version
deployment.environment
k8s.namespace.name
k8s.pod.name
http.request.method
http.response.status_code
business.operation=createOrder
dependency=payment-api
db.system.name=postgresql
camunda.topic=reserve-inventory
error.type
```

Logs:

```text id="nfbdzk"
include traceId/spanId/requestId
```

Metrics:

```text id="12utix"
HTTP latency/error
DB pool pending
downstream latency
Camunda task duration/failure
```

---

# 36. Common Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Cara Cek |
|---|---|---|
| Trace tidak muncul | Agent/SDK tidak aktif, exporter salah, collector down | app logs, collector logs |
| Trace putus antar service | `traceparent` tidak dipropagate | inspect headers/filter |
| Banyak service bernama sama | `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME` salah/default | resource attributes |
| Span terlalu banyak | Manual instrumentation terlalu granular | span count per trace |
| Backend mahal/lambat | Sampling terlalu tinggi, attribute high-cardinality | sampling/cardinality |
| DB tidak terlihat | JDBC instrumentation tidak aktif/manual belum ada | supported library/config |
| Camunda async putus | trace context tidak disimpan/extract | worker metadata |
| Logs tidak bisa dikorelasikan | traceId/spanId tidak masuk MDC | log format/filter |
| Error trace hilang | head sampling drop error request | tail sampling/error sampling |
| Sensitive data di trace | raw SQL/body/header jadi attribute | audit/redaction |

---

# 37. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Hanya Instrument Inbound HTTP

```text id="cgnc6n"
Trace:
  order-api POST /orders 3200ms
```

Tapi tidak ada child span.

Masalah:

```text id="iaxsfw"
Tidak tahu 3200ms habis di DB, downstream, atau CPU.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="wabgvj"
instrument inbound HTTP
instrument outbound HTTP
instrument JDBC/MyBatis
instrument async worker
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Trace Context Tidak Dipropagasi

Buruk:

```text id="h2ezzf"
order-api trace ID A
customer-api trace ID B
payment-api trace ID C
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="xf4d5t"
semua service dalam satu trace ID yang sama
dengan parent-child span relation
```

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Span Name High Cardinality

Buruk:

```text id="m4kd9e"
GET /orders/ORD-123
GET /orders/ORD-124
GET /orders/ORD-125
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="46zesu"
GET /orders/{id}
```

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Sensitive Data di Attributes

Buruk:

```text id="40mqpa"
user.email=alice@example.com
authorization=Bearer ...
sql.statement=INSERT ... password ...
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="29aglb"
user.hash=...
error.type=...
db.operation.name=INSERT
```

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Sampling 100% Tanpa Governance

Bisa diterima di dev/staging, tetapi di production high traffic:

```text id="d9c6md"
biaya naik
backend tracing overload
network overhead naik
storage retention membengkak
```

Gunakan sampling dan prioritaskan error/slow traces.

---

# 38. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Service name | Apakah `service.name` unik dan benar? |
| Version | Apakah `service.version` ada untuk rollout debugging? |
| Context propagation | Apakah `traceparent` diteruskan inbound/outbound? |
| Jersey server | Apakah inbound HTTP span tersedia? |
| Jersey client | Apakah outbound HTTP span tersedia? |
| MyBatis/JDBC | Apakah DB operation terlihat sebagai span? |
| Camunda worker | Apakah async task trace tersambung? |
| Messaging | Apakah message headers membawa trace context? |
| Logs | Apakah `traceId`/`spanId` masuk JSON log? |
| Metrics | Apakah trace bisa dikorelasikan dengan latency/error metrics? |
| Sampling | Apakah sampling sesuai traffic/biaya/SLO? |
| Collector | Apakah telemetry lewat collector dengan batching/enrichment? |
| Kubernetes metadata | Apakah namespace/pod/deployment/node masuk resource attributes? |
| Attribute safety | Apakah tidak ada PII/secret/high-cardinality attributes? |
| Debuggability | Apakah runbook “trace putus/tidak muncul” tersedia? |

---

# 39. Ringkasan Seri 17

```text id="2qqdw2"
Trace:
  perjalanan end-to-end satu request/operation.

Span:
  unit kerja dalam trace.

traceparent:
  header standar W3C untuk membawa trace identity.

Context propagation:
  meneruskan trace context antar service/process/thread/message.

OpenTelemetry:
  standard vendor-neutral untuk traces, metrics, logs,
  dengan Java agent, SDK, dan Collector.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey di Kubernetes:

```text id="z3chnd"
Gunakan OpenTelemetry Java agent untuk baseline.
Tambahkan manual span untuk business operation penting.
Propagate traceparent lewat Jersey Client/OpenFeign/OpenAPI client.
Instrument JDBC/MyBatis agar DB latency terlihat.
Propagate context ke Camunda/Kafka/RabbitMQ async worker.
Tambahkan traceId/spanId ke JSON logs.
Tambahkan k8s metadata melalui env/collector/k8sattributes processor.
Gunakan sampling untuk mengontrol biaya.
Jangan masukkan PII/secret/raw body ke trace attributes.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="vn4l1j"
Distributed tracing yang baik bukan hanya “ada trace”.
Tracing yang baik harus menjawab:
request ini melewati service apa saja,
waktu habis di mana,
error muncul di dependency mana,
versi/Pod mana yang terlibat,
dan log mana yang menjelaskan detailnya.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:19:48 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 18/24  
## Debugging dengan `kubectl`: logs, describe, exec, events, rollout, port-forward, debug ephemeral container, dan runbook sistematis untuk Java/Jersey Microservice

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **kalau Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice bermasalah di Kubernetes, urutan debugging yang benar itu apa?**

Di Kubernetes, `kubectl` adalah command-line tool untuk berkomunikasi dengan control plane melalui Kubernetes API. Ia dipakai untuk deploy, inspect, manage resource, dan melihat log. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Debugging

Jangan mulai debugging dari “masuk Pod lalu coba-coba”. Mulai dari layer luar ke dalam.

```text id="lx1n0g"
Symptom
│
├── User / caller error
│   ├── 404
│   ├── 502
│   ├── 503
│   ├── 504
│   ├── timeout
│   └── wrong response
│
├── Kubernetes object state
│   ├── Deployment
│   ├── ReplicaSet
│   ├── Pod
│   ├── Service
│   ├── EndpointSlice
│   ├── Ingress/Gateway
│   └── HPA
│
├── Runtime state
│   ├── Pod phase
│   ├── container status
│   ├── restart count
│   ├── readiness/liveness
│   ├── events
│   └── resource usage
│
├── Application evidence
│   ├── logs
│   ├── previous logs
│   ├── config/env
│   ├── health endpoint
│   ├── metrics
│   └── traces
│
└── Dependency evidence
    ├── PostgreSQL / MyBatis
    ├── downstream HTTP
    ├── Camunda
    ├── Kafka/RabbitMQ/Redis
    └── network policy / DNS
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="lt0xzl"
Debugging yang baik adalah isolasi layer.
Jangan langsung menyimpulkan “app bug” atau “Kubernetes error”
sebelum membuktikan layer mana yang gagal.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Debugging Flow

```text id="w83r3c"
Incident: order-api returns 503
│
├── 1. Check rollout/deployment state
│   └── kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api
│
├── 2. Check Pods
│   └── kubectl get pods
│
├── 3. Check Pod details/events
│   └── kubectl describe pod
│
├── 4. Check logs/current and previous
│   ├── kubectl logs
│   └── kubectl logs --previous
│
├── 5. Check Service and endpoints
│   ├── kubectl get svc
│   └── kubectl get endpointslice
│
├── 6. Test from inside cluster
│   ├── nslookup
│   └── curl health endpoint
│
├── 7. Inspect config/runtime if allowed
│   └── kubectl exec / kubectl debug
│
└── 8. Correlate with metrics/traces
    ├── JVM
    ├── DB pool
    ├── HTTP latency
    └── dependency failure
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Jersey/JAX-RS | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`kubectl get`** | Melihat object state ringkas. | Cek Pod Ready, restart, Service, HPA. | Tidak tahu kondisi aktual workload. |
| 2 | **`kubectl describe`** | Melihat detail object dan events terkait. Kubernetes docs menyarankan `kubectl describe pod` untuk mengambil detail Pod saat debugging Pod running/crashing. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/debug-running-pod/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Cek probe failure, image pull, OOM, scheduling issue. | Hanya lihat logs padahal root cause ada di events. |
| 3 | **`kubectl logs`** | Melihat log container. `kubectl logs` mencetak log container di Pod/resource, dan container name opsional jika Pod hanya punya satu container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_logs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug startup error, exception, config, DB failure. | CrashLoopBackOff sulit dianalisis. |
| 4 | **`--previous` logs** | Melihat log container sebelumnya setelah restart. | Sangat penting untuk OOM/crash/liveness restart. | Error asli hilang dari log container baru. |
| 5 | **`kubectl exec`** | Shell/command ke container berjalan. Kubernetes menyediakan panduan `kubectl exec` untuk mendapatkan shell di container running. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/get-shell-running-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Cek env, file config, DNS, cert, mounted Secret. | Dipakai sembarangan di prod, security risk. |
| 6 | **`kubectl debug`** | Ephemeral/debug container. `kubectl debug` bisa menambahkan ephemeral container ke Pod running untuk debugging tanpa restart Pod. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_debug/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Berguna jika image Java minimal tidak punya shell/curl. | Tidak tersedia karena RBAC/PodSecurity/policy. |
| 7 | **Ephemeral Containers** | Container sementara untuk troubleshooting. Kubernetes menyatakan ephemeral container berguna saat `kubectl exec` tidak cukup karena container crash atau image tidak punya debug utilities. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/ephemeral-containers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug image distroless/minimal. | Mengubah image production hanya demi debug tools. |
| 8 | **Events** | Melihat event scheduling, pulling image, probe, kill. | Banyak root cause K8s ada di events. | Salah fokus ke code. |
| 9 | **`rollout` commands** | Status/history/undo/restart rollout. Kubectl reference mencakup `rollout history`, `status`, `restart`, dan `undo`. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug deploy gagal atau rollback. | Rollout stuck tidak terdeteksi. |
| 10 | **`port-forward`** | Forward port lokal ke Pod/Service. `kubectl port-forward` meneruskan local port ke Pod, dan bisa memakai resource type/name seperti Deployment atau Service. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_port-forward/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Test endpoint internal tanpa expose public. | Disalahgunakan sebagai akses production permanen. |
| 11 | **Service debugging** | Service → EndpointSlice → Pod readiness → port. Kubernetes debug service guide menyarankan cek Pod health/listening port jika response tidak sesuai. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/debug-service/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Debug 502/503/connection refused. | Mengira DNS OK berarti backend sehat. |
| 12 | **RBAC-aware debugging** | Paham command bisa ditolak permission. | Senior engineer sering debug terbatas oleh role. | Bingung karena command tidak bisa jalan. |
| 13 | **Runbook discipline** | Urutan command dan interpretasi. | Incident lebih cepat selesai. | Trial-error, noise, salah eskalasi. |

---

# 4. Golden Rule: Jangan Debug Tanpa Namespace dan Label

Selalu eksplisit namespace.

Buruk:

```bash id="6oqw63"
kubectl get pods
```

Lebih baik:

```bash id="j1kz9s"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod
```

Gunakan label:

```bash id="j6tvro"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

Tampilkan label:

```bash id="msobee"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod --show-labels
```

Kenapa?

```text id="cytd3e"
Nama service bisa sama di namespace berbeda.
Pod hasil rollout punya suffix berbeda.
Label adalah cara stabil untuk memilih workload.
```

---

# 5. Command Baseline: Melihat Kondisi Workload

## Deployment

```bash id="i9g348"
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod
```

Detail YAML:

```bash id="oj75fy"
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod -o yaml
```

Cek replica:

```text id="m23pyl"
READY
UP-TO-DATE
AVAILABLE
AGE
```

Jika `AVAILABLE` lebih kecil dari expected replica, cek Pod dan events.

## Pods

```bash id="nkk3b9"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

Output yang perlu dilihat:

```text id="2qfy05"
READY
STATUS
RESTARTS
AGE
```

Contoh symptom:

```text id="wyjx0x"
READY 0/1
STATUS Running
RESTARTS 0
```

Artinya container running, tapi belum ready. Fokus ke readiness probe, app startup, dependency readiness, atau config.

```text id="7gdtto"
STATUS CrashLoopBackOff
RESTARTS 12
```

Fokus ke `kubectl logs --previous` dan `describe pod`.

---

# 6. `kubectl describe pod`: Sumber Kebenaran Runtime Detail

Command:

```bash id="kd5dfk"
kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n order-prod
```

Bagian penting:

```text id="xrsnfx"
Name
Namespace
Node
Labels
Annotations
Status
IP
Controlled By
Containers
State
Last State
Ready
Restart Count
Limits/Requests
Environment
Mounts
Conditions
Events
```

Cari:

```text id="j5xtwl"
ImagePullBackOff
ErrImagePull
CreateContainerConfigError
CrashLoopBackOff
OOMKilled
Liveness probe failed
Readiness probe failed
Startup probe failed
Back-off restarting failed container
FailedScheduling
Insufficient cpu
Insufficient memory
MountVolume.SetUp failed
Secret not found
ConfigMap not found
```

Kubernetes debugging docs memang menempatkan `kubectl describe pod` sebagai langkah awal untuk memperoleh detail Pod dan events saat Pod running/crashing. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/debug-running-pod/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Events: Jangan Diabaikan

Events sering menjelaskan hal yang tidak ada di application log.

```bash id="6fbh17"
kubectl get events -n order-prod --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

Filter warning:

```bash id="a5vvee"
kubectl get events -n order-prod \
  --field-selector type=Warning \
  --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

Events berguna untuk:

```text id="ksvagd"
scheduling failure
image pull failure
probe failure
container killed
mount failure
node pressure
quota exceeded
HPA scaling
rollout issue
```

Contoh interpretasi:

```text id="0lvj9x"
Warning FailedScheduling Insufficient cpu
```

Root cause bukan Java code; Pod belum bisa dijadwalkan karena node capacity/resource request.

```text id="l8jt9y"
Warning Unhealthy Readiness probe failed
```

App running, tapi readiness endpoint gagal. Cek `/health/ready`, config, DB pool, dependency policy.

---

# 8. `kubectl logs`: Current Logs

Deployment logs:

```bash id="x064j3"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Follow:

```bash id="7we92j"
kubectl logs -f deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Tail:

```bash id="q2k56c"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --tail=200
```

Since:

```bash id="rjoeq6"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --since=15m
```

Specific Pod:

```bash id="ln0967"
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod
```

Specific container:

```bash id="q71x81"
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod -c order-api
```

Kubernetes reference menjelaskan `kubectl logs` dapat mencetak log container dalam Pod atau resource tertentu, dan container name opsional jika hanya ada satu container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_logs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. `kubectl logs --previous`: Untuk Crash dan Restart

Jika Pod restart, log container saat ini mungkin tidak berisi error asli.

Gunakan:

```bash id="c9nzja"
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod --previous
```

Dengan container:

```bash id="01krio"
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod -c order-api --previous
```

Gunakan saat:

```text id="v3r1mp"
CrashLoopBackOff
OOMKilled
liveness probe restart
app crash saat startup
SIGTERM/SIGKILL unclear
container exit cepat
```

Cari di previous logs:

```text id="9rvfyj"
ExceptionInInitializerError
ClassNotFoundException
NoClassDefFoundError
OutOfMemoryError
BindException
Missing env var
DB authentication failed
Config parse error
Liquibase/Flyway migration failure
Permission denied due to readOnlyRootFilesystem
```

---

# 10. Rollout Debugging

Status rollout:

```bash id="jxg540"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

History:

```bash id="eh4gln"
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Detail revision:

```bash id="xouz1y"
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod --revision=3
```

Restart rollout:

```bash id="nl7u64"
kubectl rollout restart deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Undo rollback:

```bash id="1nuamk"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Kubectl rollout reference mencakup pengelolaan rollout resource seperti melihat history, status, restart, dan undo. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan rollout debug jika:

```text id="u2j31o"
deployment stuck
replica baru tidak ready
versi baru error
ingin rollback
ingin restart setelah ConfigMap/Secret update
```

---

# 11. Debugging Rollout Stuck

Symptom:

```text id="ysrlx5"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
Waiting for deployment "order-api" rollout to finish...
```

Urutan:

```bash id="z2ntvj"
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod
```

```bash id="t0jpmu"
kubectl get rs -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

```bash id="v13ym7"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

```bash id="k8u5w0"
kubectl describe pod <new-pod-name> -n order-prod
```

```bash id="aj0at3"
kubectl logs <new-pod-name> -n order-prod --previous
```

Kemungkinan root cause:

```text id="zm3v4g"
image tag salah
image pull secret salah
config missing
secret missing
readiness gagal
startup probe terlalu pendek
DB migration gagal
app listen di port salah
resource request terlalu tinggi
PodSecurity admission issue
```

---

# 12. Service Debugging: DNS → Service → EndpointSlice → Pod

Jika caller gagal call `order-api`:

```bash id="gcvdqn"
kubectl get svc order-api -n order-prod
```

```bash id="4v07zx"
kubectl describe svc order-api -n order-prod
```

EndpointSlice:

```bash id="8xnze8"
kubectl get endpointslice -n order-prod \
  -l kubernetes.io/service-name=order-api
```

Describe EndpointSlice:

```bash id="m83m3p"
kubectl describe endpointslice -n order-prod \
  -l kubernetes.io/service-name=order-api
```

Pod labels:

```bash id="81zemx"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod --show-labels
```

Port check:

```bash id="8dz7s0"
kubectl get svc order-api -n order-prod -o yaml
```

Periksa:

```text id="z8upj1"
Service selector match Pod label?
Service port benar?
targetPort benar?
Pod ready?
EndpointSlice punya address?
```

Kubernetes debug service guide menekankan bahwa jika response tidak sesuai, Pod mungkin tidak sehat atau tidak listen pada port yang Anda pikirkan; `kubectl logs` dan `kubectl exec` bisa membantu investigasi lebih lanjut. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/debug-service/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. DNS Debugging dari Dalam Cluster

Buat temporary Pod untuk DNS test:

```bash id="tmn2we"
kubectl run dns-test \
  -n order-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=registry.k8s.io/e2e-test-images/agnhost:2.39 \
  --restart=Never \
  -- nslookup order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local
```

Test cross-namespace:

```bash id="8kbnyx"
kubectl run dns-test \
  -n crm-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=registry.k8s.io/e2e-test-images/agnhost:2.39 \
  --restart=Never \
  -- nslookup order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local
```

Jika DNS gagal:

```text id="fsz0wl"
Service name salah
namespace salah
CoreDNS issue
NetworkPolicy default deny egress tanpa allow DNS
cluster DNS config issue
```

Cek CoreDNS:

```bash id="a3g5rb"
kubectl get pods -n kube-system -l k8s-app=kube-dns
```

---

# 14. HTTP Connectivity Test dari Dalam Cluster

Gunakan curl image:

```bash id="1xi4jm"
kubectl run curl-test \
  -n crm-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -i --connect-timeout 2 \
     http://order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local/health/ready
```

Verbose:

```bash id="x8h92d"
kubectl run curl-test \
  -n crm-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -v --connect-timeout 2 \
     http://order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local/orders/123
```

Interpretasi:

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| `Could not resolve host` | DNS/name/namespace problem. |
| `Connection refused` | targetPort salah atau app tidak listen. |
| Timeout connect | NetworkPolicy/CNI/path drop. |
| HTTP 503 | Pod not ready/no endpoint/upstream unavailable. |
| HTTP 404 | Path routing/Jersey `@Path` mismatch. |
| HTTP 500 | App exception/dependency failure. |

---

# 15. `kubectl exec`: Masuk ke Container Running

Command:

```bash id="ng2rtf"
kubectl exec -it <pod-name> -n order-prod -- sh
```

Jika pakai bash:

```bash id="zzjswb"
kubectl exec -it <pod-name> -n order-prod -- bash
```

Specific container:

```bash id="fbbosm"
kubectl exec -it <pod-name> -n order-prod -c order-api -- sh
```

Kubernetes docs menyediakan panduan `kubectl exec` untuk mendapatkan shell ke container yang sedang running. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/debug/debug-application/get-shell-running-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan untuk cek:

```bash id="x2r1b1"
env | sort
```

```bash id="d6m9nl"
ls -la /app/config
```

```bash id="18iwph"
cat /app/config/application.yaml
```

```bash id="dicsrb"
id
```

```bash id="0cdznv"
pwd
```

```bash id="gr20fh"
ls -la /tmp
```

Catatan security:

```text id="jzpit8"
Jangan print env jika ada secret di terminal/log sharing.
Jangan cat Secret file sembarangan.
Jangan pakai exec sebagai operasi normal.
Exec di production harus sesuai RBAC/audit policy.
```

---

# 16. Debug Image Minimal: Ketika `sh`, `curl`, `ps` Tidak Ada

Image production yang baik sering minimal:

```text id="bezjoc"
tidak ada bash
tidak ada curl
tidak ada netstat
tidak ada package manager
read-only filesystem
non-root user
```

Ini bagus untuk security, tapi menyulitkan debugging.

Solusi:

```text id="9n7bjp"
jangan menambahkan tool debug ke image production hanya karena nyaman.
gunakan ephemeral debug container jika platform mengizinkan.
```

Ephemeral containers memang dirancang untuk troubleshooting interaktif saat `kubectl exec` tidak cukup, misalnya container crash atau image tidak menyertakan debugging utilities. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/ephemeral-containers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. `kubectl debug` dengan Ephemeral Container

Tambahkan debug container ke Pod running:

```bash id="53yaqw"
kubectl debug -it <pod-name> \
  -n order-prod \
  --image=busybox:1.36 \
  --target=order-api \
  -- sh
```

Atau pakai image berisi network tools:

```bash id="so8h95"
kubectl debug -it <pod-name> \
  -n order-prod \
  --image=nicolaka/netshoot:latest \
  --target=order-api \
  -- bash
```

`kubectl debug` reference menyatakan command ini dapat menambahkan ephemeral container ke Pod running untuk menambahkan utility debugging tanpa restart Pod. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_debug/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="6ceufx"
DNS test dari network namespace Pod
curl internal dependency
inspect process/network jika namespace sharing tersedia
debug image distroless/minimal
```

Caveat:

```text id="vx5yc7"
Butuh RBAC permission.
Bisa dibatasi Pod Security Standards.
Tidak semua cluster mengizinkan image debug bebas.
Jangan gunakan image tidak terpercaya di production.
```

---

# 18. Debug Copy Pod

Kadang lebih aman membuat copy Pod dengan image/command debug.

Contoh konseptual:

```bash id="zukglp"
kubectl debug deploy/order-api \
  -n order-prod \
  --copy-to=order-api-debug \
  --container=order-api \
  --image=registry.example.com/order-api:1.8.3 \
  -- sh
```

Gunakan jika:

```text id="n0nvgl"
Pod asli production tidak boleh diganggu.
Perlu eksperimen command/env.
Perlu debug startup.
```

Jangan lupa hapus:

```bash id="juc0rb"
kubectl delete pod order-api-debug -n order-prod
```

---

# 19. `kubectl port-forward`: Test Endpoint Internal dari Lokal

Port-forward ke Service:

```bash id="bshpb8"
kubectl port-forward svc/order-api 8080:80 -n order-prod
```

Lalu lokal:

```bash id="5r7gj2"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health/ready
```

Port-forward ke Pod:

```bash id="1oc4oq"
kubectl port-forward pod/<pod-name> 8080:8080 -n order-prod
```

Kubernetes reference menjelaskan `kubectl port-forward` meneruskan satu atau lebih local port ke Pod, dan bisa memakai resource type/name seperti Deployment atau Service untuk memilih Pod; forwarding session berakhir jika Pod terpilih berhenti. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_port-forward/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="owt08y"
test endpoint internal tanpa Ingress
cek /metrics
cek /health
debug API internal sementara
```

Jangan gunakan untuk:

```text id="ot0srf"
akses production permanen
menghindari authorization
mengambil data sensitif tanpa approval
```

```text id="p0bqoj"
CPU limit terlalu ketat
GC ikut throttled
thread pool saturated
DB/downstream menunggu
```

`kubectl` sendiri tidak cukup untuk CPU throttling detail; perlu metrics pipeline seperti Prometheus/cAdvisor/platform dashboard.

# 25. Debugging NetworkPolicy

Jika DNS resolve tapi HTTP timeout:

```bash id="iuhxbu"
kubectl get networkpolicy -A
```

Policy di caller namespace:

```bash id="ngzf1p"
kubectl get networkpolicy -n crm-prod -o yaml
```

Policy di callee namespace:

```bash id="qyv65t"
kubectl get networkpolicy -n order-prod -o yaml
```

Cek labels:

```bash id="0ijnes"
kubectl get ns --show-labels
kubectl get pods -n crm-prod --show-labels
kubectl get pods -n order-prod --show-labels
```

Test allowed caller:

```bash id="39s0jp"
kubectl run curl-allowed \
  -n crm-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -i --connect-timeout 2 \
     http://order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local/health/ready
```

Test denied caller:

```bash id="d2o4oi"
kubectl run curl-denied \
  -n random-prod \
  --rm -it \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -i --connect-timeout 2 \
     http://order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local/health/ready
```

NetworkPolicy denial biasanya terlihat sebagai timeout, bukan HTTP 403.

Gejala:

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|

| Worker restart | OOM/liveness/config/dependency fatal. |
| Backlog naik | Worker capacity kurang, downstream lambat, task failure. |

# 36. Jangan Melakukan Ini Saat Debug Production

Anti-pattern debugging:

```text id="q6kz0r"
kubectl delete pod tanpa tahu Deployment/HPA behavior
kubectl edit langsung production tanpa PR/audit
decode secret dan paste ke chat
printenv lengkap berisi secret
exec ke Pod lalu ubah file manual
port-forward production sebagai akses permanen
run debug image tidak terpercaya
restart semua Pod tanpa memahami root cause
rollback tanpa cek DB migration compatibility
menganggap fix karena Pod restart
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="nbakkv"
ambil evidence dulu
catat command dan output penting
gunakan read-only command sebisa mungkin
pakai rollout restart/undo dengan change control
redact secret
eskalasi ke platform/DB/security dengan bukti layer spesifik
```

# 37. Runbook: Pod CrashLoopBackOff

```text id="713vs7"
1. kubectl get pods -n order-prod
2. kubectl describe pod <pod> -n order-prod
3. kubectl logs <pod> -n order-prod --previous
4. cek Reason/Exit Code/Events
5. cek ConfigMap/Secret/env
6. cek image/command/entrypoint
7. cek resource limit/OOMKilled
8. cek SecurityContext permission
9. cek startupProbe/livenessProbe
10. rollback/redeploy jika versi baru penyebab
```

Command bundle:

```bash id="v11ebf"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod
kubectl describe pod <pod> -n order-prod
kubectl logs <pod> -n order-prod --previous --tail=200
kubectl get events -n order-prod --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

# 41. Runbook: “It Works Locally but Not in Kubernetes”

Checklist:

```text id="ihwcit"
1. Config beda?
2. Env var missing?
3. Secret/ConfigMap key missing?
4. App listen di 127.0.0.1 bukan 0.0.0.0?
5. Port container/Service targetPort salah?
6. Read-only filesystem?
7. Non-root permission?
8. DNS base URL beda?
9. NetworkPolicy deny?
10. DB credential/host beda?
11. JVM memory limit beda?
12. Startup lebih lambat karena CPU request kecil?
```

Commands:

```bash id="emzrbm"
kubectl describe pod <pod> -n order-prod
kubectl logs <pod> -n order-prod --previous
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod -o yaml
kubectl get svc order-api -n order-prod -o yaml
kubectl get endpointslice -n order-prod \
  -l kubernetes.io/service-name=order-api
```

---

# 42. Common Failure Modes

| Gejala | Command Pertama | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|---|
| CrashLoopBackOff | `logs --previous`, `describe pod` | App crash, config, OOM, liveness. |
| ImagePullBackOff | `describe pod` | Image/tag/registry/secret. |
| Pending | `describe pod` | CPU/memory, taint, affinity, quota, PVC. |
| Ready 0/1 | `describe pod`, logs | Readiness/startup/dependency. |
| 503 | EndpointSlice, readiness | No ready endpoints. |
| 502 | Service targetPort, app listen | Connection refused/bad upstream. |
| 504 | Logs/traces/metrics | App/dependency timeout. |
| UnknownHost | DNS test | Service name/namespace/DNS/egress DNS. |
| Timeout internal | curl test, NetworkPolicy | NetworkPolicy/drop/downstream slow. |
| OOMKilled | describe pod | Memory limit/JVM/native/leak. |
| Rollout stuck | rollout status, new Pod describe | New version not ready. |
| HPA not scaling | describe hpa | Metrics unavailable/request/target. |

---

# 43. Senior Engineer Debugging Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Scope | Namespace dan workload sudah benar? |
| Timeline | Kapan mulai terjadi, setelah rollout/config change? |
| Deployment | Rollout status/history sehat? |
| Pods | Status, readiness, restart count? |
| Events | Ada warning terbaru? |
| Logs | Current dan previous logs sudah dicek? |
| Service | Selector, port, targetPort benar? |
| EndpointSlice | Ada ready backend endpoint? |
| Config | ConfigMap/Secret/env sesuai? |
| Probes | Startup/liveness/readiness path/port/timing benar? |
| Resources | CPU/memory request-limit, OOM, throttling? |
| Network | DNS, NetworkPolicy, cross-namespace FQDN? |
| Security | RBAC/SecurityContext/PodSecurity issue? |
| Dependencies | DB/downstream/Camunda/Kafka/Redis sehat? |
| Evidence | Ada requestId/traceId untuk korelasi logs/traces? |
| Action | Restart/rollback/redeploy benar-benar memperbaiki root cause? |

---

# 44. Ringkasan Seri 18

```text id="urcrch"
kubectl get:
  lihat state ringkas

kubectl describe:
  lihat detail object dan events

kubectl logs:
  lihat log container saat ini

kubectl logs --previous:
  lihat log container sebelum restart

kubectl exec:
  jalankan command di container running

kubectl debug:
  tambah ephemeral/debug container untuk troubleshooting

kubectl port-forward:
  akses sementara ke Pod/Service dari lokal

kubectl rollout:
  status/history/restart/undo deployment rollout

kubectl get events:
  lihat event warning/lifecycle cluster
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice:

```text id="4by3ar"
Debug harus berurutan:
Deployment → Pod → Events → Logs → Service → EndpointSlice → DNS → NetworkPolicy → App config → JVM/resource → DB/downstream/Camunda.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="6zrtfi"
Debugging production bukan mencari command sakti.
Debugging production adalah mengumpulkan evidence,
mengisolasi layer yang gagal,
menghindari perubahan manual yang tidak audit-able,
dan memilih tindakan paling kecil yang memperbaiki root cause.
```
