## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:22:34 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 19/24  
## Deployment Strategy: Rolling Update, Rollback, Blue-Green, Canary, Release Safety, Compatibility Old/New Version, dan Zero-Downtime Deployment untuk Java/Jersey + MyBatis/PostgreSQL

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana merilis Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice di Kubernetes dengan aman, bisa rollback, tidak memutus traffic, dan tidak merusak PostgreSQL/MyBatis/Camunda workflow?**

Kubernetes **Deployment** menyediakan mekanisme deklaratif untuk mengelola Pod dan ReplicaSet, termasuk rollout versi baru dan rollback ke revisi sebelumnya. Strategi bawaan Deployment yang umum adalah **RollingUpdate** dan **Recreate**; RollingUpdate mengontrol update bertahap memakai parameter seperti `maxSurge` dan `maxUnavailable`. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Deployment strategy bukan hanya “ganti image”.

```text id="b08f6f"
Safe Release
│
├── Kubernetes rollout mechanics
│   ├── Deployment
│   ├── ReplicaSet
│   ├── rolling update
│   ├── readiness probe
│   ├── rollback
│   └── rollout history
│
├── Application compatibility
│   ├── old version and new version run together
│   ├── API backward compatibility
│   ├── config compatibility
│   ├── database schema compatibility
│   └── workflow/message compatibility
│
├── Runtime safety
│   ├── graceful shutdown
│   ├── startup probe
│   ├── readiness probe
│   ├── resource request/limit
│   └── enough capacity during rollout
│
├── Data safety
│   ├── expand/contract migration
│   ├── backward-compatible SQL
│   ├── idempotent writes
│   └── rollback-aware schema changes
│
└── Observability and control
    ├── logs
    ├── metrics
    ├── traces
    ├── alerts
    ├── rollout status
    └── rollback procedure
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="s6hj7n"
Zero-downtime deployment bukan hanya fitur Kubernetes.
Zero-downtime deployment adalah hasil kerja sama antara:
Deployment strategy, readiness, graceful shutdown, API compatibility,
database migration, observability, dan rollback discipline.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Release Flow

```text id="qa1a7w"
GitHub Commit / Tag
  │
  ▼
CI Pipeline
  ├── test
  ├── build Maven artifact
  ├── build container image
  ├── scan image
  └── push image tag/digest
  │
  ▼
CD Pipeline / GitOps
  ├── update manifest/Helm/Kustomize
  ├── apply Deployment
  └── watch rollout
  │
  ▼
Kubernetes Deployment
  ├── create new ReplicaSet
  ├── start new Pods
  ├── wait startup/readiness
  ├── shift traffic gradually via Service endpoints
  ├── terminate old Pods gracefully
  └── mark rollout complete
  │
  ▼
Observability
  ├── error rate
  ├── latency
  ├── readiness
  ├── restarts
  ├── DB pool
  ├── downstream errors
  └── business metrics
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **RollingUpdate** | Update Pod bertahap tanpa menghentikan semua replica lama sekaligus. | Default terbaik untuk REST API stateless. | Error spike jika readiness/graceful shutdown buruk. |
| 2 | **Recreate** | Matikan Pod lama dulu, lalu buat Pod baru. | Jarang cocok untuk public API. | Downtime jelas saat deploy. |
| 3 | **maxSurge** | Jumlah Pod ekstra selama rollout. | Menjaga kapasitas saat Pod baru dibuat. | Terlalu tinggi bisa menekan node/DB. |
| 4 | **maxUnavailable** | Jumlah Pod yang boleh tidak available saat rollout. | Untuk API critical sering `0`. | Kapasitas turun saat deploy. |
| 5 | **Readiness Gate** | Pod baru hanya dapat traffic saat ready. | Jersey app harus expose `/health/ready`. | Traffic masuk ke app belum siap. |
| 6 | **Graceful Shutdown** | Pod lama drain request sebelum mati. | Mencegah 502/request putus. | In-flight request gagal saat rollout. |
| 7 | **Rollback** | Kembali ke revisi sebelumnya. `kubectl rollout undo` digunakan untuk rollback ke rollout sebelumnya atau revisi tertentu. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_rollout/kubectl_rollout_undo/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Recovery cepat jika versi baru rusak. | Rollback tidak aman jika DB migration destructive. |
| 8 | **Rollout History** | Melihat revisi rollout dan config sebelumnya. `kubectl rollout history` digunakan untuk melihat revision dan konfigurasi rollout sebelumnya. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_rollout/kubectl_rollout_history/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Audit deploy dan rollback target. | Tidak tahu versi mana yang sedang/akan di-rollback. |
| 9 | **Blue-Green** | Dua environment versi lama/baru, traffic switch cepat. | Cocok untuk release besar. | Butuh kapasitas ganda dan DB compatibility. |
| 10 | **Canary** | Rilis sebagian kecil traffic dulu. | Mengurangi blast radius. | Service native tidak punya weighted traffic; butuh Ingress/Gateway/mesh/platform. |
| 11 | **DB Migration Safety** | Expand/contract, backward-compatible schema. | MyBatis/PostgreSQL sangat critical. | Rollback app gagal karena schema sudah berubah. |
| 12 | **API Compatibility** | Consumer lama tetap bekerja saat versi baru deploy. | Contract-first OpenAPI/Jersey. | Downstream/caller error saat rollout campuran. |
| 13 | **Worker Compatibility** | Camunda worker lama/baru bisa berjalan bersamaan. | Task handler harus idempotent dan compatible. | Duplicate side effect atau workflow stuck. |
| 14 | **Observability Gate** | Release dipantau via SLI/error/latency/business metrics. | Deteksi cepat sebelum full rollout. | Canary buruk lolos ke full prod. |
| 15 | **PDB Awareness** | PodDisruptionBudget membatasi concurrent voluntary disruptions untuk availability. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/run-application/configure-pdb/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Menjaga service tetap available saat node maintenance. | Node drain/deploy/maintenance bisa mengurangi kapasitas terlalu banyak. |

---

# 4. Rolling Update

Rolling update adalah strategi paling umum untuk REST API stateless.

```text id="zc65rp"
Old ReplicaSet: order-api v1
  ├── Pod v1
  ├── Pod v1
  └── Pod v1

Rollout starts

New ReplicaSet: order-api v2
  ├── Pod v2 starting
  ├── Pod v2 ready
  └── traffic gradually shifts

Old ReplicaSet scaled down
```

Deployment rolling update memakai parameter `maxSurge` dan `maxUnavailable`; dokumentasi Kubernetes menjelaskan contoh bagaimana jumlah Pod selama update dikontrol oleh kedua parameter tersebut. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Manifest:

```yaml id="a1xebj"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  replicas: 3

  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0

  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api

  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="fbzjft"
replicas: 3
  target steady state 3 Pod.

maxSurge: 1
  saat rollout boleh ada 1 Pod ekstra.

maxUnavailable: 0
  Kubernetes tidak sengaja mengurangi Pod available.

readinessProbe:
  Pod baru tidak masuk Service endpoints sebelum ready.
```

---

# 5. Recreate Strategy

Recreate mematikan Pod lama sebelum membuat Pod baru.

```yaml id="rsvlmi"
strategy:
  type: Recreate
```

Flow:

```text id="5dxo8p"
Stop all old Pods
  │
  ▼
Start new Pods
  │
  ▼
Wait ready
```

Kapan mungkin dipakai:

```text id="7spg4a"
single-writer internal tool
batch-like service
service yang tidak boleh old/new version coexist
non-production environment
```

Untuk public/internal REST API yang harus available, Recreate biasanya bukan pilihan utama.

Risiko:

```text id="5y2gfe"
downtime saat old Pods mati dan new Pods belum ready
cold start Java terlihat ke user
rollback juga butuh waktu
```

---

# 6. `maxSurge` dan `maxUnavailable`

## `maxSurge`

```yaml id="gc7v78"
maxSurge: 1
```

Artinya Kubernetes boleh membuat Pod ekstra selama rollout.

Jika replicas = 3:

```text id="zrcx0d"
steady state:
  3 Pods

during rollout:
  up to 4 Pods
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="wwd692"
kapasitas tidak turun
Pod baru bisa siap sebelum Pod lama dikurangi
lebih aman untuk traffic stabil
```

Risiko:

```text id="51t0w4"
butuh node capacity ekstra
DB pool total bisa naik sementara
downstream concurrency bisa naik sementara
```

## `maxUnavailable`

```yaml id="t6pgzz"
maxUnavailable: 0
```

Artinya Kubernetes tidak sengaja membuat available Pod turun di bawah target saat rollout.

Untuk API production:

```text id="v48y0t"
maxUnavailable: 0
sering aman sebagai baseline
```

Tapi jika cluster tidak punya capacity tambahan untuk surge, rollout bisa stuck karena Pod baru tidak bisa dijadwalkan.

---

# 7. Release Capacity Math

Misalnya:

```text id="1o79ba"
replicas = 6
maxSurge = 2
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 10
```

Saat rollout:

```text id="7dxl1u"
max Pods sementara = 8
max DB connections sementara = 8 × 10 = 80
```

Bukan 60.

Jika HPA juga aktif:

```text id="6cdfqs"
HPA maxReplicas = 10
maxSurge = 2
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 10

worst-case rollout Pod count = 12
worst-case DB connections = 120
```

Senior-level rule:

```text id="lfud21"
Capacity review harus menghitung:
steady state replicas,
HPA maxReplicas,
maxSurge,
DB pool size,
worker concurrency,
dan downstream call concurrency.
```

---

# 8. Readiness sebagai Traffic Gate

Rolling update aman hanya jika readiness benar.

```text id="8ho2i2"
Pod v2 created
  │
  ├── JVM starts
  ├── config validated
  ├── Jersey initialized
  ├── DB pool initialized
  ├── app ready
  └── readiness returns 200
        │
        ▼
Service starts sending traffic
```

Kalau readiness terlalu longgar:

```text id="nzegoe"
Pod menerima traffic sebelum siap
→ 500/503/timeout
```

Kalau readiness terlalu ketat:

```text id="d03pje"
Pod tidak pernah ready karena optional dependency down
→ rollout stuck
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="74ro20"
/health/ready harus memastikan service siap menerima request utama,
tetapi tidak menjadikan seluruh dunia dependency sebagai syarat readiness.
```

---

# 9. `minReadySeconds` dan `progressDeadlineSeconds`

`minReadySeconds` dapat dipakai untuk memastikan Pod baru dianggap available hanya setelah ready secara stabil selama durasi tertentu.

```yaml id="od01mu"
spec:
  minReadySeconds: 10
```

`progressDeadlineSeconds` membantu mendeteksi rollout yang tidak membuat progress.

```yaml id="gibhea"
spec:
  progressDeadlineSeconds: 600
```

Pola:

```yaml id="ysamg2"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  minReadySeconds: 10
  progressDeadlineSeconds: 600
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
```

Manfaat:

```text id="jy5wef"
minReadySeconds:
  menghindari Pod dianggap available terlalu cepat.

progressDeadlineSeconds:
  membuat rollout stuck lebih mudah terdeteksi.
```

---

# 10. Rollout Commands

Status:

```bash id="66juwc"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

History:

```bash id="azjptd"
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

History revision:

```bash id="sgifka"
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod --revision=5
```

Undo ke revision sebelumnya:

```bash id="26iwr0"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Undo ke revision spesifik:

```bash id="kfh8fa"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod --to-revision=4
```

Restart rollout:

```bash id="3gio8r"
kubectl rollout restart deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Kubectl menyediakan subcommands rollout seperti `status`, `history`, `undo`, `restart`, `pause`, dan `resume` untuk mengelola rollout resource. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_rollout/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 11. Rollback: Aman atau Tidak?

Rollback image mudah:

```bash id="e9189s"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Tetapi rollback sistem belum tentu aman.

Rollback aman jika:

```text id="v8btg6"
versi lama masih compatible dengan database schema sekarang
ConfigMap/Secret masih compatible
API contract masih compatible
message/event schema masih compatible
Camunda workflow/task variables masih compatible
```

Rollback berbahaya jika release baru sudah melakukan:

```text id="j65598"
DROP column yang dipakai versi lama
rename column tanpa compatibility
ubah enum value yang tidak dikenali versi lama
ubah JSON schema event/message secara breaking
ubah Camunda variable format secara breaking
ubah API response mandatory field secara breaking
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="njzoag"
Rollback aplikasi hanya aman jika data/schema/contract tetap backward-compatible.
```

---

# 12. Database Migration Safety untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL

Untuk rolling update, Pod lama dan baru bisa berjalan bersamaan.

```text id="odm7k0"
During rollout:
  order-api v1 Pod masih melayani request
  order-api v2 Pod mulai melayani request
  keduanya memakai PostgreSQL yang sama
```

Karena itu migration harus mengikuti pola **expand/contract**.

## Release A - Expand

Tambah struktur baru tanpa merusak versi lama.

```sql id="ny2rbe"
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN external_reference TEXT;
```

Aman karena versi lama tidak peduli kolom baru.

## Release B - Dual write / compatible read

Versi baru mulai menulis kolom baru, tapi masih mempertahankan format lama jika perlu.

```text id="ap520g"
v1 reads old structure
v2 reads old + new structure
v2 writes old + new if needed
```

## Release C - Switch read path

Aplikasi mulai mengandalkan struktur baru setelah semua data backfilled.

## Release D - Contract

Hapus struktur lama setelah semua versi lama sudah tidak berjalan dan data aman.

```sql id="l7j7q1"
ALTER TABLE orders DROP COLUMN old_reference;
```

Jangan lakukan drop/rename destructive dalam release yang sama dengan app rollout jika versi lama masih mungkin berjalan atau rollback masih mungkin dibutuhkan.

---

# 13. MyBatis Mapper Compatibility

Contoh masalah:

Versi lama mapper:

```sql id="5xmexj"
SELECT id, status, total_amount
FROM orders
WHERE id = #{id}
```

Versi baru migration:

```sql id="f1kvta"
ALTER TABLE orders RENAME COLUMN total_amount TO amount;
```

Saat rollout campuran:

```text id="6kgbkc"
v1 Pod query total_amount
DB sudah rename ke amount
v1 error
rollout/rollback gagal
```

Lebih aman:

```sql id="hm54t8"
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN amount NUMERIC;
```

Lalu versi baru bisa dual-read:

```text id="h3z7i1"
amount if not null else total_amount
```

Setelah semua aman, baru drop kolom lama pada release berikutnya.

---

# 14. API Compatibility Old/New Version

Saat rolling update, caller bisa memanggil Pod v1 atau v2.

Karena Service load balances ke ready Pods, dalam periode rollout:

```text id="qdegz5"
same Service endpoint:
  some requests go to v1
  some requests go to v2
```

Maka API harus backward-compatible.

Breaking change yang berbahaya:

```text id="nqrcic"
hapus field response yang dipakai consumer
ubah tipe field
ubah status code behavior
ubah required request field mendadak
ubah semantic endpoint tanpa versioning
ubah error code contract
```

Safe change:

```text id="imshvw"
tambah optional response field
tambah optional request field
tambah endpoint baru
menjaga old endpoint tetap bekerja
default behavior kompatibel
```

Untuk contract-first approach:

```text id="3uq5x2"
OpenAPI diff harus dijalankan di CI.
Breaking change harus eksplisit dan direncanakan.
Generated client harus compatible dengan server version policy.
```

---

# 15. Config Compatibility

Config juga harus backward-compatible selama rollout.

Contoh buruk:

```text id="k8u64l"
Deployment v2 membutuhkan env NEW_PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MS
ConfigMap belum update
Pod v2 gagal startup
rollout stuck
```

Atau:

```text id="6d1pi4"
ConfigMap update menghapus OLD_PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MS
Pod v1 restart
v1 gagal startup
rollback gagal
```

Pola aman:

```text id="gmihz1"
tambahkan config baru dulu
app punya default aman jika config optional
jangan hapus config lama sampai versi lama hilang
validasi config saat startup
gunakan versioned config jika perubahan besar
```

---

# 16. Image Tagging dan Rollout Traceability

Jangan deploy dengan tag ambigu seperti:

```text id="fyh7mz"
latest
prod
stable
```

Gunakan:

```text id="wqbykv"
semantic version:
  order-api:1.9.0

git SHA:
  order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

digest:
  registry.example.com/order-api@sha256:...
```

Label recommended Kubernetes bisa membantu tooling dan operasional. Kubernetes menyediakan recommended labels dengan prefix `app.kubernetes.io`, termasuk `app.kubernetes.io/version` untuk versi aplikasi atau Git revision. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/overview/working-with-objects/common-labels/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="it5gu2"
metadata:
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
```

---

# 17. Blue-Green Deployment

Blue-green berarti ada dua versi/environment yang sama-sama siap, lalu traffic dipindahkan dari blue ke green.

```text id="ee7stv"
Blue:
  order-api v1
  currently receives traffic

Green:
  order-api v2
  deployed and tested
  not yet receiving main traffic

Switch:
  Service selector or Gateway route moves traffic to Green
```

Diagram:

```text id="b65gbg"
Service/order-api
  selector: version=blue
      │
      ▼
order-api-blue Pods v1

order-api-green Pods v2
  ready but not selected

Switch selector:
  version=green
```

Manifest Service:

```yaml id="nfxkyp"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    release-color: blue
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 80
      targetPort: 8080
```

Blue Deployment:

```yaml id="pt4p6g"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api-blue
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
      release-color: blue
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        release-color: blue
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.8.3"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.8.3
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080
```

Green Deployment:

```yaml id="a8oqzq"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api-green
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
      release-color: green
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        release-color: green
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080
```

Switch:

```bash id="sf7csy"
kubectl patch svc order-api -n order-prod \
  -p '{"spec":{"selector":{"app.kubernetes.io/name":"order-api","release-color":"green"}}}'
```

Rollback switch:

```bash id="lo9qnz"
kubectl patch svc order-api -n order-prod \
  -p '{"spec":{"selector":{"app.kubernetes.io/name":"order-api","release-color":"blue"}}}'
```

---

# 18. Blue-Green Pros and Cons

Kelebihan:

```text id="x4w31q"
rollback cepat dengan switch selector/route
versi baru bisa dites sebelum traffic utama
tidak mencampur old/new di Service jika tidak diinginkan
cocok untuk release besar/berisiko
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="q0ybal"
butuh kapasitas hampir ganda
DB schema tetap harus compatible
background worker bisa double-run jika tidak dipisahkan
stateful side effect harus hati-hati
service selector switch bisa terlalu kasar jika butuh gradual traffic
```

Blue-green tetap tidak menyelesaikan migration destructive. Jika green migration merusak blue, rollback selector tidak membantu.

---

# 19. Canary Deployment

Canary berarti merilis versi baru ke sebagian kecil traffic dulu.

```text id="49x6gk"
95% traffic → stable v1
5% traffic  → canary v2
```

Ada beberapa implementasi.

## Option A - Replica-based canary dengan Service yang sama

Dua Deployment berbagi label selector Service.

Stable:

```yaml id="0zhvyn"
metadata:
  name: order-api-stable
spec:
  replicas: 9
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        track: stable
```

Canary:

```yaml id="ilfqeg"
metadata:
  name: order-api-canary
spec:
  replicas: 1
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        track: canary
```

Service:

```yaml id="nz6bp0"
spec:
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
```

Approximation:

```text id="228skt"
9 stable Pods
1 canary Pod
≈ roughly 10% Pod share
```

Keterbatasan:

```text id="b6pfyq"
bukan traffic weighting presisi
request distribution tergantung load balancing dan connection behavior
tidak mudah target user tertentu
rollback = scale canary 0
```

## Option B - Weighted routing via Ingress/Gateway/service mesh

Untuk canary yang lebih presisi, biasanya gunakan:

```text id="n7rer2"
Ingress controller feature
Gateway API/controller feature
service mesh
progressive delivery controller
platform-specific rollout tool
```

Kubernetes Service standar tidak menyediakan weighted traffic split per versi sebagai primitive umum. Jadi untuk weighted canary, ikuti capability platform.

---

# 20. Canary Safety Gates

Canary harus punya observability gate.

Metric yang dipantau:

```text id="mo25gh"
5xx rate canary vs stable
p95/p99 latency canary vs stable
business success rate
DB pool pending
downstream timeout
JVM restarts/OOM
error logs by version
trace error by version
```

Label version harus ada:

```yaml id="pjiv2j"
labels:
  app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
  track: canary
```

Jika metrics/logs/traces tidak bisa difilter by version/track, canary kehilangan nilai.

---

# 21. Canary Decision Flow

```text id="gp85s1"
Deploy canary v2 at 5%
  │
  ├── monitor 10-30 minutes or business-defined window
  │
  ├── if error/latency/business metrics healthy
  │     └── increase to 25%
  │
  ├── monitor again
  │
  ├── increase to 50%
  │
  ├── increase to 100%
  │
  └── scale stable down
```

Jika buruk:

```text id="p87aui"
scale canary to 0
or route traffic back to stable
keep evidence
analyze logs/traces by canary version
```

Canary bukan hanya “deploy 1 Pod baru”. Canary adalah **decision process**.

---

# 22. Feature Flags

Feature flag bisa mengurangi risiko release.

```text id="8nul66"
Deploy code v2
  feature disabled

Enable feature for internal users
Enable 1% customers
Enable 10%
Enable 100%
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="9g5e9s"
deploy dan release dipisahkan
rollback feature bisa lebih cepat daripada rollback image
can target user/tenant/region
```

Risiko:

```text id="449nsl"
flag complexity
dead flags menumpuk
kombinasi flag sulit dites
DB schema tetap harus compatible
```

Prinsip:

```text id="38f2r4"
Feature flag bukan pengganti testing.
Feature flag adalah blast-radius control.
```

---

# 23. Shadow / Dark Launch

Shadow traffic berarti request dikirim ke versi baru untuk observasi, tapi response tidak dipakai user.

```text id="71wpgk"
Real request → stable v1 → response to user
          └→ shadow copy → v2 → response discarded
```

Kegunaan:

```text id="rz16gk"
uji performa versi baru dengan traffic realistis
validasi parsing/logic tanpa user impact
bandingkan response secara offline
```

Risiko:

```text id="3yg2su"
jangan lakukan side effect
jangan write DB production tanpa isolasi
jangan call payment/external side-effecting API
jangan duplicate Camunda workflow
```

Untuk order/payment/workflow system, shadow traffic harus sangat hati-hati karena banyak operasi punya side effect.

---

# 24. Deployment Strategy Decision Matrix

| Strategy | Cocok Untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| **RollingUpdate** | REST API stateless harian | Simple, native Deployment, no full downtime | Old/new coexist; butuh compatibility |
| **Recreate** | Non-critical/internal/singleton | Simple, tidak ada mixed version | Downtime |
| **Blue-Green** | Release besar, rollback cepat | Switch cepat, pre-test green | Kapasitas ganda, DB compatibility tetap wajib |
| **Canary** | High-risk release, traffic besar | Blast radius kecil | Butuh routing/observability matang |
| **Feature Flag** | Perubahan behavior | Deploy/release terpisah | Flag debt |
| **Shadow** | Validasi performa/logic tanpa response user | Realistic traffic test | Side-effect risk |

---

# 25. PodDisruptionBudget dan Release Availability

PodDisruptionBudget atau PDB membatasi jumlah Pod aplikasi yang bisa terganggu secara bersamaan saat voluntary disruption, seperti node drain. Kubernetes docs menjelaskan PDB dipakai untuk membatasi concurrent disruptions agar aplikasi tetap lebih available saat cluster admin melakukan operasi seperti maintenance node. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/run-application/configure-pdb/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="i2y2dx"
apiVersion: policy/v1
kind: PodDisruptionBudget
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  minAvailable: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
```

Jika replicas = 3:

```text id="gj1mct"
minimal 2 Pod harus tetap available
voluntary disruption tidak boleh menurunkan available Pod di bawah 2
```

Hati-hati dengan HPA:

```text id="jrab8u"
minReplicas = 2
PDB minAvailable = 2
```

Ini bisa membuat node drain sulit karena tidak ada Pod yang boleh terganggu. Untuk production critical bisa benar, tapi harus disadari platform team.

---

# 26. Graceful Shutdown During Deployment

Rolling update akan menghentikan Pod lama. Jika graceful shutdown buruk:

```text id="c8gixe"
Pod lama masih menerima request
SIGTERM datang
server stop mendadak
in-flight request terputus
client mendapat 502/connection reset
```

Baseline:

```yaml id="yl9cl8"
spec:
  terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45
  containers:
    - name: order-api
      lifecycle:
        preStop:
          exec:
            command: ["sh", "-c", "sleep 10"]
```

Aplikasi:

```text id="xwez5v"
on SIGTERM:
  readiness false
  stop accepting new work
  drain in-flight requests
  stop workers
  close DB/client pools
  exit before grace period
```

Deployment strategy tanpa graceful shutdown bukan zero-downtime.

---

# 27. Camunda Worker Deployment Safety

Worker berbeda dari REST API.

Saat rollout worker:

```text id="t6bijz"
old worker may hold locked tasks
new worker starts fetching tasks
same topic processed by old/new version
```

Risiko:

```text id="7aq00t"
task duplicate side effect
variable format incompatible
BPMN error code berubah
lock duration terlalu pendek/panjang
worker mati sebelum complete task
```

Guardrail:

```text id="0v6fnh"
handler idempotent
business key/idempotency key
old/new variable schema compatible
stop fetching new tasks on SIGTERM
active task drain
lock duration aligned with grace period
retry policy jelas
```

Untuk canary worker:

```text id="kqq2u0"
jangan hanya tambah replica canary jika task side effect tidak idempotent
batasi topic/workerId/concurrency
monitor failure/retry/backlog
```

---

# 28. PostgreSQL Connection Impact During Rollout

Rolling update bisa sementara menambah Pod.

Contoh:

```text id="mjh7x3"
replicas = 5
maxSurge = 2
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 15

steady DB max = 75
rollout DB max = 105
```

Jika PostgreSQL budget service ini hanya 80 connection, rollout bisa menyebabkan DB pool pressure.

Mitigasi:

```text id="j1hn0o"
kurangi maxSurge
kurangi DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
pakai PgBouncer jika platform mendukung
atur HPA maxReplicas
monitor active/pending/timeout pool
lakukan rollout saat traffic rendah jika perlu
```

---

# 29. Deployment Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Cara Cek |
|---|---|---|
| Rollout stuck | Pod baru tidak ready | `rollout status`, `describe pod`, logs |
| 5xx naik saat rollout | readiness/graceful shutdown buruk | metrics/logs by version |
| DB error setelah deploy | migration incompatible | DB logs, MyBatis errors |
| Rollback gagal | schema/config tidak backward-compatible | migration history |
| Latency naik hanya versi baru | code/performance regression | metrics/traces by version |
| Pod Pending saat rollout | surge butuh capacity ekstra | scheduler events |
| ImagePullBackOff | image tag/registry/pull secret | describe pod |
| Canary tidak bisa dianalisis | no version labels/metrics | labels/observability |
| Worker duplicate | non-idempotent handler | business logs/Camunda history |
| Service selector salah | blue/green switch buruk | Service YAML, EndpointSlice |

---

# 30. Debugging Commands for Release

Rollout status:

```bash id="qhh2sd"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Rollout history:

```bash id="c7gyq4"
kubectl rollout history deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Pods by version:

```bash id="b6udzu"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api \
  --show-labels
```

ReplicaSets:

```bash id="tvxdmg"
kubectl get rs -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

Events:

```bash id="na2f7p"
kubectl get events -n order-prod --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

EndpointSlice:

```bash id="9j7y2p"
kubectl get endpointslice -n order-prod \
  -l kubernetes.io/service-name=order-api
```

Logs by Deployment:

```bash id="evog58"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --since=15m
```

Undo:

```bash id="o16ukc"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

---

# 31. Pre-Deploy Checklist

| Area | Question |
|---|---|
| Image | Tag immutable? Digest available? |
| Tests | Unit/integration/contract tests passed? |
| OpenAPI | Breaking change checked? |
| DB migration | Expand/contract safe? |
| MyBatis | Old/new mapper compatible with schema? |
| Config | New config added without breaking old version? |
| Secrets | Secret rotation compatible? |
| Readiness | New version becomes ready only when truly ready? |
| Graceful shutdown | Old version drains safely? |
| Capacity | maxSurge + HPA + DB pool safe? |
| Observability | Metrics/logs/traces tagged by version? |
| Rollback | Rollback plan safe with DB/schema/config? |
| Camunda | Worker/task variable compatibility checked? |
| PDB/HPA | Rollout won’t fight disruption/autoscaling policy? |

---

# 32. During-Deploy Checklist

Pantau:

```text id="naaz47"
rollout status
new Pod readiness
old Pod termination
5xx rate
p95/p99 latency
DB pool pending
downstream timeout
JVM restart/OOM
business success rate
Camunda task failure/retry
logs by version
traces by version
```

Commands:

```bash id="5h0c3l"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api -w
```

Jika error naik:

```text id="ph35bg"
stop rollout if supported by pipeline/controller
rollback image if safe
or scale canary to 0
or switch blue-green back
preserve evidence
```

---

# 33. Post-Deploy Checklist

Pastikan:

```text id="1tc2ex"
rollout complete
all Pods ready
old ReplicaSet scaled down as expected
no unexpected restarts
error rate normal
latency normal
DB pool stable
downstream stable
business metrics normal
no alert active
```

Command:

```bash id="4fvn82"
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod
kubectl get rs -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

Observability:

```text id="amk5uw"
compare version 1.8.3 vs 1.9.0
check 5xx, latency, DB pool, worker failure
confirm no slow burn issue
```

---

# 34. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Deploy DB Breaking Change Bersamaan dengan App

Buruk:

```text id="0ujr9f"
migration rename/drop column
deploy app v2
v1 still running during rollout
v1 crashes
rollback impossible
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="9x16ae"
expand → deploy compatible app → backfill → switch read → contract later
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - `maxUnavailable: 1` untuk Replica Kecil

Jika replicas = 1:

```yaml id="mpwheu"
maxUnavailable: 1
```

Artinya service bisa punya 0 available Pod saat rollout.

Untuk API critical dengan replica kecil:

```yaml id="ah6gkg"
maxSurge: 1
maxUnavailable: 0
```

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Canary Tanpa Metrics per Version

Canary tidak berguna jika tidak bisa menjawab:

```text id="0miad8"
Apakah v2 lebih error daripada v1?
Apakah v2 lebih lambat daripada v1?
Apakah v2 merusak business success?
```

Tambahkan label `app.kubernetes.io/version` dan pastikan metrics/logs/traces membawa version.

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Rollback Buta

Buruk:

```text id="7zf0fy"
error naik
langsung rollback
tidak cek DB migration/config compatibility
```

Rollback bisa memperparah jika schema sudah tidak compatible.

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Blue-Green dengan Worker Aktif Ganda

Jika blue dan green worker sama-sama aktif:

```text id="n0rd3t"
blue worker process topic
green worker process same topic
side effect duplicate
```

Pisahkan worker activation, topic, concurrency, atau gunakan feature flag/leader control.

---

# 35. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Strategy | Rolling, blue-green, canary, atau recreate? Kenapa? |
| Readiness | Apakah readiness benar-benar traffic gate? |
| Shutdown | Apakah old Pod drain sebelum mati? |
| Capacity | Apakah maxSurge/HPA/DB pool/downstream aman? |
| DB | Apakah migration expand/contract dan rollback-safe? |
| API | Apakah contract backward-compatible? |
| Config | Apakah config lama/baru kompatibel selama rollout? |
| Observability | Apakah metrics/logs/traces bisa filter by version? |
| Rollback | Apakah rollback image aman terhadap schema/data? |
| Canary | Apakah ada health gate dan threshold? |
| Blue-green | Apakah switch/rollback procedure jelas? |
| Worker | Apakah Camunda/Kafka worker old/new coexist aman? |
| PDB | Apakah PDB tidak menghambat rollout/maintenance secara tidak sadar? |
| HPA | Apakah autoscaling tidak memperbesar risiko saat rollout? |
| Audit | Apakah release evidence tersimpan di GitHub/GitOps/CI/CD? |

---

# 36. Ringkasan Seri 19

```text id="a6u7r8"
RollingUpdate:
  update bertahap, cocok untuk REST API stateless.

Recreate:
  matikan lama dulu, cocok hanya untuk kasus tertentu yang bisa downtime.

Rollback:
  mudah secara Deployment, tetapi hanya aman jika DB/config/contract compatible.

Blue-Green:
  dua versi siap, traffic switch cepat, butuh kapasitas dan compatibility.

Canary:
  rilis sebagian traffic, butuh routing/observability/safety gate.

Feature Flag:
  pisahkan deploy dari release behavior.

Shadow:
  uji versi baru dengan traffic real tanpa response user,
  tetapi sangat berbahaya untuk side-effecting operations.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey + MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text id="iq3a78"
Gunakan RollingUpdate sebagai baseline.
Set maxSurge dan maxUnavailable berdasarkan capacity.
Pastikan readiness dan graceful shutdown benar.
Gunakan immutable image tag/digest.
Tambahkan app.kubernetes.io/version.
Jalankan DB migration expand/contract.
Pastikan MyBatis mapper old/new compatible.
Pastikan OpenAPI contract backward-compatible.
Monitor error/latency/DB pool/business metrics by version.
Rollback hanya jika schema/config/data masih compatible.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="z5cb9p"
Release aman bukan release yang tidak pernah gagal.
Release aman adalah release yang:
blast radius-nya kecil,
failure-nya cepat terdeteksi,
rollback-nya sudah dipikirkan,
dan data/contract tetap konsisten walau old dan new version hidup bersamaan.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:25:16 PM

Lanjut ke **Seri 20/24**. Fokusnya manifest management: bagaimana menyusun YAML Kubernetes agar reusable, environment-aware, aman untuk CI/CD/GitOps, dan tidak berubah menjadi kumpulan file copy-paste yang sulit diaudit.

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 20/24  
## Helm, Kustomize, Manifest Management, Environment Overlays, Values, Release Naming, Secret/Config Strategy, dan GitOps-friendly Kubernetes Manifest untuk Java/Jersey Microservice

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana mengelola YAML Kubernetes untuk Java/Jersey/JAX-RS microservice agar konsisten, reusable, aman, mudah di-review, dan cocok untuk CI/CD atau GitOps?**

Kubernetes manifest yang awalnya hanya `deployment.yaml` dan `service.yaml` biasanya cepat membesar: HPA, NetworkPolicy, ServiceAccount, RBAC, ConfigMap, Secret reference, probes, securityContext, ingress/gateway, observability, dan environment-specific overrides. Tanpa strategi, manifest berubah menjadi copy-paste antar `dev`, `staging`, dan `prod`.

Kubernetes mendokumentasikan Kustomize sebagai tool untuk meng-customize object Kubernetes melalui `kustomization.yaml`, dan `kubectl` sudah mendukung kustomization sejak Kubernetes 1.14. Helm sendiri mendeskripsikan Chart sebagai cara untuk mendefinisikan, meng-install, dan meng-upgrade aplikasi Kubernetes yang kompleks. Untuk GitOps, Argo CD mendeskripsikan pola Git sebagai source of truth untuk desired application state, dan mendukung plain YAML, Kustomize, Helm, Jsonnet, atau config management plugin. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/manage-kubernetes-objects/kustomization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="lp9x7k"
Kubernetes Manifest Management
│
├── Raw YAML
│   ├── explicit
│   ├── easy to read
│   └── copy-paste risk
│
├── Kustomize
│   ├── base + overlays
│   ├── patch without templates
│   ├── good for environment differences
│   └── native with kubectl
│
├── Helm
│   ├── chart
│   ├── templates
│   ├── values.yaml
│   ├── release lifecycle
│   └── good for reusable packages
│
├── GitOps
│   ├── Git as desired state
│   ├── pull-based reconciliation
│   ├── audit through commits/PRs
│   └── drift detection
│
└── CI/CD Controls
    ├── render
    ├── lint
    ├── validate
    ├── diff
    ├── security policy check
    └── deploy/sync
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="nvk900"
Manifest bukan sekadar YAML.
Manifest adalah release contract:
apa image yang jalan, config apa yang dipakai,
permission apa yang diberikan, service mana yang diekspos,
resource berapa yang diminta, dan policy apa yang berlaku.
```

---

# 2. Diagram

```text id="67j6qd"
Source Code Repo
│
├── src/main/java
├── pom.xml
├── Dockerfile
└── deploy/
    │
    ├── base/
    │   ├── deployment.yaml
    │   ├── service.yaml
    │   ├── serviceaccount.yaml
    │   ├── hpa.yaml
    │   ├── networkpolicy.yaml
    │   └── kustomization.yaml
    │
    └── overlays/
        ├── dev/
        │   ├── kustomization.yaml
        │   └── patch-values.yaml
        ├── staging/
        │   ├── kustomization.yaml
        │   └── patch-values.yaml
        └── prod/
            ├── kustomization.yaml
            ├── patch-resources.yaml
            ├── patch-hpa.yaml
            └── patch-ingress.yaml

CI/CD or GitOps
│
├── render manifest
├── validate manifest
├── diff desired vs live
├── apply/sync
└── monitor rollout
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Raw YAML Discipline** | Struktur manifest eksplisit dan readable. | Awal service biasanya raw YAML. | Copy-paste drift antar environment. |
| 2 | **Kustomize Base/Overlay** | Base reusable, overlay untuk dev/staging/prod. | Cocok untuk patch image, replicas, resources, HPA. | Environment berbeda tanpa jejak jelas. |
| 3 | **Helm Chart** | Packaging template + values. | Cocok untuk standard internal Java service chart. | Template terlalu kompleks dan sulit di-review. |
| 4 | **Values Management** | `values.yaml`, `values-prod.yaml`, override rules. Helm chart dapat menyertakan default `values.yaml`, dan install/upgrade dapat override dengan values tambahan. ([Helm](https://helm.sh/docs/topics/charts/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Environment-specific config. | Prod deploy pakai value dev. |
| 5 | **Release Naming** | Nama release/workload konsisten. | `order-api`, `order-worker`, `order-api-canary`. | Collision, Service selector salah. |
| 6 | **Labels & Selectors** | `app.kubernetes.io/*`, selector immutable discipline. Kubernetes merekomendasikan common labels agar object bisa dikelola dan di-query secara interoperable oleh tooling. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/overview/working-with-objects/common-labels/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Service, NetworkPolicy, HPA, dashboards, logs. | Service memilih Pod yang salah. |
| 7 | **ConfigMap Strategy** | Config dipisahkan dari image. Kubernetes menjelaskan ConfigMap membantu memisahkan configuration artifacts dari image agar aplikasi portable antar environment. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-pod-configmap/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Jersey app config: DB pool, timeout, feature flag. | Image harus rebuild untuk ubah config. |
| 8 | **Secret Strategy** | Secret tidak ditaruh plain di Git. Kubernetes Secret menyimpan sensitive data seperti password/token/key; good practices menekankan encryption at rest dan kontrol akses. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | DB password, OAuth secret, token, TLS key. | Credential leak. |
| 9 | **Immutable Image Reference** | Tag/digest jelas. | Rollout traceability. | `latest` membuat deploy tidak reproducible. |
| 10 | **Environment Overlay** | Dev/staging/prod berbeda secara terkendali. | Resource, HPA, ingress, NetworkPolicy beda per env. | Prod tidak sama dengan staging tanpa sadar. |
| 11 | **Render Before Apply** | `kubectl kustomize`, `helm template`. | Review hasil akhir, bukan template mental. | Bug baru terlihat setelah apply. |
| 12 | **GitOps Friendliness** | Desired state di Git, PR review, audit. | Onboarding dan operations lebih aman. | Manual cluster drift. |
| 13 | **Policy Validation** | SecurityContext, RBAC, NetworkPolicy, resource limits dicek sebelum deploy. | Prevent bad manifest. | Policy violation baru ketahuan saat prod apply. |
| 14 | **Diff Discipline** | Lihat perubahan manifest sebelum apply/sync. | Release review. | Deploy perubahan tak terduga. |
| 15 | **Separation of Concerns** | App repo vs platform repo vs secrets repo. | Team ownership jelas. | Semua orang bisa ubah semua hal. |

---

# 4. Masalah yang Ingin Diselesaikan

Tanpa manifest management, biasanya muncul masalah seperti:

```text id="woqpi5"
deployment-dev.yaml
deployment-staging.yaml
deployment-prod.yaml
```

Lalu setiap file punya isi hampir sama tapi sedikit berbeda.

Masalah:

```text id="sydm0c"
securityContext prod ada, staging lupa
readiness path dev beda dari prod
DB pool prod berubah tapi HPA maxReplicas tidak dihitung ulang
NetworkPolicy hanya ada di prod dan tidak pernah dites di staging
ConfigMap key dihapus dan rollback gagal
image tag latest membuat revision tidak jelas
```

Manifest management yang baik membuat perbedaan environment **eksplisit, kecil, dan reviewable**.

---

# 5. Raw YAML: Kapan Masih Cocok?

Raw YAML cocok untuk:

```text id="s9e4gl"
service kecil
learning phase
manifest sedikit
environment hanya satu
platform belum standard
```

Contoh minimal:

```yaml id="y5orwo"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/component: api
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080
```

Tetapi saat environment bertambah, raw YAML cenderung berubah menjadi copy-paste. Pada titik itu, gunakan Kustomize atau Helm.

---

# 6. Kustomize: Mental Model

Kustomize bekerja dengan konsep:

```text id="9xhj9t"
base:
  manifest umum yang berlaku untuk semua environment

overlay:
  patch environment-specific
```

Kustomize bukan templating engine dalam gaya Helm; ia melakukan customization terhadap resource YAML. Kubernetes docs menjelaskan Kustomize sebagai standalone tool untuk customize Kubernetes object melalui `kustomization.yaml`, dan `kubectl kustomize <directory>` dapat merender resource dari direktori yang berisi kustomization file. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/manage-kubernetes-objects/kustomization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Struktur:

```text id="8ymsyu"
deploy/
├── base/
│   ├── deployment.yaml
│   ├── service.yaml
│   ├── serviceaccount.yaml
│   ├── hpa.yaml
│   └── kustomization.yaml
└── overlays/
    ├── dev/
    │   ├── kustomization.yaml
    │   └── patch-resources.yaml
    ├── staging/
    │   ├── kustomization.yaml
    │   └── patch-resources.yaml
    └── prod/
        ├── kustomization.yaml
        ├── patch-resources.yaml
        ├── patch-hpa.yaml
        └── patch-networkpolicy.yaml
```

---

# 7. Kustomize Base Example

`deploy/base/kustomization.yaml`:

```yaml id="6xxgl3"
apiVersion: kustomize.config.k8s.io/v1beta1
kind: Kustomization

resources:
  - serviceaccount.yaml
  - deployment.yaml
  - service.yaml
  - hpa.yaml
  - networkpolicy.yaml

commonLabels:
  app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
```

`deploy/base/deployment.yaml`:

```yaml id="byzq7w"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: order-api-sa
      automountServiceAccountToken: false
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:REPLACED_BY_OVERLAY
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          env:
            - name: APP_NAME
              value: order-api
            - name: APP_PROFILE
              value: default

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "250m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "1"
              memory: "1024Mi"
```

`deploy/base/service.yaml`:

```yaml id="gcjprn"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  selector:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 80
      targetPort: 8080
```

---

# 8. Kustomize Overlay: Dev

`deploy/overlays/dev/kustomization.yaml`:

```yaml id="2kdaer"
apiVersion: kustomize.config.k8s.io/v1beta1
kind: Kustomization

namespace: order-dev

resources:
  - ../../base

images:
  - name: registry.example.com/order-api
    newTag: 1.9.0-dev.12

patches:
  - path: patch-env.yaml
  - path: patch-resources.yaml
```

`patch-env.yaml`:

```yaml id="0ngte7"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          env:
            - name: APP_PROFILE
              value: dev
            - name: LOG_LEVEL
              value: DEBUG
```

`patch-resources.yaml`:

```yaml id="27la5w"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  replicas: 1
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "100m"
              memory: "256Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
```

Render:

```bash id="e5p0ku"
kubectl kustomize deploy/overlays/dev
```

Apply:

```bash id="dtn7yp"
kubectl apply -k deploy/overlays/dev
```

---

# 9. Kustomize Overlay: Prod

`deploy/overlays/prod/kustomization.yaml`:

```yaml id="mx7otk"
apiVersion: kustomize.config.k8s.io/v1beta1
kind: Kustomization

namespace: order-prod

resources:
  - ../../base

images:
  - name: registry.example.com/order-api
    newTag: 1.9.0-a1b2c3d

patches:
  - path: patch-env.yaml
  - path: patch-replicas-resources.yaml
  - path: patch-hpa.yaml
```

`patch-env.yaml`:

```yaml id="2903b4"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          env:
            - name: APP_PROFILE
              value: prod
            - name: LOG_LEVEL
              value: INFO
```

`patch-replicas-resources.yaml`:

```yaml id="w4fcc2"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  replicas: 3
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-api
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1024Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2048Mi"
```

`patch-hpa.yaml`:

```yaml id="okv6ly"
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: order-api
spec:
  minReplicas: 3
  maxReplicas: 8
  metrics:
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: cpu
        target:
          type: Utilization
          averageUtilization: 65
```

Render:

```bash id="wxhjqc"
kubectl kustomize deploy/overlays/prod
```

Apply:

```bash id="p6unrt"
kubectl apply -k deploy/overlays/prod
```

---

# 10. Kustomize Strengths and Weaknesses

| Area | Kustomize |
|---|---|
| Strength | Native with `kubectl`, readable base/overlay, no template language. |
| Strength | Good for environment overlays. |
| Strength | Good for patching standard manifests. |
| Weakness | Complex conditional logic is awkward. |
| Weakness | Reusable package distribution is less natural than Helm. |
| Weakness | Very large overlays can become hard to reason about. |

Kustomize cocok jika:

```text id="8zi4a6"
Anda punya manifest jelas.
Perbedaan environment adalah patch kecil.
Team ingin hasil YAML mudah dibaca.
Platform tidak butuh chart reusable kompleks.
```

---

# 11. Helm: Mental Model

Helm adalah package manager untuk Kubernetes application. Helm Chart berisi template manifest, metadata chart, dan default values; Helm render template dengan values untuk menghasilkan Kubernetes manifest. Helm mendokumentasikan bahwa file template disimpan di folder `templates/`, dan ketika chart dirender, file di folder itu diproses oleh template engine. ([Helm](https://helm.sh/docs/topics/charts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Struktur:

```text id="ctu3go"
order-api-chart/
├── Chart.yaml
├── values.yaml
├── values-dev.yaml
├── values-staging.yaml
├── values-prod.yaml
└── templates/
    ├── _helpers.tpl
    ├── deployment.yaml
    ├── service.yaml
    ├── serviceaccount.yaml
    ├── hpa.yaml
    ├── configmap.yaml
    ├── networkpolicy.yaml
    └── ingress.yaml
```

Helm cocok jika organisasi ingin membuat “standard Java microservice chart” yang dipakai banyak service.

---

# 12. Helm `Chart.yaml`

```yaml id="m2l1wi"
apiVersion: v2
name: order-api
description: Helm chart for order-api Java/Jersey microservice
type: application
version: 0.1.0
appVersion: "1.9.0"
```

Makna:

```text id="40nwfo"
version:
  versi chart

appVersion:
  versi aplikasi yang chart ini deploy
```

Jangan campur:

```text id="ayitkg"
Chart version != application version
```

---

# 13. Helm `values.yaml`

```yaml id="nh9sd8"
replicaCount: 2

image:
  repository: registry.example.com/order-api
  tag: "1.9.0"
  pullPolicy: IfNotPresent

app:
  name: order-api
  profile: default
  version: "1.9.0"

service:
  type: ClusterIP
  port: 80
  targetPort: 8080

resources:
  requests:
    cpu: 250m
    memory: 512Mi
  limits:
    cpu: "1"
    memory: 1024Mi

hpa:
  enabled: true
  minReplicas: 2
  maxReplicas: 6
  targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70

logging:
  level: INFO

security:
  automountServiceAccountToken: false
  runAsUser: 10001
  runAsGroup: 10001
  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
```

Helm values object mengakses value dari chart, parent chart, user-supplied values file, `--set`, dan sumber lain sesuai urutan override yang didokumentasikan Helm. ([Helm](https://helm.sh/docs/chart_template_guide/values_files/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 14. Helm `values-prod.yaml`

```yaml id="fdmgo5"
replicaCount: 3

image:
  tag: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"

app:
  profile: prod
  version: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"

resources:
  requests:
    cpu: 500m
    memory: 1024Mi
  limits:
    cpu: "2"
    memory: 2048Mi

hpa:
  enabled: true
  minReplicas: 3
  maxReplicas: 8
  targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 65

logging:
  level: INFO
```

Render:

```bash id="vs049l"
helm template order-api ./order-api-chart \
  -n order-prod \
  -f values-prod.yaml
```

Install/upgrade:

```bash id="ru4n8q"
helm upgrade --install order-api ./order-api-chart \
  -n order-prod \
  -f values-prod.yaml
```

---

# 15. Helm Template: Deployment

`templates/deployment.yaml`:

```yaml id="z6bsw3"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: {{ include "order-api.fullname" . }}
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ include "order-api.name" . }}
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .Release.Name }}
    app.kubernetes.io/version: {{ .Values.app.version | quote }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: {{ .Release.Service }}
spec:
  replicas: {{ .Values.replicaCount }}
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ include "order-api.name" . }}
      app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .Release.Name }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ include "order-api.name" . }}
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .Release.Name }}
        app.kubernetes.io/version: {{ .Values.app.version | quote }}
        app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: {{ include "order-api.serviceAccountName" . }}
      automountServiceAccountToken: {{ .Values.security.automountServiceAccountToken }}
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: {{ .Values.security.runAsUser }}
        runAsGroup: {{ .Values.security.runAsGroup }}
        seccompProfile:
          type: RuntimeDefault
      containers:
        - name: {{ .Chart.Name }}
          image: "{{ .Values.image.repository }}:{{ .Values.image.tag }}"
          imagePullPolicy: {{ .Values.image.pullPolicy }}
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: {{ .Values.service.targetPort }}

          env:
            - name: APP_NAME
              value: {{ .Values.app.name | quote }}
            - name: APP_PROFILE
              value: {{ .Values.app.profile | quote }}
            - name: APP_VERSION
              value: {{ .Values.app.version | quote }}
            - name: LOG_LEVEL
              value: {{ .Values.logging.level | quote }}

          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            privileged: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: {{ .Values.security.readOnlyRootFilesystem }}
            capabilities:
              drop:
                - ALL

          resources:
            {{- toYaml .Values.resources | nindent 12 }}
```

Helm powerful, tetapi template yang terlalu pintar bisa sulit di-review. Render output harus selalu dicek.

---

# 16. Helm Helpers

`templates/_helpers.tpl`:

```gotemplate id="euro6x"
{{- define "order-api.name" -}}
{{- default .Chart.Name .Values.nameOverride | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "order-api.fullname" -}}
{{- if .Values.fullnameOverride -}}
{{- .Values.fullnameOverride | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- else -}}
{{- printf "%s" .Release.Name | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "order-api.serviceAccountName" -}}
{{- if .Values.serviceAccount.create -}}
{{- default (include "order-api.fullname" .) .Values.serviceAccount.name -}}
{{- else -}}
{{- default "default" .Values.serviceAccount.name -}}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}
```

Gunakan helpers untuk naming yang konsisten, tapi jangan membuat logic terlalu rumit.

---

# 17. Helm Strengths and Weaknesses

| Area | Helm |
|---|---|
| Strength | Package reusable application. |
| Strength | Values-based environment configuration. |
| Strength | Supports install/upgrade/rollback lifecycle. |
| Strength | Rich ecosystem. |
| Weakness | Template logic bisa sulit dibaca. |
| Weakness | Rendered YAML bisa berbeda jauh dari template mental. |
| Weakness | Values bisa menjadi nested configuration monster. |
| Weakness | Secret handling harus hati-hati. |

Helm cocok jika:

```text id="66r50l"
Banyak service memakai pola manifest sama.
Platform team ingin menyediakan standard chart.
Ada kebutuhan packaging/release lifecycle.
Service punya banyak optional components.
```

---

# 18. Kustomize vs Helm: Decision Matrix

| Kebutuhan | Raw YAML | Kustomize | Helm |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Sederhana, satu environment | Baik | Bisa | Overkill |
| Base + dev/staging/prod overlay | Kurang | Sangat cocok | Bisa |
| Package reusable antar banyak service | Kurang | Sedang | Sangat cocok |
| Template conditional logic | Tidak | Terbatas | Kuat |
| Readability hasil manifest | Tinggi | Tinggi | Perlu render |
| GitOps-friendly | Ya | Ya | Ya |
| Standard internal platform chart | Kurang | Sedang | Baik |
| Avoid templating complexity | Ya | Ya | Tidak selalu |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="cwqw0d"
Untuk satu service dengan environment overlay:
  Kustomize sering paling readable.

Untuk standard chart yang dipakai banyak Java service:
  Helm lebih cocok.

Untuk platform GitOps:
  Helm atau Kustomize sama-sama bisa, tergantung standard platform.
```

---

# 19. Helm + Kustomize Bersamaan?

Bisa, tetapi hati-hati.

Pola yang kadang dipakai:

```text id="e26y7l"
Helm chart:
  render manifest reusable

Kustomize:
  patch hasil render untuk environment/platform-specific differences
```

Risiko:

```text id="fxki1h"
debug lebih sulit
dua layer transform
source of truth lebih kabur
CI harus jelas render order-nya
```

Gunakan hanya jika ada alasan kuat, misalnya chart vendor perlu di-patch sesuai environment internal.

---

# 20. GitOps-Friendly Manifest

GitOps-friendly berarti desired state tersimpan di Git dan perubahan lewat PR.

```text id="tulnek"
Git commit
  → desired state changes
  → GitOps controller detects change
  → controller reconciles cluster
  → drift detected and corrected
```

Argo CD mengikuti pola GitOps: Git repository menjadi source of truth untuk desired application state; manifest bisa plain YAML, Kustomize, Helm, Jsonnet, atau plugin. ([Argo CD](https://argo-cd.readthedocs.io/en/stable/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Struktur GitOps yang umum:

```text id="lmv0fn"
app repo:
  source code
  Dockerfile
  Helm chart or Kustomize base

environment repo:
  desired version per environment
  values-prod.yaml
  overlays/prod/kustomization.yaml
  Argo CD Application manifests
```

Pemisahan ini berguna jika:

```text id="m9b8cg"
developer boleh ubah app code
platform/release owner mengontrol prod desired state
prod deployment butuh approval terpisah
audit deployment harus jelas
```

---

# 21. Example GitOps Repo Layout

```text id="lxqguv"
platform-gitops/
├── apps/
│   ├── order-api/
│   │   ├── dev/
│   │   │   └── kustomization.yaml
│   │   ├── staging/
│   │   │   └── kustomization.yaml
│   │   └── prod/
│   │       └── kustomization.yaml
│   └── order-worker/
│       └── prod/
│           └── kustomization.yaml
├── clusters/
│   ├── prod-aws/
│   ├── prod-azure/
│   └── onprem-prod/
└── platform/
    ├── ingress/
    ├── observability/
    └── policies/
```

Atau Helm-based:

```text id="ejpk8a"
platform-gitops/
├── apps/
│   └── order-api/
│       ├── Chart.yaml reference or chart version
│       ├── values-dev.yaml
│       ├── values-staging.yaml
│       └── values-prod.yaml
└── argocd/
    └── applications/
        └── order-api-prod.yaml
```

---

# 22. Release Naming

Gunakan naming yang predictable.

```text id="pyx57p"
Application:
  order-api

Helm release:
  order-api

Namespace:
  order-prod

Deployment:
  order-api

Service:
  order-api

ServiceAccount:
  order-api-sa

HPA:
  order-api

NetworkPolicy:
  order-api-ingress
  order-api-egress
```

Untuk worker:

```text id="4pu4fg"
order-worker
order-worker-sa
order-worker-hpa
```

Untuk canary:

```text id="wtapzp"
order-api-stable
order-api-canary
```

Jangan buat nama terlalu generic:

```text id="w71z3m"
api
backend
service
app
```

Nanti sulit dicari di logs, metrics, traces, RBAC, NetworkPolicy.

---

# 23. Label Strategy

Gunakan recommended labels:

```yaml id="uppi3x"
metadata:
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
```

Kubernetes recommended labels membuat object bisa dikelola dan di-query oleh tooling secara konsisten; `app.kubernetes.io/version` dapat berisi semantic version atau Git revision. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/overview/working-with-objects/common-labels/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Prinsip selector:

```text id="c5yf5l"
Selector Service dan Deployment jangan memasukkan version
kecuali memang sengaja memisahkan traffic per version.
```

Baik:

```yaml id="eqhjj6"
selector:
  matchLabels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: order-api
```

Hati-hati:

```yaml id="ch9qfs"
selector:
  matchLabels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
```

Jika version berubah, selector bisa tidak match atau Deployment selector immutable issue.

---

# 24. ConfigMap Strategy

ConfigMap cocok untuk non-sensitive config:

```text id="mct62u"
APP_PROFILE
LOG_LEVEL
HTTP_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
CAMUNDA_BASE_URL
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
FEATURE_FLAG_X_ENABLED
```

Kubernetes ConfigMap membantu memisahkan config artifact dari image agar containerized application tetap portable antar local, test, dan production workload. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-pod-configmap/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="oqiueb"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: order-api-config
data:
  APP_PROFILE: "prod"
  LOG_LEVEL: "INFO"
  DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE: "10"
  ORDER_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS: "3000"
  CAMUNDA_BASE_URL: "http://camunda-engine.camunda-prod.svc.cluster.local"
```

Deployment:

```yaml id="aryfd0"
envFrom:
  - configMapRef:
      name: order-api-config
```

Untuk production critical config, explicit `env` lebih audit-friendly daripada `envFrom`:

```yaml id="k0ymwx"
env:
  - name: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
    valueFrom:
      configMapKeyRef:
        name: order-api-config
        key: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
```

---

# 25. Secret Strategy

Secret cocok untuk sensitive config:

```text id="vuq0k2"
DB_PASSWORD
OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET
JWT_SIGNING_KEY
KAFKA_SASL_PASSWORD
REDIS_PASSWORD
TLS private key
```

Kubernetes Secret menyimpan sensitive data seperti password, token, atau key agar tidak perlu dimasukkan ke application code atau image. Namun good practices Kubernetes juga menyatakan Secret data di etcd tidak terenkripsi secara default sehingga encryption at rest dan RBAC harus diperhatikan. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Prinsip:

```text id="t2g2ay"
Jangan commit plain Secret ke Git.
Jangan bake Secret ke image.
Jangan print Secret di logs.
Batasi RBAC get/list/watch Secret.
Gunakan External Secrets/Sealed Secrets/Vault/cloud secret manager jika platform menyediakan.
```

Deployment reference:

```yaml id="tgq2ft"
env:
  - name: DB_PASSWORD
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: order-api-secret
        key: DB_PASSWORD
```

Untuk GitOps, Secret handling harus diputuskan oleh platform:

```text id="odx83z"
Option A:
  Git stores encrypted secret manifest.

Option B:
  Git stores ExternalSecret reference; real secret lives in Vault/AWS/Azure/etc.

Option C:
  Secret provisioned outside GitOps by platform.
```

---

# 26. Config Update dan Rollout

Perubahan ConfigMap/Secret tidak selalu otomatis membuat Pod restart. Untuk behavior-impacting config, cara aman adalah rollout restart:

```bash id="tzn6a7"
kubectl rollout restart deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Dalam GitOps, biasanya perubahan config memicu sync, tetapi Pod restart tetap harus didesain eksplisit. Beberapa tim memakai checksum annotation agar perubahan ConfigMap/Secret menyebabkan Pod template berubah.

Helm pattern:

```yaml id="94b1os"
metadata:
  annotations:
    checksum/config: {{ include (print $.Template.BasePath "/configmap.yaml") . | sha256sum }}
```

Makna:

```text id="ujcpxo"
Jika configmap template berubah,
annotation Pod template berubah,
Deployment membuat rollout baru.
```

Kustomize sering memakai `configMapGenerator` yang menghasilkan nama dengan hash. Namun untuk aplikasi yang mengacu nama ConfigMap stabil, pilih pattern dengan hati-hati agar rollout dan reference tetap benar.

---

# 27. Config Drift dan Environment Drift

Environment drift terjadi saat:

```text id="jyavhi"
dev punya readiness path berbeda dari prod
staging punya resource limit berbeda ekstrem
prod punya NetworkPolicy yang tidak pernah diuji
config key prod tidak ada di staging
secret name berbeda tanpa dokumentasi
```

Cara mengurangi drift:

```text id="4jw7l2"
base manifest sama
overlay hanya patch yang diperlukan
gunakan schema values
review rendered manifest
test staging dengan policy mendekati prod
jangan maintain file duplikat penuh
```

---

# 28. CI/CD Validation Pipeline

Minimal pipeline untuk manifest:

```text id="6mqwko"
1. Render
2. Lint
3. Validate Kubernetes schema
4. Policy check
5. Diff
6. Apply/sync
7. Watch rollout
8. Verify metrics/health
```

Kustomize:

```bash id="3qs3yd"
kubectl kustomize deploy/overlays/prod > rendered.yaml
```

Helm:

```bash id="cedklv"
helm lint ./order-api-chart

helm template order-api ./order-api-chart \
  -n order-prod \
  -f values-prod.yaml \
  > rendered.yaml
```

Kubernetes server-side dry run:

```bash id="72d5ki"
kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f rendered.yaml
```

Diff:

```bash id="mahnf7"
kubectl diff -f rendered.yaml
```

Apply:

```bash id="ubtc4a"
kubectl apply -f rendered.yaml
```

Atau direct:

```bash id="k4s4ri"
kubectl apply -k deploy/overlays/prod
```

---

# 29. Helm Validation Commands

```bash id="8g55h3"
helm lint ./order-api-chart
```

```bash id="f4zn6t"
helm template order-api ./order-api-chart \
  -n order-prod \
  -f values-prod.yaml
```

```bash id="f3fekw"
helm upgrade --install order-api ./order-api-chart \
  -n order-prod \
  -f values-prod.yaml \
  --dry-run
```

```bash id="xpi1pt"
helm get values order-api -n order-prod
```

```bash id="y8s9fg"
helm get manifest order-api -n order-prod
```

```bash id="w7jxxz"
helm history order-api -n order-prod
```

Helm chart best practices are documented by the Helm project, including guidance around chart structure, values, templates, dependencies, labels/annotations, Pods, RBAC, and CRDs. ([Helm](https://helm.sh/docs/chart_best_practices/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 30. GitOps Flow Example

```text id="dhe2sb"
Developer merges app change
  │
  ▼
CI builds image:
  registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
  │
  ▼
CI opens PR to GitOps repo:
  image tag from 1.8.3 → 1.9.0-a1b2c3d
  │
  ▼
Reviewer checks:
  rendered diff
  DB migration notes
  config changes
  resource/HPA impact
  security changes
  │
  ▼
Merge PR
  │
  ▼
GitOps controller syncs cluster
  │
  ▼
Rollout monitored
```

Keuntungan:

```text id="jcw8b6"
audit lewat Git
approval jelas
rollout changes visible
drift detection
rollback via Git revert
```

---

# 31. App Repo vs GitOps Repo

## Single repo pattern

```text id="4you4v"
order-api/
├── src/
├── pom.xml
├── Dockerfile
└── deploy/
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="vmi8td"
team kecil
service ownership kuat
simple CI/CD
```

## Separate GitOps repo pattern

```text id="khx8dk"
order-api repo:
  code + image build + chart/base

environment repo:
  desired state per cluster/env
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="42yej5"
prod approval terpisah
platform team mengontrol cluster state
banyak service/cluster
audit environment kuat
```

Tidak ada satu jawaban universal. Pilih sesuai operating model perusahaan.

---

# 32. Java/Jersey Standard Manifest Modules

Untuk service Java/Jersey production, minimal modules:

```text id="uslbzt"
Deployment
Service
ServiceAccount
ConfigMap
Secret reference
HPA
NetworkPolicy
PodDisruptionBudget
Ingress/Gateway/HTTPRoute if exposed
ServiceMonitor/PodMonitor if Prometheus Operator
```

Deployment harus memuat:

```text id="9s36k9"
probes
resources
securityContext
env/config
graceful shutdown settings
labels
image tag
logging/otel metadata
```

---

# 33. Standard Values untuk Java Service Chart

Jika platform membuat Helm chart internal, values sebaiknya mencakup:

```yaml id="a7qvq7"
image:
  repository: ""
  tag: ""
  digest: ""
  pullPolicy: IfNotPresent

app:
  name: ""
  component: api
  partOf: ""
  profile: ""
  version: ""

service:
  enabled: true
  port: 80
  targetPort: 8080

probes:
  startup:
    enabled: true
    path: /health/live
  liveness:
    enabled: true
    path: /health/live
  readiness:
    enabled: true
    path: /health/ready

resources: {}

hpa:
  enabled: false

serviceAccount:
  create: true
  automountToken: false

securityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 10001
  runAsGroup: 10001
  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true

networkPolicy:
  enabled: true

observability:
  otel:
    enabled: true
  metrics:
    enabled: true
```

Jangan memasukkan terlalu banyak business config ke chart jika itu membuat chart menjadi domain-specific.

---

# 34. Manifest Ownership

Pisahkan ownership:

| Manifest Area | Typical Owner |
|---|---|
| Deployment image/version | App team + release process |
| Resource requests/limits | App team + platform review |
| HPA | App team + platform/SRE |
| NetworkPolicy | App team + security/platform |
| Ingress/Gateway | App team + platform/network |
| RBAC/ServiceAccount | App team + platform/security |
| Secret provider | Platform/security |
| ConfigMap app config | App team |
| Observability scrape | App team + observability platform |

Senior engineer harus memastikan manifest change tidak hanya “lolos apply”, tetapi juga sesuai ownership dan governance.

---

# 35. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Copy-Paste Full YAML per Environment

Buruk:

```text id="ixbmsl"
deployment-dev.yaml
deployment-staging.yaml
deployment-prod.yaml
```

dengan 95% isi sama.

Lebih baik:

```text id="ehoaqi"
base/
overlays/dev
overlays/staging
overlays/prod
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Helm Template Terlalu Pintar

Buruk:

```text id="bltv6r"
satu template punya 15 if/else nested
values.yaml 800 baris
rendered manifest sulit ditebak
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="xbpl6k"
template sederhana
values schema jelas
render output selalu direview
pecah chart jika concern terlalu banyak
```

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Secret Plain di Git

Buruk:

```yaml id="46vqvc"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: order-api-secret
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: "prod-password"
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="yj5uu8"
encrypted secret
external secret reference
secret provisioned by platform
```

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - `latest` Image Tag

Buruk:

```yaml id="8mfyh2"
image: registry.example.com/order-api:latest
```

Lebih baik:

```yaml id="w4rmhp"
image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
```

atau digest.

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Selector Memakai Version Label

Buruk:

```yaml id="hutfla"
selector:
  matchLabels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0"
```

Service bisa kehilangan backend saat version berubah. Gunakan stable selector, version sebagai metadata observability.

---

## Anti-pattern 6 - Manual `kubectl edit` di Prod

Buruk:

```text id="b3gojj"
kubectl edit deploy order-api -n order-prod
```

untuk perubahan permanen.

Risiko:

```text id="4ssqqd"
drift dari Git
tidak ada PR review
GitOps controller bisa revert
audit lemah
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="oau3mv"
ubah manifest di Git
PR review
sync/deploy via pipeline/GitOps
```

---

# 36. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Tooling | Raw YAML, Kustomize, Helm, atau GitOps? Kenapa? |
| Render | Apakah hasil akhir manifest sudah dirender dan direview? |
| Environment | Apakah dev/staging/prod beda hanya di overlay/values yang jelas? |
| Image | Apakah image tag immutable atau digest? |
| Labels | Apakah recommended labels konsisten? |
| Selector | Apakah selector stabil dan tidak memasukkan version sembarangan? |
| Config | Apakah ConfigMap tidak berisi secret? |
| Secret | Apakah Secret tidak plain di Git? |
| Resources | Apakah requests/limits berbeda env secara sadar? |
| HPA | Apakah maxReplicas sesuai DB/downstream capacity? |
| NetworkPolicy | Apakah policy tersedia dan diuji di staging? |
| SecurityContext | Apakah hardening tidak hilang di overlay? |
| Observability | Apakah metrics/logs/traces metadata ada? |
| GitOps | Apakah cluster state bisa direkonstruksi dari Git? |
| Drift | Apakah perubahan manual bisa terdeteksi/dicegah? |
| Rollback | Apakah rollback cukup dengan Git revert atau Helm rollback? |
| Ownership | Apakah area platform/security/app jelas? |

---

# 37. Recommended Roadmap Praktis

```text id="9ew85g"
1. Mulai dari raw YAML yang bersih dan benar.
2. Tambahkan label/naming standard.
3. Pisahkan base dan environment dengan Kustomize.
4. Render manifest di CI.
5. Tambahkan validation/diff sebelum apply.
6. Jika banyak service serupa, ekstrak standard Helm chart.
7. Tentukan secret strategy: external/encrypted/platform-managed.
8. Pindahkan deployment desired state ke GitOps jika platform mendukung.
9. Tambahkan policy checks untuk RBAC, securityContext, resources, NetworkPolicy.
10. Dokumentasikan ownership dan release workflow.
```

---

# 38. Ringkasan Seri 20

```text id="5lrqgx"
Raw YAML:
  explicit, bagus untuk awal, tapi rawan copy-paste drift.

Kustomize:
  base + overlays, cocok untuk environment differences.

Helm:
  chart + templates + values, cocok untuk reusable packaging.

GitOps:
  Git sebagai source of truth, controller melakukan reconciliation.

ConfigMap:
  non-sensitive runtime configuration.

Secret:
  sensitive data; jangan plain di Git.

Labels:
  standard metadata untuk selector, observability, tooling.

Rendered manifest:
  hasil akhir yang harus direview, bukan hanya template source.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice:

```text id="9f5k15"
Manifest harus mengontrol:
Deployment, Service, ServiceAccount, ConfigMap, Secret reference,
HPA, NetworkPolicy, probes, resources, securityContext,
observability metadata, dan exposure via Ingress/Gateway.

Perbedaan dev/staging/prod harus eksplisit melalui values/overlays,
bukan file copy-paste.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="d0ixtx"
Manifest management yang baik membuat perubahan production:
kecil, reviewable, reproducible, auditable, dan rollback-aware.
Jika cluster state tidak bisa dijelaskan dari Git/manifest,
operasional Kubernetes akan menjadi debugging manual yang mahal.
```
