## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:29:15 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 21/24  
## CI/CD dengan GitHub Actions, Maven, Docker Registry, Kubernetes Deploy/GitOps, Image Tagging, Manifest Validation, Rollout Monitoring, dan Release Governance untuk Java/Jersey Microservice

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana membangun pipeline CI/CD yang aman dan reproducible untuk Java 17+ Jersey/JAX-RS microservice yang dideploy ke Kubernetes?**

GitHub Actions adalah platform workflow automation di GitHub untuk build, test, release, dan deployment. Workflow didefinisikan di repository, biasanya sebagai file YAML di `.github/workflows`. GitHub juga menyediakan panduan resmi untuk build dan test project Java dengan Maven di GitHub Actions. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/actions?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model CI/CD

```text id="6az41n"
Source Code
│
├── Pull Request CI
│   ├── checkout
│   ├── setup Java
│   ├── Maven test
│   ├── static checks
│   ├── security scan
│   ├── contract test
│   └── build artifact
│
├── Main Branch Build
│   ├── Maven package
│   ├── Docker build
│   ├── image scan
│   ├── push image to registry
│   └── produce immutable tag/digest
│
├── Manifest Update
│   ├── Kustomize image tag update
│   ├── Helm values update
│   ├── GitOps PR
│   └── manifest validation/diff
│
└── Deployment
    ├── GitOps sync or kubectl/helm apply
    ├── rollout status
    ├── smoke test
    ├── metrics/logs/traces check
    └── rollback if needed
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="srbp73"
CI/CD bukan hanya “otomatis deploy”.
CI/CD adalah release control system:
build reproducible, artifact immutable, security checked,
manifest validated, deployment observable, dan rollback path jelas.
```

---

# 2. Diagram End-to-End Pipeline

```text id="g8c2t2"
Developer PR
  │
  ▼
GitHub Actions: pull_request
  ├── mvn test
  ├── unit/integration tests
  ├── OpenAPI contract check
  ├── Dockerfile lint/security scan
  └── manifest render validation
  │
  ▼
Merge to main
  │
  ▼
GitHub Actions: main/release
  ├── mvn package
  ├── build image
  ├── tag image: 1.9.0-a1b2c3d
  ├── push registry
  ├── scan image
  └── publish artifact metadata
  │
  ▼
GitOps PR / CD step
  ├── update image tag/digest in overlay/values
  ├── render manifest
  ├── validate policy
  └── merge/sync
  │
  ▼
Kubernetes
  ├── Deployment rollout
  ├── readiness gates traffic
  ├── old Pods drain
  └── rollout monitored
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **GitHub Actions Workflow** | Trigger, jobs, steps, runners, permissions, artifacts. | CI/CD utama dari repository Java. | Pipeline sulit diprediksi dan tidak aman. |
| 2 | **Maven Build** | `mvn test`, `package`, `verify`, profiles, dependency cache. | Build Java 17+ Jersey app. | Artifact tidak reproducible. |
| 3 | **Maven Wrapper** | `./mvnw` agar versi Maven konsisten. Apache Maven Wrapper membantu memastikan user/build environment punya Maven yang diperlukan untuk menjalankan build. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/tools/wrapper/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | CI tidak tergantung Maven preinstalled. | Build beda antar machine. |
| 4 | **setup-java** | Setup JDK dan cache Maven dependency. `actions/setup-java` mendukung caching dependency Maven/Gradle/SBT. ([GitHub](https://github.com/actions/setup-java?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Build lebih cepat dan konsisten. | CI lambat dan flaky. |
| 5 | **Docker Build** | Multi-stage build, BuildKit/buildx, image metadata, cache. | Membuat image runtime Java. | Image besar/tidak aman/tidak reproducible. |
| 6 | **Registry Push** | Push ke GHCR/ECR/ACR/Harbor/internal registry. GitHub Container Registry menyimpan container image dan dapat dikaitkan dengan repository atau permission granular. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/packages/working-with-a-github-packages-registry/working-with-the-container-registry?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Artifact deployable. | Image tidak bisa di-pull cluster. |
| 7 | **Image Tagging** | Version + commit SHA + digest. | Traceability rollout. | `latest` membuat rollback/debug sulit. |
| 8 | **Manifest Validation** | Render Helm/Kustomize, dry-run, policy check. | Cegah YAML buruk masuk cluster. | Error baru muncul saat deploy. |
| 9 | **GitOps Flow** | CI update desired state, controller deploy. | Audit production release. | Manual drift. |
| 10 | **Direct Deploy Flow** | `kubectl apply`, `helm upgrade`, rollout watch. | Masih umum di beberapa org. | Credential dan permission CI harus dijaga ketat. |
| 11 | **OIDC for Cloud Auth** | Short-lived credential ke AWS/Azure/Vault. GitHub menyatakan OIDC memungkinkan workflow mengakses cloud provider tanpa menyimpan credential long-lived sebagai secret. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/actions/security-for-github-actions/security-hardening-your-deployments/configuring-openid-connect-in-cloud-providers?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Lebih aman untuk deploy cloud/on-prem hybrid. | Long-lived secrets bocor. |
| 12 | **GITHUB_TOKEN Permissions** | Least privilege per workflow/job. GitHub merekomendasikan default `GITHUB_TOKEN` read-only lalu menaikkan permission hanya jika dibutuhkan. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/reference/security/secure-use?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Supply-chain hardening. | Workflow terlalu privileged. |
| 13 | **Rollout Monitoring** | `kubectl rollout status`, metrics/logs/traces. Kubernetes `rollout status` menunggu status rollout terbaru hingga selesai secara default. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_rollout/kubectl_rollout_status/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Deployment failure cepat terdeteksi. | Deploy gagal tapi pipeline hijau. |
| 14 | **Release Governance** | Environment approval, branch protection, change record. | Production safety. | Deploy tidak audit-able. |
| 15 | **Rollback Strategy** | Git revert, Helm rollback, Kubernetes rollout undo, image pinning. | Recovery incident. | Rollback tidak bisa dilakukan karena artifact/config tidak jelas. |

---

# 4. Pipeline Stage yang Direkomendasikan

## Pull Request CI

Tujuan: memastikan perubahan aman sebelum merge.

```text id="errx89"
PR pipeline:
  compile
  unit test
  integration test
  static analysis
  formatting/checkstyle if used
  dependency/security scan
  OpenAPI compatibility check
  Docker build test
  manifest render validation
```

## Main Branch Build

Tujuan: menghasilkan artifact immutable.

```text id="58yvnl"
main pipeline:
  package jar
  build container image
  tag image with version + SHA
  push image
  scan image
  publish build metadata
```

## Release / Deploy

Tujuan: mengubah desired state secara controlled.

```text id="0ai0e4"
release pipeline:
  update GitOps repo or run deploy command
  validate rendered manifest
  wait rollout
  smoke test
  monitor SLI
```

---

# 5. Maven CI Workflow untuk PR

Contoh `.github/workflows/ci.yml`:

```yaml id="rjgp7f"
name: ci

on:
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  test:
    name: Maven test
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - name: Build and test
        run: ./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress verify
```

Catatan:

```text id="2zwjxp"
permissions: contents: read
  default aman untuk PR test.

./mvnw
  lebih reproducible daripada bergantung pada Maven runner.

--batch-mode
  cocok untuk CI.

--no-transfer-progress
  log lebih bersih.
```

GitHub memiliki panduan resmi untuk build/test Java dengan Maven di Actions, termasuk workflow Maven dan dependency caching. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/actions/guides/building-and-testing-java-with-maven?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Maven Lifecycle yang Umum

Untuk Java/Jersey service:

| Command | Kapan Dipakai | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `./mvnw test` | Unit test cepat. | PR fast feedback. |
| `./mvnw verify` | Full verification. | Bisa mencakup integration test. |
| `./mvnw package` | Build JAR/WAR. | Artifact untuk image. |
| `./mvnw -DskipTests package` | Build cepat setelah test stage terpisah. | Jangan skip test tanpa stage test sebelumnya. |
| `./mvnw dependency:go-offline` | Warm dependency cache dalam Docker build. | Berguna di multi-stage Dockerfile. |

Untuk CI utama, baseline aman:

```bash id="pboigj"
./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress verify
```

---

# 7. Test Pyramid untuk Jersey/JAX-RS Service

Minimal PR gate:

```text id="bh37ua"
Unit tests:
  domain logic, validation, mapper helper

Resource tests:
  Jersey resource/controller behavior

Integration tests:
  PostgreSQL/MyBatis using Testcontainers or test DB
  HTTP client contract tests
  Camunda worker handler tests if applicable

Contract tests:
  OpenAPI backward compatibility
  generated client compatibility
```

Jangan hanya test happy path. Untuk microservice enterprise, test ini penting:

```text id="vhapnn"
DB timeout / connection failure
downstream timeout
invalid request
authorization denied
duplicate request/idempotency
migration compatibility
Camunda retry/failure path
```

---

# 8. Build Container Image

Jika memakai Docker official build action, `docker/build-push-action` adalah GitHub Action untuk build dan push Docker image dengan Buildx/BuildKit, termasuk dukungan multi-platform, secrets, dan remote cache. ([GitHub](https://github.com/docker/build-push-action?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh build-push ke GHCR:

```yaml id="qhpo29"
name: build-image

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

permissions:
  contents: read
  packages: write

env:
  IMAGE_NAME: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/order-api

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - name: Test
        run: ./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress verify

      - name: Log in to GHCR
        uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Docker metadata
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@v5
        with:
          images: ${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}
          tags: |
            type=sha,prefix=
            type=ref,event=branch

      - name: Build and push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          push: true
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
```

Catatan versi action seperti `@v4`, `@v5`, `@v6` harus mengikuti standard perusahaan. Untuk supply-chain hardening tinggi, pertimbangkan pin ke commit SHA, bukan hanya major tag.

---

# 9. Image Tagging Strategy

Hindari:

```text id="nsmed3"
latest
prod
stable
```

Gunakan:

```text id="cphaxg"
1.9.0
1.9.0-a1b2c3d
a1b2c3d
sha256 digest
```

Recommended:

```text id="26ndkx"
human-readable tag:
  order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

immutable deploy reference:
  order-api@sha256:...
```

Kenapa digest penting?

```text id="um6cdj"
Tag bisa dipindah oleh manusia/proses.
Digest menunjuk content image spesifik.
```

Minimal yang harus tersimpan sebagai build metadata:

```text id="pl428o"
git commit SHA
image tag
image digest
build timestamp
Maven artifact version
Dockerfile version
SBOM/scan result if used
```

---

# 10. Dockerfile Baseline untuk CI/CD

```dockerfile id="52fjs4"
FROM maven:3.9-eclipse-temurin-17 AS build

WORKDIR /workspace

COPY pom.xml .
COPY .mvn .mvn
COPY mvnw .
RUN ./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress dependency:go-offline

COPY src src
RUN ./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress package -DskipTests

FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

RUN groupadd --gid 10001 appgroup \
    && useradd --uid 10001 --gid 10001 --system --home-dir /app appuser

COPY --from=build /workspace/target/order-api.jar /app/app.jar

RUN chown -R 10001:10001 /app

USER 10001:10001

EXPOSE 8080

ENV JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS="-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70 -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError"

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

CI sebaiknya sudah menjalankan `verify` sebelum image build. Docker build stage bisa `-DskipTests` untuk tidak mengulang test berat, tetapi hanya jika test stage wajib lulus sebelumnya.

---

# 11. Registry Strategy

Registry umum:

```text id="fnbayp"
GHCR
AWS ECR
Azure Container Registry
Harbor/internal registry
Artifactory/Nexus registry
```

GitHub Container Registry menyimpan container images dalam akun personal/organization dan dapat dikaitkan dengan repository atau memakai permission granular. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/packages/working-with-a-github-packages-registry/working-with-the-container-registry?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pertanyaan review:

```text id="mpg0rr"
Cluster bisa pull image dari registry?
ImagePullSecret diperlukan?
Registry private atau public?
Siapa boleh push?
Siapa boleh pull?
Retention policy bagaimana?
Vulnerability scanning di mana?
Promotion antar environment pakai rebuild atau same image?
```

Senior-level rule:

```text id="hggv1y"
Build once, promote same artifact.
Jangan rebuild image berbeda untuk staging dan prod dari commit yang sama.
```

---

# 12. GitHub Actions Security Baseline

Minimal:

```yaml id="jx687u"
permissions:
  contents: read
```

Jika perlu push package:

```yaml id="m1zbyq"
permissions:
  contents: read
  packages: write
```

Jika perlu OIDC cloud deploy:

```yaml id="8yhks8"
permissions:
  contents: read
  id-token: write
```

GitHub security guidance menyarankan `GITHUB_TOKEN` diberi minimum required permissions; default yang baik adalah read-only untuk repository contents, lalu permission dinaikkan hanya pada job yang perlu. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/reference/security/secure-use?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Anti-pattern:

```yaml id="a31qmd"
permissions: write-all
```

kecuali ada justifikasi sangat kuat.

---

# 13. OIDC untuk AWS/Azure/Cloud Deploy

Jangan simpan long-lived cloud access key di GitHub Secrets jika platform mendukung OIDC.

GitHub menjelaskan OIDC memungkinkan GitHub Actions workflow mengakses cloud provider tanpa menyimpan credential long-lived, karena workflow dapat memperoleh short-lived token dari provider yang mempercayai GitHub OIDC provider. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/concepts/security/openid-connect?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Flow:

```text id="he9lpw"
GitHub Actions job
  │
  ├── requests OIDC token from GitHub
  │
  ├── cloud provider validates token claims
  │
  ├── cloud provider issues short-lived credential
  │
  └── job uses short-lived credential for deploy/push/read secret
```

Permission workflow:

```yaml id="v0rpo3"
permissions:
  contents: read
  id-token: write
```

Guardrail:

```text id="5gx2dn"
restrict by repository
restrict by branch/environment
restrict by workflow
restrict by audience/subject claims
use environment protection for prod
```

---

# 14. Build vs Deploy Workflow Separation

Jangan semua hal dalam satu workflow tanpa boundary.

Recommended:

```text id="l6yvcl"
ci.yml:
  PR validation only

build-image.yml:
  main branch image build and push

deploy-dev.yml:
  auto deploy dev

promote-staging.yml:
  environment approval or manual dispatch

promote-prod.yml:
  protected environment approval + GitOps PR/sync
```

Kenapa?

```text id="1bi3mr"
least privilege per workflow/job
auditable release stage
prod deploy tidak otomatis dari setiap push jika governance butuh approval
lebih mudah rollback/promotion
```

---

# 15. Direct Deploy vs GitOps Deploy

## Direct deploy

CI/CD langsung menjalankan:

```bash id="tsjg2s"
kubectl apply -k deploy/overlays/prod
```

atau:

```bash id="cy2lfb"
helm upgrade --install order-api ./chart -n order-prod -f values-prod.yaml
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="pk2oyg"
simple
cepat
mudah untuk dev/staging
```

Risiko:

```text id="rjs500"
credential Kubernetes berada di CI
manual drift lebih sulit dikontrol
audit desired state bisa tersebar
rollback perlu hati-hati
```

## GitOps deploy

CI/CD membuka PR/update GitOps repo:

```text id="ah664s"
image tag/digest updated in Git
GitOps controller syncs cluster
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="teyzka"
Git source of truth
audit via PR
drift detection
rollback via Git revert
CI tidak perlu broad cluster credentials
```

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="x9tjh7"
dev:
  direct deploy boleh jika organisasi mengizinkan

staging/prod:
  GitOps lebih defensible untuk enterprise
```

---

# 16. Manifest Validation di Pipeline

Untuk Kustomize:

```yaml id="wjsyx2"
- name: Render prod manifest
  run: kubectl kustomize deploy/overlays/prod > rendered.yaml

- name: Server-side dry run
  run: kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f rendered.yaml
```

Untuk Helm:

```yaml id="dluzyi"
- name: Helm lint
  run: helm lint ./chart

- name: Helm template
  run: |
    helm template order-api ./chart \
      -n order-prod \
      -f values-prod.yaml \
      > rendered.yaml

- name: Server-side dry run
  run: kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f rendered.yaml
```

Tambahkan policy checks:

```text id="wlpk95"
no latest image
resources set
securityContext set
runAsNonRoot true
readOnlyRootFilesystem true if required
automountServiceAccountToken false unless needed
no cluster-admin
NetworkPolicy exists
probes exist
```

---

# 17. GitOps PR Update Example

Flow:

```text id="qmksfe"
build-image workflow
  → builds order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
  → opens PR to platform-gitops:
      overlays/prod/kustomization.yaml image tag updated
```

Example Kustomize image update:

```yaml id="5bgg7m"
images:
  - name: registry.example.com/order-api
    newTag: 1.9.0-a1b2c3d
```

PR description should include:

```text id="ypau9s"
service: order-api
old version: 1.8.3
new version: 1.9.0-a1b2c3d
image digest: sha256:...
commit: a1b2c3d
DB migration: yes/no
OpenAPI breaking change: yes/no
rollback note: ...
dashboard: ...
```

---

# 18. Example GitHub Actions: Build Image + Create GitOps PR

Konseptual:

```yaml id="z48tm8"
name: build-and-propose-deploy

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

permissions:
  contents: read
  packages: write
  pull-requests: write

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    outputs:
      image-tag: ${{ steps.vars.outputs.image_tag }}

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - id: vars
        run: |
          SHORT_SHA="${GITHUB_SHA::7}"
          echo "image_tag=1.9.0-${SHORT_SHA}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - run: ./mvnw --batch-mode --no-transfer-progress verify

      - uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          push: true
          tags: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/order-api:${{ steps.vars.outputs.image_tag }}

  propose-deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout GitOps repo
        uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          repository: your-org/platform-gitops
          token: ${{ secrets.GITOPS_PR_TOKEN }}
          path: platform-gitops

      - name: Update image tag
        working-directory: platform-gitops
        run: |
          # Prefer yq/kustomize edit in real pipeline.
          sed -i "s/newTag:.*/newTag: ${{ needs.build.outputs.image-tag }}/" \
            apps/order-api/prod/kustomization.yaml

      - name: Create PR
        working-directory: platform-gitops
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions"
          git config user.email "github-actions@users.noreply.github.com"
          git checkout -b promote-order-api-${{ needs.build.outputs.image-tag }}
          git add apps/order-api/prod/kustomization.yaml
          git commit -m "Promote order-api ${{ needs.build.outputs.image-tag }}"
          git push origin HEAD
          gh pr create \
            --title "Promote order-api ${{ needs.build.outputs.image-tag }}" \
            --body "Promote order-api image to ${{ needs.build.outputs.image-tag }}"
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITOPS_PR_TOKEN }}
```

Catatan security:

```text id="3jdoms"
GITOPS_PR_TOKEN harus minimal scope.
Lebih baik gunakan GitHub App atau OIDC/approved automation jika platform tersedia.
Jangan gunakan PAT personal luas untuk prod.
```

---

# 19. Direct Deploy Example dengan Kustomize

Untuk environment non-prod atau organisasi yang belum GitOps:

```yaml id="j2qt1t"
name: deploy-dev

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

permissions:
  contents: read
  id-token: write

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: dev

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Configure cluster credentials
        run: |
          echo "Configure kube credentials via OIDC/cloud provider/platform step"

      - name: Render manifest
        run: kubectl kustomize deploy/overlays/dev > rendered.yaml

      - name: Dry run
        run: kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f rendered.yaml

      - name: Apply
        run: kubectl apply -f rendered.yaml

      - name: Wait rollout
        run: kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-dev --timeout=5m
```

Kubernetes `kubectl rollout status` menonton status rollout terbaru hingga selesai secara default, dan bisa diberi timeout di workflow. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/kubectl/generated/kubectl_rollout/kubectl_rollout_status/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 20. Direct Deploy Example dengan Helm

```yaml id="zq377x"
name: deploy-staging

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  id-token: write

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: staging

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Configure cluster credentials
        run: |
          echo "Configure kube credentials securely"

      - name: Helm lint
        run: helm lint ./chart

      - name: Helm dry run
        run: |
          helm upgrade --install order-api ./chart \
            -n order-staging \
            -f values-staging.yaml \
            --dry-run

      - name: Helm upgrade
        run: |
          helm upgrade --install order-api ./chart \
            -n order-staging \
            -f values-staging.yaml \
            --atomic \
            --timeout 5m

      - name: Rollout status
        run: kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-staging --timeout=5m
```

`--atomic` bisa membantu rollback Helm release jika upgrade gagal, tetapi tetap harus dipahami bersama DB migration compatibility. Helm rollback aplikasi tidak otomatis mengembalikan schema/data.

---

# 21. Environment Protection

Untuk production:

```text id="5nfdw6"
GitHub Environment: production
  required reviewers
  deployment branches/tags restriction
  secrets scoped to environment
  audit trail
```

Workflow:

```yaml id="b1djpw"
jobs:
  deploy-prod:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: production
```

Tujuannya:

```text id="l3vetb"
prod deploy tidak bisa dilakukan sembarang branch
ada approval manual jika diperlukan
secret prod tidak tersedia untuk workflow non-prod
```

---

# 22. Branch and Tag Strategy

Contoh sederhana:

```text id="3d3ddr"
pull_request to main:
  CI only

push to main:
  build image and deploy dev/staging

tag v1.9.0:
  propose production release
```

Alternative:

```text id="80daqu"
release branch:
  release/1.9

manual workflow_dispatch:
  promote image digest to environment
```

Rule penting:

```text id="as7zw1"
Production deploy sebaiknya menunjuk artifact yang sudah dibuild,
bukan membuild ulang dari branch pada saat deploy.
```

---

# 23. Artifact Promotion

Bad:

```text id="fd05pd"
dev build image A
staging rebuild image B
prod rebuild image C
```

Good:

```text id="vr58oi"
build once:
  image digest sha256:abc

promote same digest:
  dev → staging → prod
```

Manfaat:

```text id="toht9e"
yang dites di staging sama dengan yang jalan di prod
rollback jelas
supply-chain audit lebih baik
```

---

# 24. SBOM dan Image Scan

Untuk enterprise, tambahkan:

```text id="h1swzv"
dependency vulnerability scan
container image vulnerability scan
SBOM generation
license check
secret scanning
Dockerfile lint
```

Pipeline gate harus realistis:

```text id="921onq"
Critical exploitable vuln:
  block release

Low severity in unused package:
  ticket/fix later depending policy

False positive:
  allow with documented exception
```

Jangan membuat scanner hanya dekoratif; harus ada policy.

---

# 25. OpenAPI Contract Check

Karena user memakai Jersey/JAX-RS dan contract-first approach di beberapa area, tambahkan contract gate:

```text id="q216r2"
Generate OpenAPI
Compare against previous published contract
Fail PR if breaking change unless explicitly approved
Generate client/server stubs if applicable
Validate examples/schema
```

Breaking change examples:

```text id="q02o07"
remove endpoint
remove response field
change field type
make optional request field required
change status code semantics
change enum values incompatibly
```

Contract check sangat penting untuk rolling update karena old/new version bisa coexist dan consumer mungkin deploy tidak bersamaan.

---

# 26. Database Migration Gate

Jika ada Liquibase/Flyway migration:

```text id="v7gxnw"
CI should validate migration syntax
integration test applies migration to empty DB
integration test applies migration to existing schema snapshot if possible
rollback notes reviewed
expand/contract check for destructive operations
```

Release PR harus menjawab:

```text id="vuvl6m"
Apakah migration backward-compatible?
Apakah old app masih jalan setelah migration?
Apakah rollback app aman?
Apakah contract phase dijadwalkan di release berikut?
```

Untuk MyBatis, tambahkan test mapper terhadap migrated schema.

---

# 27. Kubernetes Rollout Monitoring Step

Setelah deploy:

```bash id="eooz6n"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod --timeout=5m
```

Lalu smoke test:

```bash id="amx1dt"
kubectl run smoke-test \
  -n order-prod \
  --rm -i \
  --image=curlimages/curl:latest \
  --restart=Never \
  -- curl -fsS http://order-api.order-prod.svc.cluster.local/health/ready
```

Cek Pod:

```bash id="h3clu6"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

Cek events jika gagal:

```bash id="d87ysw"
kubectl get events -n order-prod --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

Pipeline tidak boleh hijau hanya karena `kubectl apply` sukses. Apply sukses hanya berarti desired state diterima API server, bukan rollout sukses.

---

# 28. Smoke Test Strategy

Minimal smoke test:

```text id="ct64nf"
health ready returns 200
simple read endpoint works
dependency critical reachable
metrics endpoint not public but scrapeable internally
```

Untuk order API:

```text id="s00uq2"
GET /health/ready
GET /orders/{known-test-id} in staging
POST /orders dry-run mode if supported
```

Production smoke test harus non-destructive atau memakai dedicated test tenant/data jika governance mengizinkan.

---

# 29. Release Governance Checklist dalam PR

Setiap release PR idealnya berisi:

```text id="say7ew"
Service:
  order-api

Image:
  registry/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
  digest: sha256:...

Change summary:
  ...

Risk:
  low/medium/high

DB migration:
  yes/no
  backward-compatible: yes/no

OpenAPI breaking change:
  yes/no

Config/Secret change:
  yes/no

HPA/resource impact:
  ...

Rollback:
  safe/unsafe
  rollback command or Git revert note

Observability:
  dashboard/log query/trace query

Approvers:
  app owner, platform/security/DB if needed
```

---

# 30. Rollback Patterns

## GitOps rollback

```text id="y6vj0q"
revert GitOps PR
GitOps controller syncs previous desired state
```

## Helm rollback

```bash id="0rjjbc"
helm history order-api -n order-prod
helm rollback order-api <revision> -n order-prod
```

## Kubernetes rollout undo

```bash id="40ijsp"
kubectl rollout undo deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Kubernetes docs describe Deployment rollout and rollback as part of Deployment management, and `kubectl rollout undo` rolls back to a previous rollout or selected revision. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rollback safety conditions:

```text id="a871wb"
schema still compatible
config still compatible
secret still compatible
message/workflow format still compatible
old image still available in registry
```

---

# 31. Pipeline Failure Mode Table

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Cara Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| PR CI lambat | Maven dependency download, integration tests berat | setup-java cache, test split |
| Maven build beda lokal/CI | Maven/JDK version beda | Maven Wrapper + setup-java |
| Image build sukses tapi app crash | Test tidak menjalankan startup path | container smoke test |
| Push registry gagal | permission/token/registry config | package permission minimal |
| Deploy sukses tapi Pod ImagePullBackOff | cluster pull secret/registry permission | validate imagePullSecret |
| Rollout stuck | readiness/config/probe/resource | rollout status + events/logs |
| Pipeline hijau tapi app down | tidak wait rollout/smoke test | wajib rollout status |
| Prod deploy pakai wrong value | values/overlay salah | render diff + env protection |
| Secret bocor | secret plain/log/printenv | secret strategy + masking |
| GitOps drift | manual kubectl edit | GitOps drift alerts |
| Rollback gagal | DB destructive migration | expand/contract gate |

---

# 32. GitHub Actions Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - `permissions: write-all`

Buruk:

```yaml id="bf7h2k"
permissions: write-all
```

Lebih baik:

```yaml id="xpbr2r"
permissions:
  contents: read
```

dan tambahkan hanya yang perlu, misalnya `packages: write`.

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Long-lived Cloud Secrets

Buruk:

```yaml id="vq88s2"
AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID }}
AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: ${{ secrets.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY }}
```

Lebih baik jika didukung:

```text id="mh48cj"
OIDC → short-lived cloud credential
```

GitHub OIDC memang dirancang untuk menghindari penyimpanan credential long-lived di workflow secrets. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/actions/security-for-github-actions/security-hardening-your-deployments/configuring-openid-connect-in-cloud-providers?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Deploy `latest`

Buruk:

```yaml id="coakht"
image: registry.example.com/order-api:latest
```

Lebih baik:

```yaml id="mt7fhp"
image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
```

atau digest.

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Apply tanpa Rollout Status

Buruk:

```bash id="cojkos"
kubectl apply -f deployment.yaml
```

lalu pipeline selesai.

Lebih baik:

```bash id="ppx9ag"
kubectl apply -f deployment.yaml
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod --timeout=5m
```

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Prod Deploy dari Pull Request Context

PR dari fork/untrusted context berbahaya untuk secret dan deploy.

Prinsip:

```text id="zayv3g"
PR workflow:
  validate only, no prod secrets, no deployment.

Protected main/tag/environment:
  release/deploy.
```

---

# 33. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Maven | Apakah pakai Maven Wrapper dan JDK eksplisit? |
| Tests | Apakah `verify` mencakup unit/integration/contract test? |
| Image | Apakah image build reproducible dan non-root? |
| Tagging | Apakah image tag immutable dan digest dicatat? |
| Registry | Apakah push/pull permission benar? |
| Security | Apakah token permissions least privilege? |
| OIDC | Apakah cloud deploy memakai OIDC jika tersedia? |
| Secrets | Apakah tidak ada long-lived/prod secret di PR workflow? |
| Manifest | Apakah Helm/Kustomize dirender dan divalidasi? |
| Policy | Apakah securityContext/resources/probes/NetworkPolicy dicek? |
| GitOps | Apakah prod desired state lewat PR/GitOps? |
| Rollout | Apakah pipeline menunggu rollout status? |
| Smoke | Apakah ada smoke test non-destructive? |
| Observability | Apakah dashboard/log/trace query tersedia setelah deploy? |
| DB | Apakah migration backward-compatible? |
| Rollback | Apakah rollback aman dan documented? |
| Governance | Apakah prod memakai environment protection/approval? |

---

# 34. Recommended Pipeline Blueprint

```text id="0lh5ve"
PR:
  checkout
  setup-java
  mvn verify
  contract check
  render manifest
  policy validation
  no deploy

Main:
  checkout
  setup-java
  mvn verify
  docker build
  image scan
  push immutable image
  publish digest

Dev deploy:
  update dev overlay or direct apply
  rollout status
  smoke test

Staging:
  promote same image digest
  run integration/smoke tests
  validate DB migration
  observe metrics

Prod:
  GitOps PR
  approval
  sync/deploy
  rollout status
  smoke test
  monitor SLI
  rollback if needed
```

---

# 35. Ringkasan Seri 21

```text id="v5plp9"
CI:
  memastikan code/build/test/contract/security valid sebelum merge.

CD:
  mempromosikan artifact immutable ke environment dengan kontrol.

GitHub Actions:
  workflow automation untuk build, test, package, publish, deploy.

Maven Wrapper:
  build Maven lebih reproducible.

Docker/Registry:
  image immutable sebagai deploy artifact.

GitOps:
  Git menjadi desired state production.

OIDC:
  short-lived credential untuk cloud deploy, mengurangi long-lived secrets.

Rollout monitoring:
  apply sukses belum berarti deploy sukses.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey + Kubernetes:

```text id="jlp4d6"
Pipeline yang baik harus:
  run Maven verify
  build image non-root
  tag image dengan version + SHA/digest
  scan dependencies/image
  render Helm/Kustomize
  validate manifest
  update GitOps desired state
  wait rollout status
  run smoke test
  monitor logs/metrics/traces
  support rollback
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="n7w5li"
CI/CD production-grade bukan tentang seberapa cepat deploy,
tetapi seberapa reproducible, secure, observable, auditable,
dan reversible release tersebut.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:45:55 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 22/24  
## PostgreSQL, MyBatis, Connection Pooling, Kubernetes Runtime Constraints, DB Connection Budget, Transaction Timeout, Migration Safety, dan Runtime Failure Mode untuk Java/Jersey Microservice

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana menjalankan Java/Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL secara aman di Kubernetes, terutama saat replica bertambah, rollout terjadi, HPA aktif, dan database punya connection limit?**

Di Kubernetes, aplikasi bisa scale horizontal dengan menambah Pod. Tetapi PostgreSQL tidak otomatis ikut “lega” hanya karena Pod bertambah. Justru sering terjadi sebaliknya: **scale-out aplikasi memperbesar jumlah koneksi, query concurrency, lock contention, dan pressure ke database**.

Kubernetes mendokumentasikan bahwa resource requests/limits di Pod menentukan kebutuhan CPU/memory container dan memengaruhi scheduling/runtime behavior. Namun DB capacity bukan resource Kubernetes native; Anda harus memodelkannya sendiri lewat pool size, replica count, HPA maxReplicas, dan rollout surge. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/manage-resources-containers/))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="41ixb1"
Java/Jersey Pod
│
├── HTTP server threads
├── Jersey resource layer
├── Service/domain layer
├── MyBatis mapper
├── JDBC DataSource
├── HikariCP connection pool
└── PostgreSQL connections
        │
        ▼
PostgreSQL
├── max_connections
├── active sessions
├── idle sessions
├── idle in transaction sessions
├── locks
├── transactions
├── query execution
├── WAL/checkpoint/autovacuum
└── CPU/memory/IO
```

Kubernetes scaling relationship:

```text id="j4hqix"
Total possible DB connections
=
number of running Pods
× max pool size per Pod
```

Saat rollout atau HPA, jumlah Pod bisa sementara atau permanen bertambah:

```text id="ghj5nr"
normal replicas:
  6 Pods

rolling update maxSurge:
  +2 Pods

HPA maxReplicas:
  12 Pods

effective worst-case:
  HPA maxReplicas + maxSurge
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="qz2iy8"
Connection pool bukan hanya setting aplikasi.
Connection pool adalah capacity contract antara Kubernetes workload dan PostgreSQL.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Runtime Path

```text id="14bw7x"
HTTP Request
  │
  ▼
Jersey Resource
  │
  ▼
Service Method
  │
  ▼
MyBatis Mapper
  │
  ▼
SqlSession / Executor
  │
  ▼
HikariCP DataSource
  │
  ├── acquire connection
  ├── wait if pool busy
  ├── timeout if pool exhausted
  └── return connection after close/commit/rollback
  │
  ▼
PostgreSQL backend session
  │
  ├── parse/bind/execute
  ├── lock wait if needed
  ├── transaction commit/rollback
  └── connection returned to pool
```

Failure bisa terjadi di banyak titik:

```text id="sc8z0p"
HTTP request stuck
  → waiting DB connection

Connection acquired
  → query slow

Query running
  → lock wait

Transaction open
  → idle in transaction

Pod terminating
  → connection not closed cleanly

HPA scale out
  → DB connection pressure
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Java/Jersey + MyBatis | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **DB Connection Budget** | Hitung `replicas × poolSize`, termasuk HPA dan rollout surge. | Menghindari PostgreSQL connection exhaustion. | Scale-out aplikasi membuat DB tumbang. |
| 2 | **HikariCP Configuration** | `maximumPoolSize`, `minimumIdle`, `connectionTimeout`, `idleTimeout`, `maxLifetime`. HikariCP mendokumentasikan `maximumPoolSize` sebagai ukuran maksimum pool termasuk koneksi idle dan in-use, dan `connectionTimeout` sebagai waktu maksimum menunggu koneksi dari pool. ([github.com](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/HikariCP)) | Runtime pool utama Java service. | Thread request menunggu connection terlalu lama. |
| 3 | **MyBatis SqlSession Lifecycle** | Session, mapper, commit/rollback/close. MyBatis menjelaskan setiap aplikasi MyBatis berpusat pada `SqlSessionFactory`, yang dibuat melalui `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder`. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html)) | Mencegah connection leak dan transaction leak. | Connection tidak kembali ke pool. |
| 4 | **Transaction Boundary** | Batas transaksi jelas, pendek, dan idempotent. | Critical untuk order/payment/workflow. | Lock lama, duplicate write, rollback sulit. |
| 5 | **PostgreSQL Timeouts** | `statement_timeout`, `lock_timeout`, `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout`, `transaction_timeout`. PostgreSQL docs menjelaskan `transaction_timeout` membatasi durasi transaksi dan default `0` berarti disabled. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html)) | Mencegah query/transaction menggantung. | Request/transaction menggantung lama. |
| 6 | **Lock Awareness** | Row/table locks, `SELECT FOR UPDATE`, deadlock, lock wait. | MyBatis SQL manual mudah menciptakan lock issue. | Latency spike dan timeout cascade. |
| 7 | **Pool Metrics** | Active, idle, pending, timeout, usage duration. | Mendeteksi DB bottleneck lebih awal. | Latency naik tanpa tahu pool saturated. |
| 8 | **JVM/K8s Resource Coupling** | CPU/memory limit mempengaruhi DB latency dan pool behavior. | CPU throttling bisa memperlambat query handling. | Salah diagnosis sebagai DB issue. |
| 9 | **Migration Compatibility** | Expand/contract migration, backward-compatible mapper. | Rolling update old/new Pod. | Rollback gagal dan v1/v2 crash. |
| 10 | **Readiness DB Policy** | DB check di readiness harus hati-hati. | Mencegah traffic ke app belum siap tanpa membebani DB. | Readiness storm ke DB. |
| 11 | **Graceful Shutdown** | Stop request, finish transaction, close pool. | Pod rollout/scale-down aman. | In-flight transaction putus. |
| 12 | **Retry/Idempotency** | Retry DB operation hanya untuk kasus aman. | Mencegah duplicate business side effect. | Double insert/payment/workflow. |
| 13 | **PgBouncer Awareness** | Pooling external jika connection count tinggi. | Banyak Pod + PostgreSQL. | Terlalu banyak backend session. |
| 14 | **Observability** | Logs, metrics, traces untuk DB operation. | Debug root cause cepat. | Semua error terlihat seperti 500 generic. |

---

# 4. Connection Budget

Formula dasar:

```text id="f3fzh9"
steady_state_max_connections
=
replicas × DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
```

Formula production yang lebih aman:

```text id="wfp3ce"
worst_case_app_connections
=
(HPA maxReplicas + maxSurge) × DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
```

Tambahkan komponen lain:

```text id="icxkmo"
total_database_connection_demand
=
API Pods
+ Worker Pods
+ CronJobs/Jobs
+ migration jobs
+ admin tools
+ monitoring
+ BI/reporting
+ other services using same DB
```

Contoh:

```text id="iqglyt"
order-api:
  HPA maxReplicas = 10
  maxSurge = 2
  DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 10
  worst-case = 12 × 10 = 120

order-worker:
  replicas = 4
  DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 5
  worst-case = 20

migration/admin/monitoring reserve:
  20

total possible:
  160
```

Jika PostgreSQL `max_connections` atau reserved budget untuk aplikasi hanya 120, setting ini berbahaya.

Senior review:

```text id="xqu7lc"
Jangan hanya tanya:
  pool size berapa?

Tanya:
  pool size × max replicas × surge × all workloads masih aman?
```

---

# 5. Pool Size Tidak Selalu Lebih Besar Lebih Baik

Pool size besar bisa tampak seperti solusi latency, tetapi sering memperburuk DB.

```text id="n2075k"
Pool besar:
  lebih banyak concurrent query
  lebih banyak PostgreSQL backend session
  lebih banyak memory per connection
  lebih banyak lock contention
  lebih banyak IO pressure
  lebih sulit bagi DB scheduler/cache
```

Pool kecil:

```text id="ejtqse"
lebih cepat memberi backpressure ke app
mengurangi DB overload
lebih mudah dikontrol dengan HPA/circuit breaker
```

Prinsip:

```text id="g7x3by"
Pool size harus berdasarkan DB capacity dan workload,
bukan berdasarkan jumlah HTTP threads.
```

Baseline untuk REST API:

```text id="r0wzrr"
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE:
  mulai dari 5-15 per Pod untuk service umum

kemudian validasi dengan:
  load test
  query latency
  DB CPU/IO
  pool pending
  connection timeout
  p95/p99 HTTP latency
```

Angka ini bukan aturan universal, tetapi starting point. Service heavy DB atau worker bisa berbeda.

---

# 6. HikariCP Baseline

HikariCP adalah connection pool populer di Java. Dokumentasi HikariCP menyatakan `maximumPoolSize` mengontrol jumlah maksimum koneksi di pool, termasuk idle dan in-use, dan ketika pool mencapai ukuran ini tanpa idle connection, pemanggil `getConnection()` akan block sampai `connectionTimeout` sebelum timeout. ([github.com](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/HikariCP))

Contoh config via env:

```yaml id="tkfu2d"
env:
  - name: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
    value: "10"

  - name: DB_POOL_MIN_IDLE
    value: "2"

  - name: DB_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS
    value: "2000"

  - name: DB_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS
    value: "600000"

  - name: DB_MAX_LIFETIME_MS
    value: "1800000"
```

Contoh Java config:

```java id="4hafxi"
HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

config.setJdbcUrl(System.getenv("DB_URL"));
config.setUsername(System.getenv("DB_USERNAME"));
config.setPassword(System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"));

config.setMaximumPoolSize(
        Integer.parseInt(System.getenv().getOrDefault("DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE", "10"))
);

config.setMinimumIdle(
        Integer.parseInt(System.getenv().getOrDefault("DB_POOL_MIN_IDLE", "2"))
);

config.setConnectionTimeout(
        Long.parseLong(System.getenv().getOrDefault("DB_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS", "2000"))
);

config.setIdleTimeout(
        Long.parseLong(System.getenv().getOrDefault("DB_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS", "600000"))
);

config.setMaxLifetime(
        Long.parseLong(System.getenv().getOrDefault("DB_MAX_LIFETIME_MS", "1800000"))
);

HikariDataSource dataSource = new HikariDataSource(config);
```

Prinsip:

```text id="ga640o"
connectionTimeout jangan terlalu lama.
Jika pool habis, request harus gagal cepat/terkontrol,
bukan menggantung sampai gateway timeout.
```

---

# 7. Timeouts: Layering yang Benar

Timeout harus berlapis dari luar ke dalam.

```text id="vn3j2d"
Client timeout
  > Gateway/Ingress timeout
    > Jersey request handling budget
      > Downstream HTTP timeout
      > DB connection acquire timeout
      > DB statement timeout
      > Lock timeout
```

Contoh budget:

```text id="tn6xk4"
External API request budget:
  3 seconds

DB pool acquire:
  200ms-500ms for latency-sensitive read
  1s-2s for heavier operation

statement_timeout:
  2s for normal API query
  longer for explicitly allowed batch/reporting

lock_timeout:
  200ms-1s for OLTP updates

gateway timeout:
  slightly above app budget, not 60s by accident
```

Tujuannya:

```text id="d70jy6"
failure terjadi dekat sumber masalah
bukan semua request menunggu sampai gateway 504
```

---

# 8. PostgreSQL Timeout Settings

PostgreSQL menyediakan beberapa timeout yang penting untuk aplikasi.

| Timeout | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `statement_timeout` | Membatalkan statement yang berjalan terlalu lama. |
| `lock_timeout` | Membatalkan statement yang menunggu lock terlalu lama. |
| `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` | Menghentikan session yang idle terlalu lama dalam transaction. |
| `transaction_timeout` | Membatasi total durasi transaksi. |

PostgreSQL current docs menjelaskan `transaction_timeout` membatasi durasi session dalam transaksi, mencakup explicit transaction dan implicit transaction untuk single statement; default `0` menonaktifkannya. Docs juga menyatakan jika `transaction_timeout` lebih pendek atau sama dengan `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` atau `statement_timeout`, timeout yang lebih panjang diabaikan. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html))

Contoh per session dari JDBC URL:

```text id="xm68c6"
jdbc:postgresql://postgresql.data-prod.svc.cluster.local:5432/orderdb?options=-c%20statement_timeout=3000%20-c%20lock_timeout=1000
```

Atau setelah connection acquired:

```sql id="eubwks"
SET statement_timeout = '3s';
SET lock_timeout = '1s';
SET idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '30s';
```

Hati-hati:

```text id="bcdz78"
Jangan set timeout global di postgresql.conf tanpa koordinasi DBA,
karena semua aplikasi/session bisa terdampak.
```

PostgreSQL docs juga menyatakan setting `transaction_timeout` di `postgresql.conf` tidak direkomendasikan karena memengaruhi semua session. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html))

---

# 9. `idle in transaction`: Musuh Diam-diam

`idle in transaction` berarti aplikasi membuka transaction, lalu tidak commit/rollback, tetapi juga tidak aktif menjalankan query.

Efek:

```text id="c5nkqh"
lock bisa tertahan
vacuum terhambat
table bloat meningkat
connection pool slot tertahan
query lain bisa menunggu
```

PostgreSQL transaction isolation docs secara eksplisit menyarankan jangan meninggalkan connection “idle in transaction” lebih lama dari yang diperlukan, dan menyebut `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` dapat dipakai untuk disconnect session yang menggantung. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

Penyebab umum di Java/MyBatis:

```text id="kt52am"
SqlSession tidak ditutup
exception path tidak rollback
transaction boundary terlalu luas
request menunggu downstream saat transaction masih terbuka
manual transaction management lupa finally
streaming result tidak ditutup
```

Anti-pattern:

```java id="1t05x0"
SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession();
Order order = session.selectOne("findOrder", id);
// exception happens before close/commit/rollback
```

Lebih aman:

```java id="vptjwm"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
    Order order = session.selectOne("findOrder", id);
    session.commit();
    return order;
} catch (RuntimeException e) {
    // rollback if transaction has started and manual management used
    throw e;
}
```

Lebih lengkap:

```java id="1prkzk"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
    OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

    Order order = mapper.findById(orderId);
    session.commit();

    return order;
} catch (Exception e) {
    // With try-with-resources, close is guaranteed.
    // If managing transaction manually, ensure rollback in catch before close.
    throw e;
}
```

Jika menggunakan framework transaction manager, pastikan lifecycle session dikelola oleh framework. MyBatis-Spring `SqlSessionTemplate` mendokumentasikan bahwa ia mengelola lifecycle session termasuk close, commit, atau rollback sesuai kebutuhan dan menerjemahkan exception MyBatis ke Spring `DataAccessException`. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/spring/sqlsession.html))

---

# 10. MyBatis Lifecycle: Apa yang Harus Dipahami

MyBatis core:

```text id="19ka24"
SqlSessionFactoryBuilder:
  membangun SqlSessionFactory

SqlSessionFactory:
  membuat SqlSession

SqlSession:
  menjalankan mapped SQL dan mengelola scope database operation

Mapper:
  interface yang dipakai aplikasi untuk query/command
```

MyBatis getting started menyatakan setiap aplikasi MyBatis berpusat pada instance `SqlSessionFactory`, yang dapat diperoleh dengan `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder`. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

Prinsip runtime:

```text id="nz7slk"
SqlSessionFactory:
  buat sekali saat startup, reuse.

SqlSession:
  scope pendek, per unit of work/request/transaction.
  jangan simpan sebagai singleton field.
  harus close.
```

Pattern manual:

```java id="h4z4o9"
public Order findOrder(String orderId) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);
        return mapper.findById(orderId);
    }
}
```

Untuk write transaction:

```java id="tdaw00"
public void createOrder(Order order) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(order);

        session.commit();
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        // If rollback is needed, use explicit session variable and rollback before close.
        throw e;
    }
}
```

Safer explicit rollback:

```java id="tjw9ow"
public void createOrder(Order order) {
    SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
    try {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(order);

        session.commit();
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        session.rollback();
        throw e;
    } finally {
        session.close();
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Transaction Boundary Design

Good transaction boundary:

```text id="i2482h"
pendek
jelas
tidak menunggu network call eksternal di tengah transaction
punya timeout
commit/rollback deterministic
idempotent bila retry bisa terjadi
```

Bad transaction boundary:

```text id="6kngy3"
begin transaction
insert order
call payment API 3 seconds
call customer API
call Camunda
update order
commit
```

Masalah:

```text id="8aqj3b"
DB connection ditahan selama outbound HTTP call
lock ditahan lama
pool cepat habis
rollback side effect eksternal sulit
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="n0b0ih"
Transaction A:
  validate + insert order pending
  commit

Call external/payment/Camunda outside DB transaction
  with idempotency key

Transaction B:
  update status based on result
  commit
```

Atau gunakan outbox pattern untuk side effect yang harus reliable.

---

# 12. Query Timeout vs HTTP Timeout

Misalnya HTTP read timeout 3 detik, tetapi DB query bisa jalan 60 detik.

Efek:

```text id="u57x8n"
client sudah timeout
gateway sudah 504
Jersey request mungkin interrupted
tetapi DB query masih jalan
connection tetap busy
DB tetap bekerja untuk hasil yang tidak dipakai
```

Karena itu:

```text id="fmhexu"
DB statement_timeout harus <= application request budget
untuk request path biasa.
```

Batch/reporting boleh punya budget berbeda, tapi jangan campur dengan API latency-sensitive.

---

# 13. Kubernetes Probe dan DB

Readiness check boleh memeriksa kesiapan DB secara terbatas, tetapi hati-hati.

Bad:

```text id="ce68p0"
Setiap 5 detik, 50 Pods menjalankan SELECT 1 ke DB
```

Hitung:

```text id="8oipgg"
50 Pods × 12 checks/minute = 600 DB checks/minute
```

Jika DB sedang degraded, readiness check ikut menambah beban.

Better:

```text id="u1vsll"
startup:
  validate DB config and initial connection if critical

readiness:
  check local app initialized
  optionally cached DB health state
  avoid heavy query each probe

metrics:
  expose DB pool/connection errors separately

traffic:
  circuit breaker/backpressure for DB failure
```

Jika DB wajib untuk semua request, readiness boleh false saat DB benar-benar unavailable, tetapi jangan membuat probe menjadi DDoS kecil.

---

# 14. Graceful Shutdown dan DB Pool

Saat Pod termination:

```text id="t946xd"
SIGTERM
  │
  ├── readiness false
  ├── stop accepting new HTTP requests
  ├── finish in-flight DB transactions
  ├── rollback unfinished transactions
  ├── close HikariDataSource
  └── exit before terminationGracePeriodSeconds
```

Contoh shutdown hook:

```java id="akq7et"
public final class ShutdownManager {
    private final HikariDataSource dataSource;
    private final ExecutorService executor;

    public ShutdownManager(HikariDataSource dataSource, ExecutorService executor) {
        this.dataSource = dataSource;
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public void register() {
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(this::shutdown, "shutdown-hook"));
    }

    private void shutdown() {
        // 1. mark readiness false before this if possible
        // 2. stop background work
        executor.shutdown();

        try {
            executor.awaitTermination(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }

        // 3. close pool after active work has drained
        dataSource.close();
    }
}
```

Jangan close DataSource sebelum request drain selesai; in-flight request bisa gagal.

---

# 15. Deployment Rollout dan DB Spike

Saat rolling update:

```text id="3g4ix9"
old Pods still running
new Pods starting
both old and new may open DB pools
```

Jika Hikari `minimumIdle` tinggi, new Pods bisa langsung membuka banyak idle connections.

Contoh:

```text id="szf4x5"
replicas=10
maxSurge=2
minimumIdle=10
maximumPoolSize=10

new 2 Pods start
immediately open up to 20 new connections
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="xkd82p"
jangan set minimumIdle terlalu tinggi tanpa alasan
biarkan Hikari fixed/small idle sesuai traffic
validasi startup connection spike
atur maxSurge sesuai DB capacity
```

---

# 16. HPA dan DB Bottleneck

Jika HTTP latency naik karena DB lambat, HPA CPU-based mungkin tidak membantu.

Scenario:

```text id="0dwbe6"
DB lambat
request threads menunggu DB
CPU Java rendah
HPA tidak scale karena CPU rendah
latency naik
```

Atau sebaliknya:

```text id="lbrp9n"
CPU tinggi
HPA scale out
lebih banyak Pods
lebih banyak DB connections/query concurrency
DB makin lambat
error makin tinggi
```

Senior rule:

```text id="y5eicb"
Jangan autoscale DB-bound service hanya dari CPU.
Monitor DB pool pending, query latency, DB CPU/IO, lock wait, and error rate.
```

HPA maxReplicas harus menghormati DB connection budget.

---

# 17. PostgreSQL Lock Wait dan Deadlock

Gejala:

```text id="3c9z5p"
p95 latency spike
DB query duration naik
pool active tinggi
pending connections naik
some requests timeout
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="80n1k0"
row lock contention
long transaction
SELECT FOR UPDATE terlalu luas
update order berbeda urutan
missing index menyebabkan update/delete scan besar
deadlock retry
```

Gunakan `lock_timeout` untuk mencegah request menunggu lock terlalu lama:

```sql id="pzvfih"
SET lock_timeout = '1s';
```

PostgreSQL docs menjelaskan `lock_timeout` membatalkan statement yang menunggu lock lebih lama dari durasi yang dikonfigurasi, dan berbeda dari `statement_timeout` karena berlaku hanya saat menunggu lock. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html))

App handling:

```text id="3h8vsi"
lock timeout untuk user-facing API:
  return controlled 409/503 depending domain
  retry only if operation idempotent and safe
  log errorCode=DB_LOCK_TIMEOUT
  trace span status error
```

---

# 18. Idempotency untuk Retry

Retry bisa berbahaya.

Aman untuk retry:

```text id="frmqwi"
read query
idempotent update by deterministic key
insert with idempotency key / unique constraint
transient connection failure before side effect
serialization failure with retry policy
```

Berbahaya:

```text id="4j4ye4"
insert order tanpa unique key
charge payment
start Camunda process
publish event
send email/SMS
reserve inventory
```

Pattern:

```text id="gdnwpo"
Use idempotency key:
  request_id
  business_key
  external_reference
  unique constraint
```

Example:

```sql id="aixsrk"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_order_external_reference
ON orders (external_reference);
```

Insert safely:

```sql id="otcj2c"
INSERT INTO orders (external_reference, status, total_amount)
VALUES (#{externalReference}, #{status}, #{totalAmount})
ON CONFLICT (external_reference) DO NOTHING;
```

Atau `DO UPDATE` sesuai semantics.

---

# 19. MyBatis Mapper Safety

Bad mapper:

```xml id="e98k3t"
<select id="searchOrders" resultType="Order">
  SELECT *
  FROM orders
  WHERE customer_name LIKE '%' || #{query} || '%'
</select>
```

Risiko:

```text id="u74r48"
full table scan
unbounded result
large memory use
slow query
API timeout
```

Better:

```xml id="gf6wl6"
<select id="searchOrders" resultType="Order">
  SELECT id, status, total_amount, created_at
  FROM orders
  WHERE customer_id = #{customerId}
  ORDER BY created_at DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
  OFFSET #{offset}
</select>
```

Guardrails:

```text id="d9aswz"
always limit list queries
prefer keyset pagination for large datasets
select required columns only
avoid N+1 queries
validate sort fields via whitelist
avoid raw ${} substitution unless absolutely controlled
```

Important MyBatis security:

```text id="zz0uq9"
#{param}
  uses prepared parameter binding

${param}
  string substitution, dangerous for SQL injection unless tightly whitelisted
```

---

# 20. Pagination dan Memory

Jangan return ribuan/jutaan row ke Jersey response.

Bad:

```text id="9mb9ox"
GET /orders?status=ALL
returns 500,000 rows
```

Efek:

```text id="ka2fqy"
DB slow
JDBC result large
MyBatis maps huge object graph
heap usage spike
GC pause
OOMKilled possible
network response huge
```

Better:

```text id="pz9ba9"
limit required
max limit enforced
keyset pagination
streaming/reporting async for large export
```

API contract:

```text id="346mgo"
limit default 50
limit max 500
cursor-based pagination for large lists
```

---

# 21. N+1 Query Problem

Bad flow:

```text id="af8q63"
SELECT 100 orders
for each order:
  SELECT order_items WHERE order_id = ?
```

Total:

```text id="cv0acm"
1 + 100 queries
```

Effect in Kubernetes:

```text id="z9dlba"
more DB round trips
longer request duration
connection held longer
pool pressure
latency worsens during scale
```

Fix:

```text id="7jv90n"
join where appropriate
batch select by IDs
use dedicated query DTO
avoid lazy-load style behavior hidden in mapper
```

---

# 22. Batch Jobs, CronJobs, and DB Pressure

CronJob/Job can overload DB if not controlled.

Example risk:

```text id="jmj7nw"
order-reconciliation CronJob starts at midnight
parallelism=20
each worker pool max=10
possible DB connections=200
```

CronJob config:

```yaml id="7x4f03"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: CronJob
metadata:
  name: order-reconciliation
spec:
  schedule: "0 * * * *"
  concurrencyPolicy: Forbid
  jobTemplate:
    spec:
      template:
        spec:
          containers:
            - name: reconciliation
              image: registry.example.com/order-reconciliation:1.0.0
              env:
                - name: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
                  value: "3"
```

For DB-heavy batch:

```text id="f4hh6g"
small pool
bounded parallelism
chunk processing
checkpoint progress
lock timeout
statement timeout
off-peak scheduling
do not run unlimited Jobs
```

---

# 23. Migration Safety in Kubernetes

Migration bisa dijalankan:

```text id="zsq20y"
CI/CD pre-deploy step
Kubernetes Job
initContainer
external DBA process
migration tool controller
```

Untuk production, hindari initContainer migration pada setiap replica jika migration tidak didesain distributed-safe.

Bad:

```text id="j3nd8k"
Deployment replicas=5
each Pod initContainer runs migration
race condition / lock / startup delay
```

Better:

```text id="1so65d"
single migration Job
then app rollout
or controlled CI/CD migration step
or migration tool with locking and governance
```

Flyway/Liquibase punya locking mechanism, tetapi tetap perlu governance agar migration tidak berjalan bersamaan secara tidak sengaja dan tidak menjadi bottleneck startup.

---

# 24. Expand/Contract Migration Recap

Rolling update membutuhkan old/new compatibility.

```text id="pk7i3x"
Release 1:
  expand schema
  add nullable column/table/index
  deploy app compatible with old/new

Release 2:
  backfill data
  dual-write or switch read path

Release 3:
  remove old code usage

Release 4:
  contract schema
  drop old column/table
```

Bad:

```sql id="wwqzw6"
ALTER TABLE orders DROP COLUMN old_status;
```

dalam release yang sama saat old Pods masih bisa jalan.

Better:

```sql id="x27r3p"
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN new_status TEXT;
```

lalu app dual-read/dual-write sampai safe.

---

# 25. PostgreSQL Managed DB vs In-Cluster DB

Untuk production enterprise, PostgreSQL sering lebih baik sebagai managed DB/on-prem DBA-managed service, bukan StatefulSet manual oleh app team.

| Pattern | Cocok Untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Managed DB AWS/Azure | Production cloud | Network/IAM/cost/vendor specifics. |
| On-prem DBA-managed PostgreSQL | Enterprise datacenter | Change process dan network firewall. |
| In-cluster PostgreSQL via operator | Platform yang matang | Operator expertise required. |
| Plain StatefulSet by app team | Dev/test/lab | Backup/HA/failover/upgrade risk. |

Untuk software engineer aplikasi:

```text id="rr8ix2"
Anda harus tahu endpoint, SSL, pool budget, timeout, migration process, observability,
bukan selalu mengelola PostgreSQL cluster sendiri.
```

---

# 26. SSL/TLS and Certificates

Production DB biasanya perlu TLS.

JDBC URL pattern:

```text id="xjxpgr"
jdbc:postgresql://db.example.internal:5432/orderdb?sslmode=verify-full
```

Mount truststore/CA:

```yaml id="pw9rhj"
volumeMounts:
  - name: db-ca
    mountPath: /app/certs
    readOnly: true

volumes:
  - name: db-ca
    secret:
      secretName: postgres-ca
```

Config:

```yaml id="3zn6om"
env:
  - name: DB_SSL_MODE
    value: "verify-full"
  - name: DB_CA_CERT_PATH
    value: "/app/certs/ca.crt"
```

Failure modes:

```text id="vk31tx"
certificate expired
hostname mismatch
wrong CA
sslmode disabled in prod accidentally
truststore not readable by non-root user
```

---

# 27. Observability: Metrics Wajib

DB app metrics:

```text id="m7q0ec"
hikaricp_connections_active
hikaricp_connections_idle
hikaricp_connections_pending
hikaricp_connections_max
hikaricp_connections_timeout_total
hikaricp_connections_usage_seconds
db_operation_duration_seconds
db_operation_errors_total
db_operation_slow_total
```

Kubernetes metrics:

```text id="3vhd64"
Pod replicas
Pod restarts
OOMKilled
CPU throttling
memory usage
HPA current/desired replicas
```

PostgreSQL-side metrics:

```text id="sl48ib"
active connections
idle in transaction
long running queries
lock waits
deadlocks
transaction rate
buffer/cache hit
CPU/IO
replication lag if relevant
autovacuum activity
```

Dashboard should answer:

```text id="yexl5v"
Is app waiting for DB pool?
Is DB query slow?
Is DB locked?
Is DB saturated?
Did rollout increase connections?
Did HPA scale out and worsen DB?
```

---

# 28. Observability: Logs

Log DB failures with structured fields:

```json id="9sldeh"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "traceId": "abc",
  "dbOperation": "OrderMapper.insertOrder",
  "sqlState": "23505",
  "errorCode": "DB_UNIQUE_VIOLATION",
  "message": "Database constraint violation"
}
```

Slow operation log:

```json id="bbfshs"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "service": "order-api",
  "requestId": "req-456",
  "dbOperation": "OrderMapper.searchOrders",
  "durationMs": 1250,
  "thresholdMs": 500,
  "message": "Slow database operation"
}
```

Jangan log:

```text id="u7ihh2"
DB password
full JDBC URL with credentials
raw SQL with sensitive literals
large result sets
PII values
```

---

# 29. Observability: Traces

Trace should show:

```text id="vscgse"
HTTP POST /orders
├── OrderService.createOrder
├── Hikari acquire connection
├── OrderMapper.insertOrder
├── PaymentClient.authorize
└── OrderMapper.updatePaymentStatus
```

Useful attributes:

```text id="3m896l"
db.system.name=postgresql
db.operation.name=SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE
db.collection.name=orders
db.namespace=orderdb
dbOperation=OrderMapper.findById
```

Do not use:

```text id="kh586o"
full SQL with literal values
orderId as high-cardinality span name
password/token
```

OpenTelemetry semantic conventions define common DB client attributes such as database system and operation concepts, though exact attribute names can evolve by version. ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/db/))

---

# 30. Read/Write Splitting Awareness

Jika DB punya read replica:

```text id="xo5a6p"
writes → primary
reads → replica
```

Risks:

```text id="yt8rqs"
replication lag
read-after-write inconsistency
transaction semantics more complex
routing bugs
```

For order workflow:

```text id="rvp0vs"
After creating order on primary,
do not immediately read from replica if user expects fresh status,
unless app handles lag.
```

Read/write splitting harus eksplisit di repository/service layer, bukan kebetulan via random URL.

---

# 31. PgBouncer / External Pooler Awareness

Jika banyak Pod menyebabkan terlalu banyak PostgreSQL backend connection, platform mungkin memakai PgBouncer.

Mental model:

```text id="mpz0he"
Java Hikari pool
  → PgBouncer
  → fewer PostgreSQL backend connections
```

Benefits:

```text id="h4qz91"
reduce backend connection pressure
absorb many app connections
faster connection reuse
```

Caveats:

```text id="amdwvs"
transaction pooling has limitations
session-level settings may not persist
prepared statements behavior needs checking
LISTEN/NOTIFY/session features may not work as expected
SET commands need care
```

Jika perusahaan memakai PgBouncer, minta standard configuration dari DBA/platform.

---

# 32. Runtime Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| HTTP p95 naik | DB pool pending, slow query, lock wait | Hikari pending, traces |
| 500 `connection timeout` | Pool exhausted | Hikari timeout metric/log |
| PostgreSQL max connections reached | replicas × pool terlalu tinggi | DB metrics, app logs |
| OOMKilled | result set besar/cache/thread/native | Pod describe, JVM metrics |
| CPU low tapi latency tinggi | waiting DB/downstream | traces, pool active/pending |
| CPU high + DB slow | query CPU, JSON mapping, GC | DB CPU, app CPU, GC |
| Readiness flapping | DB check heavy/degraded | events, health logs |
| Rollout causes DB spike | maxSurge + minimumIdle | connection metrics by version |
| Idle in transaction | session leak/long transaction | pg_stat_activity |
| Deadlocks | inconsistent update order | DB logs/sqlState |
| Migration breaks app | old/new mapper mismatch | rollout logs, DB errors |
| Worker duplicate side effect | retry/idempotency issue | business logs/Camunda history |

---

# 33. Debugging Commands

Kubernetes side:

```bash id="8owuep"
kubectl get pods -n order-prod \
  -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-api
```

```bash id="i84d04"
kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n order-prod
```

```bash id="n1h5o9"
kubectl logs deploy/order-api -n order-prod --since=15m
```

```bash id="cjv3q2"
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod --previous
```

```bash id="o85d4m"
kubectl top pod -n order-prod
```

Check rollout:

```bash id="fylvj9"
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-api -n order-prod
```

Check env config carefully:

```bash id="2y03gj"
kubectl get deploy order-api -n order-prod -o yaml
```

Do not blindly:

```bash id="rqef3f"
kubectl exec ... printenv
```

because secrets may be present.

---

# 34. PostgreSQL Query Snippets for DBA/App Debug

Use with DBA approval.

Active sessions:

```sql id="8tse7c"
SELECT pid, usename, application_name, state, wait_event_type, wait_event,
       now() - query_start AS query_age,
       now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
       left(query, 200) AS query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE datname = current_database()
ORDER BY query_age DESC;
```

Idle in transaction:

```sql id="lrve7w"
SELECT pid, usename, application_name,
       now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
       now() - state_change AS idle_age,
       left(query, 200) AS query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state = 'idle in transaction'
ORDER BY idle_age DESC;
```

Lock waits:

```sql id="ifhsgq"
SELECT blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
       blocked.query AS blocked_query,
       blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
       blocking.query AS blocking_query
FROM pg_stat_activity blocked
JOIN pg_locks blocked_locks
  ON blocked_locks.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking_locks
  ON blocking_locks.locktype = blocked_locks.locktype
 AND blocking_locks.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.database
 AND blocking_locks.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.relation
 AND blocking_locks.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.page
 AND blocking_locks.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.tuple
 AND blocking_locks.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.virtualxid
 AND blocking_locks.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.transactionid
 AND blocking_locks.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.classid
 AND blocking_locks.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objid
 AND blocking_locks.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objsubid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking
  ON blocking.pid = blocking_locks.pid
WHERE NOT blocked_locks.granted
  AND blocking_locks.granted;
```

Production caution:

```text id="0za1wh"
Jangan jalankan query diagnosis berat sembarangan di incident.
Koordinasi dengan DBA/platform.
```

# 35. Kubernetes Manifest Baseline

```yaml id="n1ltxv"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0

  template:
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45

      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

          env:
            - name: DB_URL
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: order-api-db
                  key: DB_URL

            - name: DB_USERNAME
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: order-api-db
                  key: DB_USERNAME

            - name: DB_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: order-api-db
                  key: DB_PASSWORD

            - name: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
              value: "10"

            - name: DB_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS
              value: "2000"

            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: >-
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
                -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1024Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2048Mi"

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3
```

Review dengan formula:

```text id="w5c00a"
HPA maxReplicas?
maxSurge?
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE?
total possible connection?
DB budget?
```

---

# 36. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Pool Size Besar di Setiap Pod

```text id="akw6sx"
replicas=20
pool=50
possible connections=1000
```

PostgreSQL mungkin tidak mampu atau tidak perlu menerima 1000 app connections.

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Transaction Menunggu Downstream HTTP

```text id="3p2ocr"
BEGIN
insert order
call payment-api
call Camunda
commit
```

Menahan connection/lock selama network call.

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Query List Tanpa Limit

```sql id="41soxl"
SELECT * FROM orders WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

Untuk API endpoint, selalu batasi.

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Readiness Probe Query DB Terlalu Sering

```text id="dhhpry"
100 Pods × SELECT 1 every 5s
```

Menambah load saat DB sedang bermasalah.

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Destructive Migration dalam Rolling Update

```sql id="8xywdj"
DROP COLUMN used_by_old_version;
```

Rollback bisa gagal.

---

## Anti-pattern 6 - Logging SQL Raw dengan Data Sensitif

```text id="ovpzkw"
INSERT INTO users(email, password_hash, ...)
```

Jangan log literal/PII/secret.

---

## Anti-pattern 7 - Retry Semua DB Error

Retry tanpa idempotency bisa membuat duplicate write atau double side effect.

---

# 37. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Connection budget | Apakah `(HPA maxReplicas + maxSurge) × poolSize` aman? |
| Pool config | Apakah `maximumPoolSize`, `connectionTimeout`, `maxLifetime` jelas? |
| DB ownership | Apakah DB budget disepakati dengan DBA/platform? |
| Transaction | Apakah transaksi pendek dan tidak menunggu downstream call? |
| MyBatis session | Apakah `SqlSession` lifecycle aman dan selalu close? |
| Query | Apakah query list punya limit/pagination? |
| N+1 | Apakah mapper menghindari N+1? |
| Timeout | Apakah statement/lock/connection timeout sesuai request budget? |
| Readiness | Apakah DB readiness check tidak membebani DB? |
| Shutdown | Apakah DataSource close setelah request drain? |
| HPA | Apakah scale-out tidak melebihi DB capacity? |
| Migration | Apakah expand/contract dan rollback-safe? |
| Observability | Apakah Hikari, DB operation, slow query, errors termonitor? |
| Security | Apakah DB credential via Secret dan tidak di-log? |
| TLS | Apakah SSL mode/cert/truststore sesuai prod policy? |
| Batch | Apakah Job/CronJob DB parallelism dibatasi? |
| Retry | Apakah retry hanya untuk operation idempotent/safe? |

---

# 38. Ringkasan Seri 22

```text id="0ps9em"
Kubernetes scale-out:
  menambah Pod

Java connection pool:
  menambah potensi koneksi DB

PostgreSQL:
  punya batas koneksi, CPU, memory, IO, lock, transaction capacity

Total DB pressure:
  replicas × pool size × query complexity × transaction duration
```

Untuk Java/Jersey + MyBatis:

```text id="ve2xzo"
Gunakan HikariCP dengan pool size sadar DB budget.
Hitung HPA maxReplicas dan rollout maxSurge.
Jaga transaction pendek.
Jangan menunggu downstream call di dalam transaction.
Pastikan SqlSession selalu close.
Gunakan timeout DB yang selaras dengan HTTP timeout.
Monitor pool active/idle/pending/timeout.
Gunakan pagination dan hindari N+1.
Jalankan migration expand/contract.
Hindari readiness probe yang membebani DB.
Pastikan graceful shutdown menutup DataSource setelah drain.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="bxz3ig"
Database adalah shared dependency yang paling mudah menjadi bottleneck saat Kubernetes scaling.
Senior engineer tidak hanya membuat app bisa connect ke PostgreSQL,
tetapi memastikan concurrency, transaction, migration, timeout, observability,
dan rollout strategy tidak menghancurkan database saat traffic atau replica naik.
```
