## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:48:56 PM

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 23/24  
## Camunda, Worker Runtime, Job Execution, Kubernetes Deployment Pattern, Scaling Worker, Lock Duration, Retries, Idempotency, Graceful Shutdown, dan Observability untuk Workflow Processing

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **bagaimana menjalankan Camunda worker di Kubernetes secara aman, scalable, idempotent, observable, dan tidak menciptakan duplicate side effect saat Pod restart, rollout, atau timeout?**

Catatan versi: di enterprise, “Camunda” bisa berarti **Camunda 7 External Task Worker** atau **Camunda 8 / Zeebe Job Worker**. Prinsip operasionalnya mirip: worker mengambil pekerjaan dari engine/broker, mengerjakan business logic, lalu memberi sinyal **complete**, **fail**, atau **business error**. Di Camunda 8, job worker mem-poll jobs berdasarkan job type, menjalankan logic, lalu mengirim complete/fail ke cluster; Camunda docs juga menegaskan banyak worker bisa request job type yang sama untuk scaling, dan Zeebe memastikan satu job dikirim ke satu worker pada satu waktu selama job tersebut aktif. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="2zdt20"
Camunda Workflow Runtime
│
├── Process engine / orchestration cluster
│   ├── process instance
│   ├── service task / job / external task
│   ├── lock / activation timeout
│   ├── retry count
│   ├── incident
│   └── BPMN error
│
├── Worker runtime
│   ├── polls / subscribes to job type or topic
│   ├── fetches / activates work
│   ├── executes business logic
│   ├── calls DB/downstream APIs
│   ├── completes job
│   ├── fails job with retry/backoff
│   └── handles BPMN business error
│
└── Kubernetes runtime
    ├── Deployment replicas
    ├── rolling update
    ├── readiness/liveness
    ├── graceful shutdown
    ├── HPA scaling
    ├── resource limits
    └── Pod restart / eviction / OOMKilled
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="l1zwb0"
Camunda worker harus diasumsikan at-least-once.
Worker code harus idempotent karena task/job bisa diproses lebih dari sekali
akibat timeout, retry, Pod restart, network failure, atau complete/fail command yang gagal.
```

Camunda 8 docs secara eksplisit menyatakan timeout bisa menyebabkan job dikerjakan lebih dari sekali dan worker code harus idempotent karena Zeebe bersifat “at least once” untuk job delivery. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

---

# 2. Diagram Runtime

```text id="5o0owo"
Camunda Engine / Zeebe
  │
  │ job type/topic: reserve-inventory
  ▼
Kubernetes Deployment: order-worker
  ├── Pod 1
  │   └── Java worker process
  ├── Pod 2
  │   └── Java worker process
  └── Pod 3
      └── Java worker process

Each Pod:
  ├── activate/fetch job
  ├── execute handler
  ├── call PostgreSQL/MyBatis
  ├── call downstream API
  ├── complete/fail/BPMN error
  └── emit logs/metrics/traces
```

Scaling model:

```text id="3z7cwz"
Total worker capacity
≈ replicas × max active jobs per worker × average job throughput
```

But real capacity is constrained by:

```text id="cly1qa"
PostgreSQL connection budget
downstream API rate limit
Camunda broker/engine capacity
CPU/memory
lock duration / activation timeout
handler latency
retry/backoff policy
```

---

# 3. Practical Skill Map

| No | Skill / Domain | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Relevansi untuk Kubernetes + Java Worker | Risiko Kalau Lemah |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Job Type / Topic Design** | Nama job type/topic stabil, bounded, domain-specific. | Worker subscribe ke work item yang benar. | Worker salah topic atau tidak process job. |
| 2 | **Deployment Pattern** | Worker sebagai Kubernetes Deployment. | Scale replica worker horizontal. | Worker dijalankan sebagai CronJob padahal harus long-running. |
| 3 | **Activation / Lock Timeout** | Durasi job/task dipegang worker. | Harus lebih besar dari normal processing time. | Job diproses worker lain sebelum worker pertama selesai. |
| 4 | **maxJobsActive / maxTasks** | Batas concurrency per worker. | Mengontrol memory, DB, downstream pressure. | Worker mengambil terlalu banyak job dan overload. |
| 5 | **Retries** | Fail transient dengan retry/backoff; incident jika habis. | Recovery dependency failure. | Retry storm atau incident terlalu cepat. |
| 6 | **BPMN Error vs Technical Failure** | Business error ≠ system failure. | Model workflow lebih akurat. | Semua error jadi retry padahal harus branch BPMN. |
| 7 | **Idempotency** | Side effect aman dieksekusi ulang. | Wajib untuk payment, order, inventory, email, Camunda complete. | Duplicate order/payment/reservation. |
| 8 | **Graceful Shutdown** | Stop polling, drain active jobs, complete/fail safely. | Rolling update aman. | Job timeout dan duplicate side effect. |
| 9 | **Backpressure** | Worker mengurangi load saat Camunda/DB/downstream overload. | Mencegah cascade failure. | Scaling worker memperparah incident. |
| 10 | **Observability** | Metrics/logs/traces per job type/result/duration. | Debug backlog, retries, failures. | Workflow stuck tanpa evidence. |
| 11 | **Kubernetes HPA** | Scale berdasarkan backlog/lag/duration, bukan hanya CPU. | Worker sering I/O-bound. | CPU rendah tapi backlog naik. |
| 12 | **DB Pool Budget** | `replicas × maxJobsActive × DB usage`. | MyBatis/PostgreSQL aman. | Worker scale-out membunuh DB. |
| 13 | **Timeout Layering** | Job timeout > handler budget > DB/downstream timeout. | Avoid duplicate concurrent execution. | Handler masih jalan tapi job sudah reassigned. |
| 14 | **Version Compatibility** | Worker v1/v2 coexist saat rolling update. | Variable schema dan BPMN compatible. | Workflow gagal saat rollout. |
| 15 | **Incident Handling** | Kapan retry habis menjadi incident. | Operasional workflow jelas. | Task stuck diam-diam atau retry infinite. |

---

# 4. Camunda 8 Job Worker: Konsep Inti

Camunda 8 job worker memproses jobs dari service task BPMN berdasarkan **job type**. Worker mem-poll job type tertentu, mengeksekusi logic, lalu memberi command `complete` atau `fail`. Camunda docs menjelaskan job memiliki type, key, variables, custom headers, dan properties lain; worker dapat mengatur timeout, max jobs to activate, dan variables yang di-fetch. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

Core flow:

```text id="ufq33d"
1. BPMN service task creates job type = "reserve-inventory"
2. Worker opens subscription / polling for "reserve-inventory"
3. Zeebe activates job and assigns it to worker
4. Worker processes business logic
5. Worker completes job with variables
   or fails job with retries/backoff
   or throws business/BPMN modeled error if applicable
```

Key point:

```text id="x1vamv"
Job worker boleh diskalakan horizontal.
Tapi setiap worker harus membatasi concurrency agar tidak mengambil lebih banyak job
daripada yang bisa diselesaikan dengan aman.
```

---

# 5. Camunda 7 External Task: Konsep Inti

Di Camunda 7 External Task pattern, worker melakukan `fetchAndLock` external tasks untuk topic tertentu. Camunda 7 docs menjelaskan bahwa task yang di-fetch akan dikunci secara eksklusif untuk worker id tertentu selama durasi lock; selama lock valid, worker lain tidak bisa mengambil task yang sama. Jika task gagal, worker bisa menentukan retries dan retry timeout; ketika retries menjadi 0, incident dibuat. ([Camunda Docs](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.5/user-guide/process-engine/external-tasks/?__hsfp=3006156910&__hssc=252030934.1.1762732800223&__hstc=252030934.2f3f33a24b44870ec4a577029c49e44b.1762732800220.1762732800221.1762732800222.1&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Core flow:

```text id="jv61q1"
1. BPMN external service task creates external task topic = "reserve-inventory"
2. Worker fetchAndLock topic
3. Task locked for workerId for lockDuration
4. Worker executes business logic
5. Worker completes task
   or handles failure with retries/retryTimeout
   or handles BPMN error
```

Camunda 7 Javadocs juga menyediakan operasi seperti `complete`, `fetchAndLock`, `handleBpmnError`, `setRetries`, dan `extendLock` untuk external tasks. ([Camunda Docs](https://docs.camunda.org/javadoc/camunda-bpm-platform/7.17/org/camunda/bpm/engine/ExternalTaskService.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Kubernetes Deployment Pattern untuk Worker

Worker long-running biasanya cocok sebagai **Deployment**, bukan Job/CronJob.

```yaml id="0fqlq9"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-worker
  namespace: order-prod
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-worker
    app.kubernetes.io/component: worker
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-worker
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-worker
        app.kubernetes.io/component: worker
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: order-worker-sa
      automountServiceAccountToken: false
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 90

      containers:
        - name: order-worker
          image: registry.example.com/order-worker:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

          env:
            - name: CAMUNDA_JOB_TYPE
              value: "reserve-inventory"

            - name: CAMUNDA_MAX_JOBS_ACTIVE
              value: "8"

            - name: CAMUNDA_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS
              value: "120000"

            - name: WORKER_SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS
              value: "60000"

            - name: DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
              value: "8"

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1024Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2048Mi"

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 24

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 3
```

Kubernetes Deployment cocok untuk workload stateless long-running karena Deployment mengelola Pods dan ReplicaSets secara deklaratif serta menyediakan update bertahap. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. REST API vs Worker Deployment

Pisahkan REST API dan worker jika workload berbeda.

```text id="6m6zg2"
order-api Deployment
  ├── handles HTTP request
  ├── request/response latency sensitive
  ├── HPA maybe HTTP RPS/CPU
  └── small DB pool per Pod

order-worker Deployment
  ├── handles Camunda jobs
  ├── throughput/backlog sensitive
  ├── HPA maybe backlog/active jobs
  └── worker concurrency controlled
```

Jangan campur tanpa alasan:

```text id="42q8kf"
Satu Deployment menjalankan REST API + worker thread
```

Risiko:

```text id="wy0xi5"
HTTP traffic dan worker traffic saling memakan CPU/memory/DB pool
readiness API bisa hijau walau worker mati
scale API ikut scale worker secara tidak sengaja
shutdown API/worker jadi lebih kompleks
```

Boleh digabung hanya jika workload kecil dan konsekuensinya dipahami.

---

# 8. Job Type / Topic Naming

Gunakan nama stabil dan domain-specific.

Baik:

```text id="q5bltf"
reserve-inventory
authorize-payment
generate-order-document
submit-order-to-fulfillment
notify-customer
```

Buruk:

```text id="rf3g6k"
worker1
task
service-task
process
doSomething
```

Prinsip:

```text id="skxuro"
Job type/topic adalah contract antara BPMN model dan worker deployment.
Perubahan nama job type adalah breaking change untuk worker.
```

Untuk multi-version rollout, pastikan BPMN model dan worker version compatible.

---

# 9. Concurrency Model

Worker concurrency biasanya dikontrol oleh:

```text id="3s0jti"
replicas
maxJobsActive / maxTasks
executor thread pool size
DB pool size
downstream HTTP client pool
Camunda engine/broker capacity
```

Formula kasar:

```text id="4mok75"
max active jobs in cluster
=
replicas × maxJobsActive
```

Contoh:

```text id="eivb4y"
replicas = 4
maxJobsActive = 8

max active jobs ≈ 32
```

Jika setiap job bisa memakai 1 DB connection:

```text id="uzcelt"
minimum DB capacity needed for worker path ≈ 32 concurrent DB operations
```

Tapi pool-nya:

```text id="o03zg5"
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 8 per Pod
replicas = 4
max worker DB connections = 32
```

Jangan set:

```text id="7ih364"
maxJobsActive = 100
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 5
```

kecuali handler sebagian besar tidak butuh DB dan Anda punya backpressure internal.

---

# 10. Camunda 8 `maxJobsActive`

Camunda 8 Java job worker docs menjelaskan bahwa worker melakukan polling untuk `maxJobsActive` jobs dan akan melakukan polling lagi saat jumlah unhandled jobs turun di bawah threshold tertentu; jika streaming enabled, worker hanya akan buffer sampai `maxJobsActive`, sehingga nilai ini juga membantu membatasi memory usage. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/job-worker/))

Praktis:

```text id="w0nsv2"
maxJobsActive:
  bukan sekadar throughput setting.
  Ini adalah concurrency, memory, DB, dan downstream pressure setting.
```

Baseline:

```text id="g40eui"
CPU/IO-light handler:
  maxJobsActive bisa lebih tinggi

DB-heavy handler:
  maxJobsActive harus dekat dengan DB pool budget

External API-heavy handler:
  maxJobsActive harus menghormati rate limit dan timeout

Memory-heavy handler:
  maxJobsActive harus rendah
```

---

# 11. Timeout / Lock Duration Design

Timeout harus lebih besar dari normal processing time, tetapi tidak terlalu besar.

```text id="e8yorr"
job timeout / lock duration
  > p99 normal handler duration
  > DB/downstream timeout budget
  > expected graceful shutdown drain for active job
```

Tetapi jika terlalu panjang:

```text id="3xxtba"
worker crash
job baru tersedia lagi sangat lama
workflow stuck menunggu timeout
incident detection lambat
```

Jika terlalu pendek:

```text id="qxk14u"
worker pertama masih memproses
timeout habis
job diambil worker kedua
duplicate side effect risk
complete command worker pertama bisa gagal
```

Camunda 8 docs menjelaskan jika job tidak completed/failed dalam activation timeout, Zeebe dapat reassign job ke worker lain; timeout bisa membuat dua worker mengerjakan job yang sama, dan hanya satu complete command yang berhasil. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

---

# 12. Timeout Layering

Contoh budget:

```text id="44dcx3"
Job timeout / lock duration:
  120s

Worker handler budget:
  90s

Downstream API timeout:
  3s per call

DB statement timeout:
  5s

Worker shutdown drain:
  60s

Kubernetes terminationGracePeriodSeconds:
  90s
```

Rule:

```text id="ofvd4c"
terminationGracePeriodSeconds
  harus cukup untuk stop polling + drain active jobs + close resources.

job timeout / lock duration
  harus cukup agar graceful shutdown dapat menyelesaikan active job
  atau fail secara eksplisit sebelum timeout.
```

Jika handler bisa 10 menit, jangan hanya menaikkan timeout begitu saja. Evaluasi apakah handler harus dipecah menjadi beberapa BPMN service tasks lebih kecil.

---

# 13. Long-Running Job Pattern

Bad:

```text id="p1m2hp"
one Camunda job:
  generate 100,000 invoices
  call external APIs
  write DB
  takes 45 minutes
```

Risiko:

```text id="ub7eya"
lock timeout besar
restart duplicate risk
progress hilang
observability buruk
rollback sulit
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="8t76tu"
Split workflow:
  job 1: prepare batch
  job 2: process chunk
  job 3: aggregate result
  job 4: notify completion
```

Atau:

```text id="69qyue"
Use checkpoint:
  process chunk
  save progress
  complete current task
  create next task for next chunk
```

Camunda 8 supports timeout update for long-running jobs via `UpdateJobTimeout`, but design-wise, long jobs still need idempotency and progress checkpoints. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

---

# 14. Retries: Technical Failure

Technical failure:

```text id="ood7yn"
payment-api timeout
inventory-api 503
PostgreSQL transient connection failure
Camunda gateway unavailable
temporary network issue
rate limit 429
```

Handling:

```text id="iyqb15"
fail job with remaining retries
set retry backoff
log structured error
emit metric
preserve error details safely
```

Camunda 8 docs state fail job command includes remaining retries; if retries are greater than zero, job is retried/reassigned; if zero or negative, an incident is raised. It also supports retry backoff to delay retry when external systems need time to recover. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

Retry policy example:

```text id="xbr32i"
attempt 1:
  fail, retry after 30s

attempt 2:
  fail, retry after 2m

attempt 3:
  fail, retry after 10m

attempt 4:
  retries = 0 → incident / manual intervention
```

---

# 15. BPMN Business Error vs Technical Failure

Business error:

```text id="0cdiq1"
payment declined
customer not eligible
inventory unavailable
order validation failed
credit check rejected
```

Technical failure:

```text id="svd1nt"
payment API timeout
DB connection error
unexpected 500
network failure
serialization bug
```

Business error should usually route BPMN flow:

```text id="eyqy3v"
handle BPMN error
  → compensation path
  → manual review
  → rejection notification
```

Technical failure should usually retry or incident:

```text id="w890e0"
fail job with retries/backoff
  → retry later
  → incident if exhausted
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="xxjtso"
Payment declined → fail technical retry 5 times
```

It wastes capacity and hides a business outcome.

---

# 16. Idempotency: Core Requirement

Worker handler must survive re-execution.

Risky operations:

```text id="uq4diz"
charge payment
reserve inventory
send email/SMS
create order
create invoice
start external workflow
publish event
complete/fail Camunda job after side effect
```

Pattern:

```text id="xg2g48"
Use idempotency key:
  processInstanceKey
  businessKey
  jobKey
  orderId
  externalReference
  deterministic operation key
```

Database constraint:

```sql id="m4lr94"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_authorization_idempotency
ON payment_authorization (idempotency_key);
```

Handler pattern:

```text id="f8n0dc"
1. derive idempotency key
2. check if operation already completed
3. if completed, return existing result
4. if not, perform operation
5. persist result atomically
6. complete Camunda job
```

If `complete job` fails after side effect, the job may be retried. Idempotency ensures retry observes the side effect already happened and completes safely.

---

# 17. Idempotent Handler Skeleton

```java id="u4v6kn"
public final class ReserveInventoryHandler implements JobHandler {

    private final InventoryReservationService reservationService;

    @Override
    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        String processInstanceKey = String.valueOf(job.getProcessInstanceKey());
        String jobKey = String.valueOf(job.getKey());
        String orderId = extractRequiredVariable(job, "orderId");

        String idempotencyKey = "reserve-inventory:" + orderId;

        try {
            ReservationResult result = reservationService.reserveIfAbsent(
                    idempotencyKey,
                    orderId
            );

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "reservationId", result.reservationId(),
                            "inventoryReserved", true
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (InventoryUnavailableException businessError) {
            // Model as BPMN business error if process expects this branch.
            client.newThrowErrorCommand(job.getKey())
                    .errorCode("INVENTORY_UNAVAILABLE")
                    .errorMessage("Inventory unavailable for order")
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (TransientDependencyException transientError) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .retryBackoff(Duration.ofMinutes(2))
                    .errorMessage("Transient dependency failure")
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception unexpected) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(0)
                    .errorMessage("Unexpected worker failure")
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

Code ini konseptual. Sesuaikan API client sesuai versi Camunda Java client yang dipakai perusahaan.

---

# 18. Worker Shutdown Pattern

Saat Pod menerima SIGTERM:

```text id="csoj46"
1. mark readiness false
2. stop polling / stop activating new jobs
3. wait active jobs to finish within drain timeout
4. complete/fail active jobs if possible
5. close Camunda client
6. close DB pool / HTTP clients
7. exit before terminationGracePeriodSeconds
```

Kubernetes rolling update akan menghentikan Pod lama. Jika worker tidak stop polling saat shutdown, Pod bisa mengambil job baru tepat sebelum mati.

Baseline lifecycle:

```java id="bmdsbr"
public final class WorkerLifecycle {

    private final AtomicBoolean shuttingDown = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final List<JobWorker> workers = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final HikariDataSource dataSource;

    public void registerShutdownHook() {
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(this::shutdown, "worker-shutdown"));
    }

    public boolean isReady() {
        return !shuttingDown.get();
    }

    private void shutdown() {
        shuttingDown.set(true);

        // Stop polling / activation.
        for (JobWorker worker : workers) {
            try {
                worker.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
                // log safely in real implementation
            }
        }

        executor.shutdown();

        try {
            executor.awaitTermination(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }

        dataSource.close();
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="iqi2tv"
Readiness false tidak otomatis menghentikan Camunda polling.
Aplikasi harus eksplisit menghentikan worker/client saat shutdown.
```

---

# 19. Readiness dan Liveness untuk Worker

Worker readiness bukan hanya “HTTP server hidup”.

Readiness should check:

```text id="1lgj5b"
app initialized
worker subscriptions opened
Camunda client configured
not shutting down
DB/client pools initialized if required
```

Liveness should check:

```text id="rp6m3q"
process is alive
main worker loop not deadlocked
critical executor not terminated unexpectedly
```

Avoid liveness depending on Camunda availability:

```text id="wn6e9m"
Camunda temporarily unavailable
  → liveness fails
  → all workers restart
  → more load and churn
```

Better:

```text id="lvy2fs"
Camunda unavailable:
  readiness may go false if worker cannot do useful work
  worker backs off
  metrics/alerts fire
  liveness remains local
```

---

# 20. Scaling Strategy

Worker scaling candidates:

```text id="z93gza"
backlog / queued jobs
active jobs
job activation rate
job completion rate
job duration p95
failure/retry rate
Camunda incident count
downstream saturation
DB pool pending
```

Not enough:

```text id="zlxrvy"
CPU only
```

Because many workers are I/O-bound:

```text id="328xzs"
waiting DB
waiting downstream HTTP
waiting Camunda
low CPU but backlog rising
```

Scaling formula:

```text id="4g1q0h"
needed replicas
≈ incoming jobs per second × average job duration seconds / maxJobsActive per replica
```

Example:

```text id="c8xw4r"
incoming = 20 jobs/s
average duration = 2s
maxJobsActive = 8

needed active concurrency = 40
replicas ≈ 40 / 8 = 5
```

Then validate against DB/downstream:

```text id="jw6vzo"
replicas 5 × DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE 8 = 40 DB connections
replicas 5 × maxJobsActive 8 = 40 active jobs
```

---

# 21. HPA Manifest for Worker

CPU-based baseline:

```yaml id="7wqmh7"
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: order-worker
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    apiVersion: apps/v1
    kind: Deployment
    name: order-worker
  minReplicas: 2
  maxReplicas: 8
  metrics:
    - type: Resource
      resource:
        name: cpu
        target:
          type: Utilization
          averageUtilization: 70
```

Better if custom metrics available:

```text id="b3tp9k"
camunda_job_backlog
camunda_active_jobs
camunda_job_duration_p95
camunda_job_completion_rate
```

But never scale blindly:

```text id="3l247r"
maxReplicas must be capped by:
  DB connection budget
  downstream rate limits
  Camunda engine/broker capacity
  node capacity
```

---

# 22. Backpressure

Backpressure means worker intentionally limits work intake.

Camunda 8 Java worker docs describe backoff behavior when polling fails, including exponential backoff, and explain that `GRPC_STATUS_RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` indicates the Zeebe cluster is under too much load and has rejected a request; backing off helps reduce load. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/job-worker/))

Worker-level backpressure:

```text id="f3cucf"
reduce maxJobsActive
increase pollInterval / backoff
pause worker for dependency outage
limit executor queue
limit DB pool
limit downstream HTTP concurrency
```

Camunda broker/cluster backpressure:

```text id="2a64lb"
Zeebe may reject requests under load.
Worker should back off, not retry aggressively.
```

Camunda docs also recommend reducing request rate when backpressure is active. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/self-managed/components/orchestration-cluster/zeebe/operations/backpressure/))

---

# 23. Worker + PostgreSQL / MyBatis

If each job performs DB work:

```text id="bu3qzm"
replicas × maxJobsActive
  must be aligned with:
    DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
    PostgreSQL connection budget
    query duration
    lock contention
```

Example bad:

```text id="hrzo7i"
replicas = 10
maxJobsActive = 50
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 10

active jobs possible = 500
DB connections possible = 100
400 jobs may wait on DB or executor queue
```

Better:

```text id="ejwrsw"
replicas = 5
maxJobsActive = 8
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE = 8

active jobs possible = 40
DB connections possible = 40
```

If job handler does external API + DB:

```text id="q0m2nn"
do not hold DB transaction while waiting external API
```

Use short transactions:

```text id="e4g7ah"
transaction 1:
  mark processing / reserve idempotency key
commit

external call:
  payment/inventory/etc

transaction 2:
  persist result
commit

complete job
```

---

# 24. Version Compatibility During Rolling Update

During rollout:

```text id="xcirwl"
order-worker v1 Pod still active
order-worker v2 Pod starts
both may process same job type
```

Therefore:

```text id="82bv0e"
BPMN variables must be backward/forward compatible.
Job type should not change abruptly.
Error codes should remain compatible.
Output variables should be additive if possible.
Downstream contract should remain compatible.
```

Bad:

```text id="y5evem"
v2 expects variable "customerProfileV2"
v1 process instances only have "customerProfile"
worker v2 fails old instances
```

Better:

```text id="qx23oa"
v2 reads customerProfileV2 if present
else reads customerProfile
writes both during transition if needed
```

---

# 25. Multi-topic / Multi-job-type Worker

Avoid one process handling too many unrelated job types if they have different profiles.

Bad:

```text id="c2sl7b"
one worker handles:
  authorize-payment
  generate-pdf
  send-email
  reserve-inventory
```

Risiko:

```text id="794vve"
PDF job memory-heavy starves payment job
email provider outage affects inventory worker
scaling one topic scales all topics
observability less clear
```

Better:

```text id="8f297b"
order-payment-worker
order-inventory-worker
order-document-worker
notification-worker
```

Each has own:

```text id="bmpqdn"
replicas
maxJobsActive
resources
HPA
NetworkPolicy
DB pool
timeout
alerts
```

---

# 26. Metrics Wajib Worker

Minimal:

```text id="stpl8p"
camunda_jobs_activated_total
camunda_jobs_completed_total
camunda_jobs_failed_total
camunda_jobs_bpmn_error_total
camunda_job_duration_seconds
camunda_active_jobs
camunda_job_retries_remaining
camunda_job_timeout_total
camunda_worker_poll_errors_total
camunda_worker_backoff_total
camunda_incidents_total
```

Labels bounded:

```text id="ne9ggj"
jobType
workerName
result
errorCode
environment
version
```

Avoid as metric labels:

```text id="skg3wf"
processInstanceId
businessKey
orderId
customerId
jobKey
```

Camunda 8 Java job worker docs mention worker metrics for activated and handled jobs, and also note a Micrometer integration option. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/job-worker/))

---

# 27. Logs Wajib Worker

Structured log for job start:

```json id="5bb8g6"
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-worker",
  "jobType": "reserve-inventory",
  "workerName": "order-worker",
  "processInstanceKey": "2251799813685250",
  "businessKey": "ORD-123",
  "jobKey": "2251799813686011",
  "traceId": "abc",
  "message": "Camunda job started"
}
```

Job completed:

```json id="hkumv8"
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "order-worker",
  "jobType": "reserve-inventory",
  "businessKey": "ORD-123",
  "durationMs": 842,
  "result": "completed",
  "message": "Camunda job completed"
}
```

Job failed:

```json id="vcg8lp"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "service": "order-worker",
  "jobType": "reserve-inventory",
  "businessKey": "ORD-123",
  "durationMs": 3000,
  "result": "failed",
  "errorCode": "INVENTORY_API_TIMEOUT",
  "retriesLeft": 2,
  "retryBackoffMs": 120000,
  "message": "Camunda job failed with retry"
}
```

Do not log:

```text id="gpul4f"
full process variables if they contain PII
tokens
raw customer data
full request/response body
secrets
```

---

# 28. Tracing Worker

Trace should continue from workflow/process start if possible.

```text id="zhjq0p"
POST /orders trace
  └── Camunda start process
      └── later: reserve-inventory worker span
          ├── DB span
          ├── inventory-api span
          └── complete job span
```

Worker span attributes:

```text id="czt0z2"
camunda.job.type=reserve-inventory
camunda.worker.name=order-worker
camunda.process.instance.key=...
business.operation=reserveInventory
result=completed/failed/bpmn_error
```

Avoid high-cardinality span names:

```text id="zf9f10"
Bad:
  reserveInventory ORD-123

Good:
  Camunda job reserve-inventory
```

For async tracing, carry `traceparent` as process variable/header if platform standard allows, then extract it in worker.

---

# 29. NetworkPolicy for Worker

Worker usually needs egress to:

```text id="lvcn9x"
Camunda engine/gateway
PostgreSQL
downstream APIs
DNS
OpenTelemetry collector
```

Example:

```yaml id="tf7jfs"
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: order-worker-egress
  namespace: order-prod
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-worker
  policyTypes:
    - Egress
  egress:
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              name: camunda-prod
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 26500
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 8080

    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              name: data-prod
          podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app.kubernetes.io/name: postgresql
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 5432
```

Adjust ports to your actual Camunda 7 REST or Camunda 8 gateway setup.

---

# 30. Security and Secrets

Worker secrets:

```text id="xg3x22"
CAMUNDA_CLIENT_SECRET
CAMUNDA_BASIC_AUTH_PASSWORD
DB_PASSWORD
DOWNSTREAM_API_TOKEN
KAFKA/REDIS credentials if used
```

Rules:

```text id="4qb2sr"
store in Kubernetes Secret or external secret provider
do not print env
do not log full Camunda variables
rotate credentials with rollout plan
use least-privilege Camunda credentials
```

Worker should not need Kubernetes API RBAC unless it creates Kubernetes resources. For normal Camunda job processing:

```yaml id="mrcvne"
automountServiceAccountToken: false
```

---

# 31. Deployment Rollout Safety for Workers

Recommended strategy:

```yaml id="q5mznu"
strategy:
  type: RollingUpdate
  rollingUpdate:
    maxSurge: 1
    maxUnavailable: 0
```

But for workers, consider:

```text id="j9zax3"
maxSurge adds extra worker capacity temporarily
extra workers may increase DB/downstream pressure
old and new versions process same job type concurrently
```

If worker handles sensitive side effects, a safer rollout may be:

```text id="nudgcl"
reduce maxSurge
use canary worker with small replicas
separate job type for new version if breaking
feature flag behavior
pause old worker only after new worker validated
```

---

# 32. Canary Worker Pattern

Stable worker:

```text id="5l07s2"
order-worker-stable replicas=5
jobType=reserve-inventory
```

Canary worker:

```text id="hfr1t8"
order-worker-canary replicas=1
jobType=reserve-inventory
version=1.10.0
```

Risiko:

```text id="wo2lpl"
both stable and canary consume same job type
you cannot easily guarantee only 5% of process instances unless routing/job type/tenant/variable strategy supports it
```

Safer canary options:

```text id="mj316g"
canary by tenant
canary by dedicated job type
canary by process version
canary by feature flag inside handler
canary in staging with production-like backlog replay
```

---

# 33. Incident Handling

When retries exhausted:

```text id="k7az8j"
technical failure becomes incident
manual/operator action may be needed
```

Runbook should include:

```text id="hhecsx"
job type
error code
error message
process instance id/key
business key
worker version
last failure timestamp
retries left
downstream dependency status
how to safely retry
how to compensate if side effect partial
```

Do not “just reset retries” without checking idempotency and side effects.

---

# 34. Failure Mode Table

| Gejala | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Backlog naik | Worker capacity kurang, downstream lambat, Camunda issue | activated/completed rate, backlog |
| Duplicate side effect | Timeout/retry/complete failure, non-idempotent handler | business logs, unique constraint |
| Job stuck | retries exhausted, incident, no worker for job type | Camunda incident/job metrics |
| Worker running tapi tidak process | wrong job type/topic, auth, tenant, NetworkPolicy | worker logs, Camunda metrics |
| CPU rendah tapi backlog naik | I/O-bound, DB/downstream wait | traces, DB pool pending |
| OOMKilled | maxJobsActive too high, variables too large | Pod describe, memory metrics |
| DB exhausted | replicas × pool too high | Hikari metrics, PostgreSQL sessions |
| 504 to Camunda | proxy/gateway timeout, long polling/streaming issue | worker logs, Camunda docs/config |
| Job timeout | handler duration > timeout, shutdown too slow | duration metrics, timeout logs |
| Rollout duplicate | old/new process same job, lock timeout | logs by version |
| Incident storm | retry policy too aggressive or downstream outage | incidents_total, fail rate |

---

# 35. Debugging Commands

Kubernetes:

```bash id="qpl096"
kubectl get deploy order-worker -n order-prod
kubectl get pods -n order-prod -l app.kubernetes.io/name=order-worker
kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n order-prod
kubectl logs deploy/order-worker -n order-prod --since=30m
kubectl logs <pod-name> -n order-prod --previous
kubectl rollout status deploy/order-worker -n order-prod
kubectl get events -n order-prod --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

Check resource pressure:

```bash id="crnzx4"
kubectl top pod -n order-prod
kubectl get hpa -n order-prod
kubectl describe hpa order-worker -n order-prod
```

Check config:

```bash id="ee4e22"
kubectl get deploy order-worker -n order-prod -o yaml
```

Look for:

```text id="47d9n9"
CAMUNDA_* config
job type/topic
maxJobsActive / maxTasks
timeout / lock duration
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
resources
probes
terminationGracePeriodSeconds
version label
```

---

# 36. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Non-idempotent Handler

```text id="wqq54f"
job retry
  → payment charged twice
```

Fix:

```text id="1szydz"
idempotency key + unique constraint + external idempotency support
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - `maxJobsActive` Terlalu Tinggi

```text id="horqms"
replicas=10
maxJobsActive=100
```

Worker buffers too much, memory spikes, DB/downstream overload.

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Worker Tidak Stop Polling Saat Shutdown

```text id="0g119x"
SIGTERM received
worker still activates new jobs
Pod killed before completion
jobs timeout and retry
```

Fix:

```text id="9ayqm5"
readiness false + close worker + drain active jobs
```

---

## Anti-pattern 4 - Semua Error Dianggap Technical Retry

```text id="pmfgr5"
payment declined
retry 5 times
incident
```

Fix:

```text id="pve5h4"
model business error as BPMN error / business branch
```

---

## Anti-pattern 5 - Worker dan API Digabung Tanpa Control

```text id="9e9wy3"
HTTP API and Camunda worker in same Pod
HPA scales both together
DB pool shared
shutdown mixed
```

Fix:

```text id="t5kxe3"
separate Deployment unless strong reason
```

---

## Anti-pattern 6 - No Version Compatibility

```text id="fa6v8r"
worker v2 only understands new variable format
old process instances fail
```

Fix:

```text id="gb5fsq"
dual-read/compatible variables during migration
```

---

# 37. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Worker type | Camunda 7 external task atau Camunda 8 job worker? |
| Deployment | Worker dipisah dari REST API? |
| Job type | Nama topic/job type stabil dan jelas? |
| Concurrency | `replicas × maxJobsActive` aman? |
| DB | `replicas × DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE` aman? |
| Timeout | Job timeout/lock duration > p99 handler + drain budget? |
| Retry | Technical retry/backoff jelas? |
| BPMN error | Business error dimodelkan sebagai BPMN branch? |
| Idempotency | Semua side effect punya idempotency key? |
| Shutdown | Stop polling + drain active jobs saat SIGTERM? |
| Readiness | Worker not ready saat shutting down? |
| Liveness | Tidak restart semua worker saat Camunda/downstream temporary down? |
| Scaling | HPA berdasarkan backlog/throughput jika tersedia, bukan CPU saja? |
| Observability | Metrics/logs/traces per job type/result/version tersedia? |
| Versioning | v1/v2 worker compatible saat rolling update? |
| NetworkPolicy | Egress hanya ke Camunda/DB/downstream/DNS/OTel? |
| Incident | Runbook reset retries/incident aman terhadap side effect? |

---

# 38. Ringkasan Seri 23

```text id="3j97b3"
Camunda worker:
  long-running process yang mengambil job/task dari Camunda,
  menjalankan business logic,
  lalu complete/fail/BPMN error.

Kubernetes Deployment:
  pattern umum untuk worker long-running.

Concurrency:
  replicas × maxJobsActive/maxTasks.

Timeout / lock duration:
  harus cukup untuk normal processing,
  tetapi tidak terlalu lama agar stuck job cepat pulih.

Retries:
  untuk technical transient failure.

BPMN error:
  untuk business outcome yang memang dimodelkan.

Idempotency:
  wajib karena worker delivery harus diasumsikan at-least-once.

Graceful shutdown:
  stop polling, drain active jobs, close client/pool.

Observability:
  job duration, activated/completed/failed, retries, incidents, backlog,
  logs dengan businessKey/processInstance/jobType,
  traces untuk downstream/DB calls.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey + MyBatis + Camunda di Kubernetes:

```text id="gt14as"
Pisahkan order-api dan order-worker bila workload berbeda.
Batasi maxJobsActive sesuai DB/downstream capacity.
Hitung HPA maxReplicas dan rollout maxSurge.
Gunakan idempotency key untuk semua side effect.
Jangan hold DB transaction saat call external API.
Stop polling saat SIGTERM.
Pastikan lock/job timeout selaras dengan handler duration dan shutdown grace.
Monitor backlog, retries, incidents, job duration, DB pool, downstream errors.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text id="7v6hw0"
Worker yang benar bukan worker yang “cepat mengambil banyak job”.
Worker yang benar adalah worker yang:
mengambil job sesuai kapasitas,
aman dieksekusi ulang,
bisa berhenti dengan graceful,
tidak membunuh DB/downstream,
dan memberi evidence jelas saat workflow gagal.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:52:29 PM

Lanjut ke **Seri 24/24**, seri penutup. Fokusnya perbedaan praktis Kubernetes di **on-prem, AWS/EKS, dan Azure/AKS** dari sudut pandang software engineer aplikasi: apa yang berubah di networking, identity, registry, storage, observability, security policy, dan operasi deployment.

# Kubernetes Series - Seri 24/24  
## On-Prem vs AWS/EKS vs Azure/AKS: Perbedaan Praktis untuk Software Engineer

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan: **apa yang benar-benar berubah bagi software engineer ketika aplikasi Java/Jersey/JAX-RS dideploy ke Kubernetes on-prem, AWS/EKS, atau Azure/AKS?**

Secara API, Kubernetes tetap memberi primitive yang sama: `Deployment`, `Service`, `Ingress/Gateway`, `ConfigMap`, `Secret`, `HPA`, `NetworkPolicy`, `ServiceAccount`, `PVC`, dan seterusnya. Tetapi secara operasional, implementasinya bisa sangat berbeda: load balancer, CNI, identity, registry, storage, observability, autoscaling node, dan security policy biasanya mengikuti platform tempat cluster berjalan. Kubernetes sendiri memisahkan integrasi cloud melalui komponen seperti **cloud-controller-manager**, yang menghubungkan cluster ke API cloud provider untuk hal seperti load balancer dan node lifecycle. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/architecture/cloud-controller/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
Kubernetes API terlihat sama
│
├── Deployment
├── Service
├── ConfigMap
├── Secret
├── HPA
├── Ingress / Gateway
├── NetworkPolicy
├── PVC
└── ServiceAccount

Tapi implementasi platform berbeda
│
├── On-Prem
│   ├── load balancer: MetalLB / F5 / HAProxy / NGINX / hardware LB
│   ├── registry: Harbor / Nexus / Artifactory / private registry
│   ├── identity: LDAP/OIDC/internal IAM
│   ├── storage: SAN/NAS/Ceph/Longhorn/CSI internal
│   └── observability: self-managed Prometheus/Loki/ELK/Splunk/etc
│
├── AWS/EKS
│   ├── networking: VPC, subnets, security groups, Amazon VPC CNI
│   ├── load balancer: AWS Load Balancer Controller / NLB / ALB
│   ├── registry: ECR / GHCR / internal registry
│   ├── identity: IAM, EKS Pod Identity / IRSA
│   ├── storage: EBS/EFS CSI
│   └── observability: CloudWatch / AMP / Grafana / OTel
│
└── Azure/AKS
    ├── networking: Azure VNet, subnets, NSG, AKS networking
    ├── load balancer: Azure Standard Load Balancer / App Gateway / Gateway
    ├── registry: Azure Container Registry
    ├── identity: Microsoft Entra ID, managed identity, Workload ID
    ├── storage: Azure Disk / Azure Files CSI
    └── observability: Azure Monitor / Managed Prometheus / Managed Grafana
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text
Manifest Kubernetes boleh sama,
tetapi failure mode, permission model, networking path,
cost model, dan troubleshooting flow sangat bergantung pada platform.
```

---

# 2. Practical Skill Map

| No | Domain | On-Prem | AWS/EKS | Azure/AKS | Dampak ke Software Engineer |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Cluster Ownership** | Biasanya platform/internal infra team penuh. | Managed control plane EKS. | Managed control plane AKS. | Tahu siapa owner DNS/LB/CNI/storage/identity. |
| 2 | **Pod Networking** | Tergantung CNI: Calico/Cilium/Flannel/etc. | EKS memakai Amazon VPC CNI yang memberi Pod IP dari VPC. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/managing-vpc-cni.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | AKS memakai integrasi Azure networking sesuai mode cluster. | Debug IP, route, NetworkPolicy, DNS. |
| 3 | **LoadBalancer Service** | Tidak otomatis tersedia kecuali ada MetalLB/hardware/cloud-like LB. | Bisa membuat AWS LB via controller/provider. | Bisa membuat Azure Load Balancer. | `type: LoadBalancer` tidak selalu portable. |
| 4 | **Ingress/Gateway** | Biasanya NGINX/HAProxy/Traefik/F5/API gateway internal. | AWS Load Balancer Controller dapat membuat ALB/NLB untuk Service/Ingress. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/aws-load-balancer-controller.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Bisa pakai Azure LB/App Gateway/Gateway stack sesuai platform. | Annotation dan behavior controller berbeda. |
| 5 | **Registry** | Harbor/Nexus/Artifactory/internal. | ECR/GHCR/internal. | ACR/GHCR/internal; AKS-ACR integration memakai permission seperti `AcrPull`. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/cluster-container-registry-integration?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | ImagePullBackOff debugging berbeda. |
| 6 | **Workload Identity** | OIDC/Vault/internal secret broker. | EKS Pod Identity / IRSA ke IAM. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/pod-identities.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Microsoft Entra Workload ID via service account token projection dan OIDC federation. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/workload-identity-overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Akses cloud resource tanpa static secret. |
| 7 | **Storage** | SAN/NAS/Ceph/Longhorn/CSI. | EBS/EFS CSI. | Azure Disk/Azure Files CSI. | PVC behavior, performance, backup, zone constraints berbeda. |
| 8 | **Observability** | Self-managed Prometheus/Grafana/Loki/ELK/Splunk. | CloudWatch/AMP/Grafana/vendor. | Azure Monitor, managed Prometheus, Managed Grafana, container logs. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/azure-monitor/containers/kubernetes-monitoring-enable?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Query logs/metrics/traces beda platform. |
| 9 | **Security Boundary** | Network firewall, VLAN, internal PKI, PodSecurity, RBAC. | IAM, security groups, VPC, Pod Identity, RBAC. | Entra ID, managed identity, NSG, Workload ID, RBAC. | Jangan campur Kubernetes RBAC dengan cloud IAM. |
| 10 | **Autoscaling Nodes** | Sering terbatas atau manual. | Cluster Autoscaler/Karpenter/EKS Auto Mode tergantung platform. | Cluster autoscaler/AKS autoscaling features. | Pending Pod bisa butuh infra action. |
| 11 | **Cost Model** | Fixed capacity/capex, quota internal. | Pay-as-you-go, LB/NAT/data transfer/managed services. | Pay-as-you-go, LB/public IP/storage/logging cost. | Scaling dan logs bisa berdampak biaya. |
| 12 | **Release Operations** | Change window sering lebih ketat. | Cloud-native automation lebih mudah jika IAM/VPC siap. | Azure-native integration kuat jika Entra/ACR/Monitor siap. | Pipeline harus mengikuti environment governance. |

---

# 3. Platform Responsibility Model

## On-Prem

Di on-prem, platform team biasanya mengelola lebih banyak hal:

```text
control plane
worker nodes
CNI
load balancer
storage backend
certificate/PKI
registry
monitoring
backup
DNS
firewall
node patching
cluster upgrade
```

Bagi software engineer, artinya banyak dependency Kubernetes yang di cloud terasa “otomatis” harus dikonfirmasi:

```text
Apakah Service type LoadBalancer tersedia?
Ingress controller apa yang dipakai?
NetworkPolicy benar-benar enforced oleh CNI apa?
StorageClass default apa?
Registry pull secret bagaimana?
DNS internal siapa yang mengelola?
Apakah ada cluster autoscaler?
```

MetalLB adalah salah satu solusi populer untuk menyediakan load balancer di cluster bare-metal/on-prem; dokumentasi MetalLB menjelaskan ia sebagai implementasi load balancer untuk bare metal Kubernetes menggunakan standard routing protocols. ([MetalLB](https://metallb.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## AWS/EKS

Amazon EKS adalah platform untuk menjalankan cluster Kubernetes di AWS dan juga punya opsi untuk data center melalui varian seperti EKS Anywhere/Hybrid Nodes. EKS cluster berjalan dalam Amazon VPC; Amazon VPC CNI memasang ENI ke node EC2 dan memberikan private IPv4/IPv6 address dari VPC ke Pod. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/what-is-eks.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Bagi software engineer:

```text
Pod IP bisa berasal dari VPC.
Subnet dan security group penting.
Load balancer sering ALB/NLB.
Akses AWS service sebaiknya lewat Pod Identity/IRSA, bukan static key.
ECR permission/image pull harus benar.
CloudWatch/AMP/Grafana bisa menjadi observability path.
```

## Azure/AKS

AKS adalah managed Kubernetes service untuk deploy dan manage containerized applications; Azure mengurangi overhead pengelolaan Kubernetes dengan mengelola banyak aspek cluster. Untuk identitas workload, Microsoft Entra Workload ID memakai service account token projection dan OIDC federation agar Pod bisa mengakses Azure resources berbasis annotated service account. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/what-is-aks?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Bagi software engineer:

```text
Cluster terintegrasi dengan Azure VNet, NSG, managed identity, ACR, Azure Monitor.
Pod yang perlu akses Key Vault/Storage/Cosmos/etc sebaiknya memakai Workload ID.
Image pull dari ACR butuh integrasi permission, misalnya AcrPull ke kubelet identity.
Observability sering lewat Azure Monitor / managed Prometheus / Managed Grafana.
```

---

# 4. Networking: Pod IP, Service, Load Balancer

Kubernetes `Service` tetap menjadi abstraction utama untuk mengekspos aplikasi yang berjalan sebagai Pod. Untuk `type: LoadBalancer`, Kubernetes biasanya membuat perubahan mirip `NodePort`, lalu cloud-controller-manager atau provider implementation mengonfigurasi external load balancer jika platform mendukungnya. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/services-networking/service/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## On-Prem

```text
Service type ClusterIP:
  biasanya aman dan sama seperti Kubernetes umum.

Service type LoadBalancer:
  butuh MetalLB, F5, HAProxy, hardware LB, atau integrasi platform.

Ingress:
  biasanya NGINX/HAProxy/Traefik/F5/API Gateway internal.

External DNS:
  sering manual atau dikelola DNS enterprise.
```

Risiko umum:

```text
Service LoadBalancer pending selamanya.
NodePort dibuka manual ke firewall.
Ingress IP berubah tanpa DNS update.
TLS certificate internal chain tidak dipercaya Java client.
```

## AWS/EKS

```text
Service type LoadBalancer:
  biasanya mengarah ke AWS load balancer sesuai controller/annotation/platform.

Ingress:
  bisa memakai AWS Load Balancer Controller untuk membuat ALB/NLB.

Pod networking:
  Amazon VPC CNI memberi Pod IP dari VPC.
```

AWS Load Balancer Controller mengelola AWS Elastic Load Balancers untuk cluster Kubernetes dan dapat mengekspos aplikasi cluster ke internet dengan LB yang menunjuk ke Service atau Ingress resources. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/aws-load-balancer-controller.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Azure/AKS

```text
Service type LoadBalancer:
  biasanya mengarah ke Azure Load Balancer.

Ingress:
  bisa memakai NGINX ingress, Application Gateway Ingress Controller, Gateway stack, atau platform internal.

Networking:
  perlu memahami VNet, subnet, NSG, private/public endpoint.
```

AKS menyediakan integrasi load balancing Azure untuk mengekspos Service melalui Azure Load Balancer; dokumentasi AKS juga memiliki panduan Standard Load Balancer untuk Service publik. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/load-balancer-standard?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Diagram Perbedaan Exposure Path

```text
On-Prem
Client
  → Enterprise DNS
  → F5/HAProxy/MetalLB/NGINX
  → Ingress Controller
  → Service
  → Pod Java/Jersey

AWS/EKS
Client
  → Route53 / enterprise DNS
  → ALB/NLB
  → Ingress / Service
  → Pod IP in VPC
  → Pod Java/Jersey

Azure/AKS
Client
  → Azure DNS / enterprise DNS
  → Azure Load Balancer / Application Gateway
  → Ingress / Service
  → Pod Java/Jersey
```

Application engineer harus tahu:

```text
External URL publik/internal apa?
TLS terminate di mana?
Path rewrite ada atau tidak?
Forwarded headers disanitasi atau tidak?
Timeout LB/Ingress berapa?
Request size limit berapa?
Source IP yang dilihat app berasal dari mana?
```

---

# 6. CNI dan NetworkPolicy

Kubernetes NetworkPolicy hanya efektif jika CNI/plugin mendukung enforcement. Dari sisi aplikasi, manifest `NetworkPolicy` bisa sama, tapi enforcement dan fitur tambahan berbeda.

```text
On-Prem:
  Calico/Cilium/Antrea/Flannel + plugin policy bisa berbeda.
  Kadang NetworkPolicy tidak enforced jika CNI tidak support.

AWS/EKS:
  Amazon VPC CNI memberi IP VPC ke Pod.
  Network policy bisa bergantung pada setup/add-on/policy engine yang dipakai.

Azure/AKS:
  Network policy provider tergantung mode AKS dan konfigurasi cluster.
```

Yang perlu ditanyakan software engineer:

```text
Apakah NetworkPolicy enforced?
CNI apa?
Default deny sudah diterapkan?
Apakah egress FQDN policy tersedia?
Apakah flow logs tersedia?
Apakah Pod-to-Pod cross-namespace allowed by default?
```

---

# 7. Identity: Kubernetes RBAC vs Cloud IAM

Pisahkan tiga layer:

```text
Kubernetes RBAC:
  boleh get/list/watch/create object Kubernetes?

Cloud IAM:
  boleh akses S3/SQS/Secrets Manager/Key Vault/Storage/ACR/ECR?

Application Auth:
  user/client boleh melakukan operasi bisnis apa?
```

## AWS/EKS

EKS Pod Identity memberi least-privilege access dengan mengasosiasikan IAM role ke Kubernetes service account; hanya Pod yang menggunakan service account tersebut mendapat permission IAM yang terkait. Credential sementara diberikan melalui EKS Pod Identity Agent di node, berbeda dari IRSA yang memakai web identity token dan STS `AssumeRoleWithWebIdentity`. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/pod-identities.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pattern:

```text
Kubernetes ServiceAccount:
  order-api-sa

EKS Pod Identity association:
  order-api-sa → IAM role with access to Secrets Manager/SQS/S3/etc

Java app:
  AWS SDK default credential provider reads temporary credentials
```

## Azure/AKS

Microsoft Entra Workload ID menggunakan service account token projection dan OIDC federation agar aplikasi dalam Pod bisa mengakses Azure resources secara aman berdasarkan annotated service account. AKS Workload ID dapat diaktifkan dengan OIDC issuer dan workload identity, lalu service account dikaitkan dengan managed identity/federated credential. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/workload-identity-overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pattern:

```text
Kubernetes ServiceAccount:
  order-api-sa

Azure Workload Identity:
  service account annotated/federated with managed identity

Java app:
  Azure SDK DefaultAzureCredential / workload identity flow
```

## On-Prem

On-prem lebih bervariasi:

```text
Vault Kubernetes auth
internal OIDC
LDAP/AD integration
static secret via external secret operator
mTLS certificate identity
custom secret broker
```

Rule:

```text
Jangan menyimpan cloud/service credential statis di image.
Jangan memberi semua Pod credential yang sama.
Ikuti service account per workload + least privilege.
```

---

# 8. Registry dan Image Pull

## On-Prem

Umum:

```text
Harbor
Nexus
Artifactory
internal Docker registry
```

Perlu dicek:

```text
imagePullSecret
internal CA certificate
registry mirror
air-gapped environment
vulnerability scanning
retention policy
```

## AWS/EKS

Umum:

```text
ECR
GHCR
internal registry
```

Perlu dicek:

```text
node IAM/pull permission
cross-account ECR
private subnet access to registry
VPC endpoints if no internet egress
image tag/digest
```

## Azure/AKS

AKS dan ACR dapat diintegrasikan agar cluster bisa pull image dari ACR; dokumentasi AKS menyebut penggunaan `--attach-acr` atau pemberian role `AcrPull` ke kubelet managed identity. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/cluster-container-registry-integration?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Failure mode umum:

```text
ImagePullBackOff
ErrImagePull
unauthorized
certificate unknown authority
manifest unknown
registry timeout
```

Checklist untuk aplikasi:

```text
image tag immutable?
image digest dicatat?
cluster punya permission pull?
registry reachable dari node?
imagePullSecret benar?
CA registry dipercaya node?
```

---

# 9. Storage: PVC, StorageClass, dan Stateless Bias

Kubernetes `PersistentVolume` adalah resource storage di cluster, sedangkan `StorageClass` mendeskripsikan kelas storage yang ditawarkan administrator, misalnya QoS, backup policy, atau policy lain; Kubernetes sendiri tidak menentukan apa arti kelas-kelas tersebut. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/persistent-volumes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice stateless:

```text
Biasakan tidak butuh PVC.
Gunakan stdout logs, bukan file log lokal.
Gunakan object storage untuk file permanen.
Gunakan PostgreSQL/Redis/Kafka/Camunda sebagai external state.
Gunakan emptyDir hanya untuk scratch/tmp.
```

Perbedaan platform:

```text
On-Prem:
  StorageClass bisa map ke SAN/NAS/Ceph/Longhorn/VMware CSI.

AWS:
  StorageClass bisa map ke EBS/EFS CSI.

Azure:
  StorageClass bisa map ke Azure Disk/Azure Files CSI.
```

Pertanyaan untuk app engineer:

```text
Apakah app benar-benar butuh persistent volume?
Apakah volume bisa pindah node?
Apakah ReadWriteOnce/ReadWriteMany diperlukan?
Apakah backup/restore tersedia?
Apakah latency storage cocok?
Apakah zone binding bisa membuat Pod Pending?
```

---

# 10. Observability

## On-Prem

Biasanya self-managed atau enterprise-managed:

```text
Prometheus
Grafana
Loki
ELK/OpenSearch
Splunk
Jaeger/Tempo
OpenTelemetry Collector
enterprise APM
```

Risiko:

```text
retention berbeda antar environment
collector tidak standar
logs hilang jika node mati
TLS/internal CA issue
query language beda
```

## AWS/EKS

Umum:

```text
CloudWatch logs/metrics
Amazon Managed Service for Prometheus
Amazon Managed Grafana
OpenTelemetry Collector
vendor APM
```

## Azure/AKS

Azure Monitor untuk AKS dapat mengaktifkan Prometheus metrics, Managed Grafana, container logging, dan control plane logs. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/azure-monitor/containers/kubernetes-monitoring-enable?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Application engineer harus memastikan:

```text
JSON logs punya service/env/namespace/pod/version/traceId.
Metrics punya HTTP/JVM/DB pool/downstream/business SLI.
Traces punya service.name/service.version/k8s metadata.
Dashboard bisa filter by cluster/env/version.
Alert bukan hanya CPU/memory.
```

---

# 11. Security Policy dan Admission

Platform bisa memakai:

```text
Pod Security Admission
OPA Gatekeeper
Kyverno
custom admission webhook
image policy
signature verification
secret policy
network policy baseline
```

Manifest yang lolos di dev belum tentu lolos di prod jika prod punya policy lebih ketat.

Common rejection:

```text
runAsNonRoot missing
allowPrivilegeEscalation not false
seccompProfile missing
resources not set
latest image tag
privileged container
hostPath used
ServiceAccount token automount enabled
no NetworkPolicy
```

Portable hardening baseline:

```yaml
securityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 10001
  runAsGroup: 10001
  seccompProfile:
    type: RuntimeDefault

containers:
  - name: order-api
    securityContext:
      allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
      privileged: false
      readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
      capabilities:
        drop:
          - ALL
```

---

# 12. Autoscaling dan Capacity

## On-Prem

```text
Node capacity sering fixed.
Cluster autoscaler belum tentu ada.
Pending Pod bisa berarti butuh platform team menambah node.
Quota internal bisa ketat.
```

## AWS/EKS

```text
Horizontal Pod Autoscaler:
  scale Pod

Cluster Autoscaler/Karpenter/EKS Auto Mode:
  bisa membantu node provisioning, tergantung platform

Tetap harus hitung:
  DB connections
  downstream rate limits
  NAT/data transfer cost
```

EKS Auto Mode memperluas manajemen AWS ke infrastruktur yang mendukung workload, termasuk beberapa keputusan operasional untuk networking/load balancing/capacity, tetapi detail penggunaan tetap bergantung pada cluster configuration. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/eks/latest/userguide/automode.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Azure/AKS

```text
HPA scale Pod.
Cluster autoscaler dapat menambah node pool jika dikonfigurasi.
Node pool, VM SKU, subnet IP, quota, dan identity harus benar.
```

Senior rule:

```text
Autoscaling aplikasi tidak boleh melebihi budget DB, Camunda, Kafka, Redis, downstream API, dan license/cost limit.
```

---

# 13. External Dependency: PostgreSQL, Camunda, Redis, Kafka

Perbedaan platform memengaruhi dependency:

```text
On-Prem:
  DB/Camunda/Kafka/Redis bisa berada di data center internal.
  Latency rendah tapi firewall/change process ketat.

AWS:
  dependency bisa RDS/MSK/ElastiCache/SQS/Secrets Manager/etc.
  IAM/VPC/security group penting.

Azure:
  dependency bisa Azure Database for PostgreSQL, Event Hubs, Cache for Redis,
  Key Vault, Storage, Service Bus.
  Managed identity/Private Link/NSG penting.
```

Aplikasi harus punya config eksternal:

```text
DB_URL
CAMUNDA_BASE_URL
KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS
REDIS_HOST
DOWNSTREAM_BASE_URL
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
```

Jangan hardcode hostname cloud-specific di code.

---

# 14. Deployment Pipeline Differences

## On-Prem

```text
CI/CD mungkin perlu:
  VPN/internal runner
  internal registry
  internal CA
  change window
  manual approval
  limited internet egress
```

## AWS/EKS

```text
CI/CD mungkin perlu:
  OIDC to AWS IAM
  ECR login
  kubeconfig via EKS API
  private subnet/VPN runner if API private
  GitOps controller inside cluster
```

## Azure/AKS

```text
CI/CD mungkin perlu:
  OIDC to Azure
  ACR permission
  az aks get-credentials
  private cluster connectivity
  GitOps/Flux/Argo integration
```

Portable release principle:

```text
Build once.
Promote same image digest.
Use environment-specific manifest values/overlays.
Validate rendered manifest.
Wait rollout status.
Smoke test internal Service.
Monitor SLI.
```

---

# 15. Comparison Table: Apa yang Harus Ditanyakan Saat Onboarding

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Cluster | Cluster ini on-prem, EKS, AKS, atau hybrid? |
| Kubernetes Version | Versi cluster dan upgrade cadence? |
| CNI | CNI apa dan NetworkPolicy enforced atau tidak? |
| Ingress | Controller apa? NGINX, ALB, App Gateway, F5, Gateway API? |
| Load Balancer | `Service type LoadBalancer` didukung atau tidak? |
| DNS | External DNS dan internal DNS dikelola siapa? |
| TLS | Sertifikat dari mana? cert-manager, enterprise PKI, ACM, Key Vault? |
| Registry | Registry apa? Pull permission bagaimana? |
| Identity | Pod akses cloud resource pakai apa? IAM/Workload ID/Vault/static secret? |
| Secrets | Secret source of truth di mana? K8s Secret, Vault, AWS SM, Azure Key Vault? |
| Storage | StorageClass default apa? App stateless atau butuh PVC? |
| Observability | Logs, metrics, traces masuk ke mana? |
| Policy | PodSecurity/Gatekeeper/Kyverno policy apa? |
| Autoscaling | HPA saja atau node autoscaler juga? |
| Quota | Namespace quota/resource limit apa? |
| Deployment | GitOps atau direct deploy? |
| Rollback | Rollback standard via Git revert, Helm rollback, atau rollout undo? |
| Incident | Siapa owner platform/network/DB/cloud saat incident? |

---

# 16. Platform-Specific Failure Modes

## On-Prem

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| LoadBalancer pending | Tidak ada MetalLB/hardware LB integration. |
| DNS internal gagal | Enterprise DNS belum update. |
| ImagePullBackOff | Registry internal CA/pull secret salah. |
| Pod Pending | Node capacity fixed, no autoscaler. |
| TLS error Java | Internal CA tidak dimount/trusted. |
| Network timeout | Firewall/VLAN/NetworkPolicy. |
| Storage mount gagal | CSI/SAN/NAS issue. |

## AWS/EKS

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Pod tidak bisa akses AWS service | Pod Identity/IRSA/role trust/policy salah. |
| Load balancer tidak muncul | AWS Load Balancer Controller/annotation/IAM/subnet tag issue. |
| Pod IP exhaustion | Subnet IP capacity/VPC CNI planning. |
| ImagePullBackOff ECR | IAM/pull permission/private endpoint issue. |
| Timeout keluar internet | NAT gateway/route/security group/NACL. |
| Pending Pod | Node group/Karpenter/Auto Mode/quota/instance capacity. |

## Azure/AKS

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| Pod tidak bisa akses Key Vault/Storage | Workload ID/federated credential/role assignment salah. |
| ImagePullBackOff ACR | AcrPull role/managed identity/registry networking. |
| LoadBalancer pending/problem | Azure LB/public IP/subnet/NSG/quota issue. |
| Log tidak muncul | Azure Monitor/agent/config/workspace issue. |
| Pod Pending | VM SKU quota/node pool/subnet IP/PVC zone issue. |
| TLS/private endpoint issue | DNS zone/private link/cert trust. |

---

# 17. Portable Manifest Baseline

Untuk Java/Jersey REST API, usahakan manifest portable ini konsisten di semua platform:

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-api
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
    app.kubernetes.io/component: api
    app.kubernetes.io/part-of: order-management
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app.kubernetes.io/name: order-api
        app.kubernetes.io/component: api
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.9.0-a1b2c3d"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: order-api-sa
      automountServiceAccountToken: false
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45

      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 10001
        runAsGroup: 10001
        seccompProfile:
          type: RuntimeDefault

      containers:
        - name: order-api
          image: registry.example.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            privileged: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            capabilities:
              drop:
                - ALL

          env:
            - name: APP_NAME
              value: "order-api"
            - name: APP_PROFILE
              valueFrom:
                configMapKeyRef:
                  name: order-api-config
                  key: APP_PROFILE

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 24

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 3

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1024Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2048Mi"

          volumeMounts:
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp

      volumes:
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 256Mi
```

Yang biasanya berbeda per platform:

```text
image registry host
Service/Ingress/Gateway annotations
load balancer class
identity annotations
storage class
secret provider
observability endpoint
network policy destinations
node selectors/tolerations
```

---

# 18. Environment Overlay Strategy

Gunakan overlay/values per platform:

```text
deploy/
├── base/
│   ├── deployment.yaml
│   ├── service.yaml
│   ├── hpa.yaml
│   └── networkpolicy.yaml
└── overlays/
    ├── onprem-prod/
    ├── aws-eks-prod/
    └── azure-aks-prod/
```

Contoh beda registry:

```text
onprem-prod:
  registry.internal.company.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

aws-eks-prod:
  123456789012.dkr.ecr.ap-southeast-1.amazonaws.com/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d

azure-aks-prod:
  company.azurecr.io/order-api:1.9.0-a1b2c3d
```

Contoh beda identity:

```text
onprem:
  Vault role / external secret reference

AWS:
  service account associated with IAM role

Azure:
  service account annotated for Workload ID
```

---

# 19. Application-Level Portability Checklist

Aplikasi Java/Jersey harus:

```text
Tidak hardcode cloud endpoint.
Membaca config dari env/file.
Tidak bergantung pada Pod IP.
Menggunakan Service DNS internal.
Mempunyai timeout eksplisit.
Mempunyai graceful shutdown.
Mempunyai health endpoint.
Mempunyai structured logs.
Mempunyai metrics/traces.
Tidak menulis state permanen ke local filesystem.
Tidak menyimpan secret di image.
Bisa berjalan non-root.
```

Jika semua ini terpenuhi, pindah on-prem ↔ EKS ↔ AKS jauh lebih mudah.

---

# 20. Senior Engineer Runbook Saat Pindah Platform

```text
1. Render manifest untuk target platform.
2. Cek image registry dan pull permission.
3. Cek Service/Ingress/Gateway exposure.
4. Cek DNS dan TLS.
5. Cek Workload Identity / Secret source.
6. Cek NetworkPolicy dan firewall.
7. Cek DB/Camunda/Redis/Kafka connectivity.
8. Cek observability endpoint.
9. Cek resource requests/limits sesuai node size.
10. Cek HPA dan node autoscaling.
11. Cek rollout strategy.
12. Deploy staging.
13. Smoke test internal and external route.
14. Load test critical path.
15. Verify logs/metrics/traces.
16. Validate rollback.
```

---

# 21. Anti-patterns

## Anti-pattern 1 - Menganggap Kubernetes Sama di Semua Platform

```text
Service LoadBalancer jalan di EKS,
lalu diasumsikan pasti jalan di on-prem.
```

Tidak selalu. On-prem butuh implementasi seperti MetalLB atau load balancer enterprise.

## Anti-pattern 2 - Cloud IAM Credential Statis di Secret

```text
AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID
AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY
AZURE_CLIENT_SECRET
```

Lebih baik gunakan EKS Pod Identity/IRSA atau AKS Workload ID jika tersedia.

## Anti-pattern 3 - Hardcode Cloud URL di Code

```java
String endpoint = "https://s3.ap-southeast-1.amazonaws.com/...";
```

Lebih baik lewat config dan abstraction.

## Anti-pattern 4 - Observability Vendor Lock-in di Code

Jangan membuat business logic bergantung pada vendor observability tertentu. Gunakan OpenTelemetry/Micrometer abstraction sejauh mungkin.

## Anti-pattern 5 - Tidak Menguji NetworkPolicy di Staging

Prod punya default-deny, staging tidak. Akibatnya aplikasi “lulus staging” tapi timeout di prod.

## Anti-pattern 6 - Menggunakan PVC untuk State yang Seharusnya External

REST API stateless jangan menyimpan file bisnis permanen di local PVC jika seharusnya object storage/database.

---

# 22. Senior Engineer Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Runtime | Cluster on-prem, EKS, AKS, atau hybrid? |
| CNI | Pod networking dan NetworkPolicy behavior apa? |
| Exposure | LoadBalancer/Ingress/Gateway path apa? |
| DNS/TLS | Siapa owner DNS dan certificate? |
| Registry | Registry host dan pull permission? |
| Identity | Pod-to-cloud/resource identity pakai apa? |
| Secrets | Secret source dan rotation process? |
| DB | PostgreSQL endpoint, firewall, TLS, pool budget? |
| Camunda | Engine/gateway endpoint, auth, timeout? |
| Observability | Logs/metrics/traces ke mana? |
| Policy | PodSecurity/admission rules apa? |
| Scaling | HPA dan node autoscaling tersedia? |
| Capacity | Namespace quota dan node capacity cukup? |
| Deployment | GitOps/direct deploy? |
| Rollback | Cara rollback per platform? |
| Cost | LB, logs, NAT, storage, data transfer, node cost? |
| Ownership | Siapa dihubungi untuk platform/network/cloud/DB incident? |

---

# 23. Ringkasan Seri 24

```text
Kubernetes API portable.
Kubernetes operations tidak sepenuhnya portable.

Yang berbeda antar platform:
  CNI
  Load balancer
  Ingress/Gateway
  Registry
  Identity
  Storage
  Observability
  Security policy
  Autoscaling
  Cost model
  Incident ownership
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice:

```text
Tulis aplikasi se-stateless mungkin.
Gunakan Service DNS, bukan Pod IP.
Gunakan config eksternal.
Gunakan timeout eksplisit.
Gunakan health endpoint.
Gunakan structured logs, metrics, traces.
Jalankan non-root.
Jangan simpan secret di image.
Hitung DB/Camunda/downstream capacity.
Pisahkan platform-specific manifest lewat overlays/values.
```

Senior-level invariant:

```text
Software engineer yang efektif di Kubernetes tidak hanya tahu YAML.
Ia tahu bagian mana yang portable,
bagian mana yang platform-specific,
dan pertanyaan apa yang harus ditanyakan sebelum menyalahkan aplikasi,
cluster, network, cloud, atau database.
```
